# Full item specification
### P1.5 — Беспотерьная область CAS

**Intent:** Область дедупликации CAS выражается строковым `BlobScope` и колонкой `v_blobs.scope_key`, так что ULID/UUID/
строковые ключи drive и tenant больше не схлопываются в `snull`/`tnull`; старые blobs мигрируют, а смешанные области
ремонтируются командой `vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes` без потери байтов (E1, N02-часть, AD20; V01, V13, V14).

**Why:** OPUS 13 P1.5, 02 D20, 06 §9, 08 §2 (`v_blobs`), §3 №7, 09 §7. Сегодня `UploadFileAction::tenantId()/storageIdForScope()`
(`packages/drive/src/Actions/UploadFileAction.php:280–296`) отбрасывают нечисловые id (`is_numeric`), `BlobStore::scopeSegment()`
(`packages/media/src/Cas/BlobStore.php`) даёт `snull`/`tnull`, `findOrCreate` ищет по `storage_id`/`tenant_id` = NULL — все
ULID/UUID7-drives делят одну область; `BlobReconciler::KEY_PATTERN` (`packages/media/src/Cas/BlobReconciler.php:70`) не знает UUID.

**Scope Included:**
1. `packages/core/src/ValueObjects/Cas/BlobScope.php` (new, `final readonly`): фабрики `global()`, `tenant(string $id)`, `drive(string $id)`,
   `legacy(string $segment)` (только для старых объектов: `snull`, `tnull`, `s<digits>`, `t<digits>`, `global`);
   `forUpload(string $dedupScope, ?string $tenantId, string $storageId): self` — `global` → `global()`; `tenant` → `tenant($tenantId)`
   или `global()` при `null` (одна область хоста без тенантов); `storage` → `drive($storageId)`;
   `key(): string` → `global` | `tenant:{id}` | `drive:{id}` | для legacy — `global`, `tenant:{n}`, `drive:{n}`, `legacy:snull`, `legacy:tnull`
   (id — строка без преобразований); `segment(): string` → `g` | `t-{s}` | `d-{s}`, где `s` = id, если он соответствует
   `^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$`, иначе `~` + base64url(id) без `=`; legacy → исходный сегмент; `fromSegment(string): self` — обратное.
2. Контракты core: `Contracts\Cas\ContentStore::keyFor(BlobScope $scope, string $sha256): string`;
   `Contracts\Cas\BlobStore::commit(mixed $stream, BlobScope $scope, ?string $disk = null): Blob` (`attach`/`detach`/`read` — без
   изменений, имена `FileVersion` — до P2.1). Реализации media: `Sha256ContentStore::keyFor` — новые области
   `{segment}/{sha[0:2]}/{sha}` (02 D20); legacy-области — прежняя раскладка `{segment}/{sha[0:2]}/{sha[2:4]}/{sha}`;
   `BlobStore::commit` — `advisoryLock('cas:'.$scope->key().':'.$sha256)` через `VaulterDatabase` (уровень 5 D21; формат
   заменяет `scope|sha` из P1.1), поиск/вставка по `scope_key`.
3. `packages/core/src/Repositories/Cas/BlobStoreRepository.php`: `findOrCreate(string $sha256, int $sizeBytes, string $storageKey, string $disk, BlobScope $scope, ?string $tenantId, ?string $storageId)`;
   `lockedLookup`/`applyScope` → `where('scope_key', $scope->key())`; при вставке заполняются `scope_key` и, как сейчас,
   `dedup_scope`, `tenant_id`, `storage_id` (информационные).
4. `UploadFileAction`: `BlobScope::forUpload(Config::dedupScope(), $storage->tenant_id === null ? null : (string) $storage->tenant_id, (string) $storage->getKey())`;
   методы `tenantId()`/`storageIdForScope()` удаляются.
5. GC и reconciler (`packages/media/src/Cas/{BlobGarbageCollector,BlobReconciler}.php`): ключ блокировки — `cas:{scope_key}:{sha}`
   (GC — из строки blob; reconciler — `BlobScope::fromSegment(<сегмент пути>)->key()`); `KEY_PATTERN` распознаёт оба формата:
   старый `^(global|[ts](\d+|null|[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26}))/[0-9a-f]{2}/[0-9a-f]{2}/[0-9a-f]{64}$` и новый
   `^(g|[td]-(~?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}))/[0-9a-f]{2}/[0-9a-f]{64}$`; проверка `keyFor(fromSegment(seg), sha) === path` сохраняется.
6. Схема и миграция №7 (R3):
   - `PanelTablesBuilder::defineBlobs()`: `$table->string('scope_key', 191)`; уникальность `UNIQUE(scope_key, sha256)` `{t}_scope_sha_uniq`
     вместо `{t}_dedup_uniq` (`dedupUniqueColumns()` удаляется); `{t}_storage_key_uniq` остаётся.
   - `core/database/migrations/2026_10_01_000007_blob_scope_key.php`: если `scope_key` нет — добавить nullable; заполнить
     по `dedup_scope` строки: `global` → `global`; `tenant` → `tenant:{tenant_id}` либо `legacy:tnull`; `storage` → `drive:{storage_id}`
     либо `legacy:snull` (значения id — как текст); затем NOT NULL, `{t}_scope_sha_uniq`, удалить `{t}_dedup_uniq` (если есть).
     Конфликт уникальности при заполнении невозможен (старый ключ уникальнее); если он возникает — миграция падает (стоп).
     `down()`: вернуть `{t}_dedup_uniq` по текущему `dedup.scope`, удалить `{t}_scope_sha_uniq` и колонку.
7. Команда `packages/media/src/Console/RepairBlobScopesCommand.php` — `vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes {--dry-run} {--execute}` (ровно
   один флаг, иначе exit 2 с подсказкой); регистрация в `VaulterMediaServiceProvider`. Алгоритм:
   1) кандидаты — blobs с `scope_key IN ('legacy:snull','legacy:tnull')`, батчами по 100;
   2) для каждого — ссылки: `file_version_blobs` → `file_versions` → `files` → `nodes` → `storages`; целевая область на каждый
      storage: `legacy:snull` → `drive:{storage_id}`; `legacy:tnull` → `tenant:{storages.tenant_id}` или `global` при NULL;
      blob без ссылок → строка отчёта `unreferenced` (не трогается);
   3) `--dry-run`: таблица `blob_id | sha256 | scope_key | storage_id | refs | target | action` (`move` | `unreferenced` | `hash_mismatch`), exit 0;
   4) `--execute`, для каждой пары (blob, target) в отдельной `VaulterDatabase::transaction()`: sha256 байтов объекта старого
      ключа пересчитывается потоково; несовпадение → `hash_mismatch` в отчёт, пропуск; `advisoryLock('cas:'.$target.':'.$sha)`;
      найти blob `(target, sha)` или создать строку и скопировать байты в `keyFor(target, sha)` (если объекта нет); переключить
      `file_version_blobs.blob_id` и `file_versions.blob_id` для ревизий storages этой цели; `ref_count` старого −n, нового +n;
      старый объект не удаляется (его заберёт GC по `ref_count`); строка журнала JSON в
      `storage/logs/vaulter-repair-scopes.log` `{ts, blob_id, target, action, edges}`; итог `Repaired: N, skipped: M`, exit 0
      (1 — если была ошибка, не `hash_mismatch`). Повторный запуск идемпотентен (переключённые рёбра не находятся).
8. Тесты:
   - V01 — `packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobScopeKeyTest.php` (любая БД): dataset `ids.default` ∈ {bigint, ulid, uuid7} × `dedup.scope` ∈
     {global, tenant, storage}: два storage, одинаковые байты → для `storage` — разные `scope_key`, разные blobs и объекты; для
     `tenant` с разными тенантами — разные; для `global` — один blob; purge в одном storage не удаляет объект другого;
     `BlobReconciler` распознаёт ключи обоих storages (не считает их сиротами).
   - `BlobScope` — `packages/core/tests/Unit/BlobScopeTest.php`: `key()`/`segment()`/`fromSegment()` для UUID с дефисами, ULID,
     числа, строки с `/` (base64url), legacy-сегментов.
   - V13 — `packages/media/tests/Cas/ScopeKeyReconcileTest.php` (PG, `ForkRace`): сбой между promote и SQL commit (ContentStore,
     бросающий после `promote`) → объект-сирота нового формата; reconcile до grace не удаляет, после grace — удаляет, перепроверяя
     отсутствие строки под `advisoryLock`; параллельно GC и writer одной `(scope_key, sha)` → достижимые байты не удалены.
   - V14 — `packages/media/tests/Cas/RepairBlobScopesCommandTest.php` (PG): blob `legacy:snull` с рёбрами из двух storages (A, B) →
     `--dry-run` печатает 2 строки `move`; `--execute` → blobs `drive:A`, `drive:B`, рёбра переключены, старый объект на диске,
     `ref_count` старого = 0; сбой посреди (ContentStore, падающий на копии для B) и повторный `--execute` → итог тот же, без
     дублей; blob `legacy:tnull`, storage с `tenant_id = NULL` → цель `global`; без ссылок → `unreferenced`; подменённые байты → `hash_mismatch`.
   - Миграция — `packages/core/tests/Upgrade/BlobScopeKeyMigrationTest.php` (`LoadsV02Fixture`, P1.7): на `pgsql-bigint.sql` и
     `pgsql-ulid.sql` все blobs получили `scope_key`; blobs `storage_id IS NULL` (scope storage) → `legacy:snull`, `tenant_id IS NULL`
     (scope tenant) → `legacy:tnull`; индекс `{t}_scope_sha_uniq` есть, `{t}_dedup_uniq` нет; повторный `migrate` no-op.
   Негативный контроль V01: временно вернуть в `forUpload` приведение `is_numeric` → dataset ulid/uuid7 × storage красный.
9. `docs/guide/storage-and-blobs.md` (scope_key, сага, `repair-scopes`), `UPGRADE.md` (миграция №7, запуск
   `vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes --dry-run` → `--execute`, AD20), `CHANGELOG.md [Unreleased]`, golden `PublicApiSnapshotTest`
   (сигнатуры `BlobStore`/`ContentStore`, `BlobScope`).

**Scope Excluded:** переименование `storage_id` → `drive_id`, `FileVersion` → `FileRevision` (P2.1); канонические ключи
`vaulter-media.*` (P2.5); значение `dedup.scope = drive` вместо `storage` (P2.5); doctor-проверка изоляции дисков (P7.1);
удаление drive и GC его рёбер (P4.6); переименования прочих команд (P7.2).

**Inputs:** `plans/2026.09.29-№1-VAULTER-STABLE/artifacts/P1-design/P1.5-opus.md`;
`audits/2026-09-29-stable-audit/opus/02-decisions.md` (OPUS AD20); `audits/2026-09-29-stable-audit/opus/09-integrity-and-security.md` (§7);

`packages/core/src/Database/VaulterDatabase.php` (producer: P1.1); `packages/core/tests/Upgrade/LoadsV02Fixture.php` (producer: P1.7)

**Files:** `packages/core/src/ValueObjects/Cas/BlobScope.php` (new), `packages/core/src/Contracts/Cas/{BlobStore,ContentStore}.php`,

**Required Reads:** 0) `plans/2026.09.29-№1-VAULTER-STABLE/artifacts/P1-design/P1.5-opus.md` (дословные требования OPUS — исполнить все);

**Authority:** routine

**Implementation Rules:**
- Строка blob и её `storage_key` авторитетны для чтения: существующие объекты не переименовываются и не перемещаются миграцией.
- Repair не угадывает тенант и не удаляет объекты; удаление — только GC/reconcile по своим правилам.
- Все блокировки CAS — `VaulterDatabase::advisoryLock('cas:{scope_key}:{sha}')`.
- Тесты media, сверявшие старые сигнатуры/сегменты для новых записей, обновляются на `BlobScope`; иное падение — стоп.

**Code Guidance:** `grep -rn "is_numeric(\$tenantId)\|is_numeric(\$storageId)\|'snull'\|'tnull'" packages/drive/src packages/media/src/Cas/BlobStore.php`
→ пусто; legacy-сегменты встречаются только в `BlobScope` и `BlobReconciler::KEY_PATTERN`.

**Validation:**
- `(cd packages/media && env $SQ "$R/scripts/php84" vendor/bin/pest --ci tests/Cas/BlobScopeKeyTest.php)` → `9` dataset-кейсов passed.
- `(cd packages/media && env $PG "$R/scripts/php84" vendor/bin/pest --ci tests/Cas)` → 0 failed (вкл. V13, V14; 0 skipped у новых тестов).

- Негативный контроль V01 (Scope п.8) → красный; после возврата — зелёный.
- Code Guidance → пусто.
- Гейт core и гейты drive, media → exit 0.

**Deliverables:** `BlobScope`, `scope_key` + миграция №7, новые сигнатуры CAS, reconciler старых/новых ключей, `vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes`, V01, V13, V14.

## 01-review.md — 3. Codex (01–04): позиция

- `01:b362eeda`

| Вывод Codex | Позиция |

- `01:d1c63e40`

| E1 CAS scope loss | Согласен. Добавляю: колонки `tenant_id`/`storage_id` в `v_blobs` сами типизированы по стратегии ID Vaulter, а tenant — это ключ хоста (N02) |


## 01-review.md — 4. Новые находки (не было ни в аудите, ни у Codex) / N02 (S1). Колонки ключей хоста типизированы стратегией ID Vaulter

- `01:5368342b`

`v_storages.owner_id`, `v_node_grants.subject_id`, все `tenant_id` создаются `$ids->reference()`, а все
actor-колонки (`created_by`, `owner_id` узла, `trashed_by`, `granted_by`, `actor_id`) — `$ids->actor()`
(`packages/core/src/Schema/UlidIds.php:31-39`, `AutoIncrementIds.php:22-30`, `PanelTablesBuilder.php:164,218`).
Это ключи **хоста**, а не Vaulter. По умолчанию (`ids.default = ulid`) они `char(26)`: хост с UUID-ключами
пользователей/проектов/ролей (36 символов) получает ошибку вставки на PostgreSQL; при `bigint` — хост с
ULID/UUID-пользователями не может ни выдать grant, ни записать аудит.


## 02-decisions.md — D20 — CAS: беспотерьная область дедупликации · T0

- `02:8faad32b`

**Решение.**

- `02:6fd49a70`

- `Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope`: `global()`, `tenant(string)`, `drive(string)`;
  `key()` → `global` | `tenant:{id}` | `drive:{id}` (id — строка без преобразований).

- `02:a727345d`

- `BlobStore::commit(mixed $stream, BlobScope $scope, ?string $disk = null): Blob`.

- `02:c6d163c2`

- `v_blobs.scope_key varchar(191) NOT NULL`, `UNIQUE(scope_key, sha256)` заменяет пару
  «unique по колонкам scope + отдельный unique storage_key» (NULL больше не участвует).

- `02:579e2a66`

- Физический ключ объекта: `{scope-segment}/{sha[0:2]}/{sha}` с URL-safe сегментом; reconciler
  распознаёт старые (`s{int}`, `t{int}`, `snull`, `tnull`) и новые ключи, включая UUID с дефисами.

- `02:6464dc87`

- `vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes --dry-run|--execute`: инвентаризация объектов в `snull/tnull`,
  вывод правильной области из связей ревизия→файл→узел→drive, проверка sha256, копирование в
  новые области, атомарное переключение ссылок, удержание старого объекта до GC. Неизвестный
  тенант не угадывается (отчёт, ручное решение).

- `02:8b5cbc5d` — _P1.5 — disk из строки blob при чтении; doctor-проверка изоляции дисков — P7.1_

- `blob.disk` — авторитетен для чтения; дедупликация между дисками при `scope=global|tenant`
  допустима (ADR-0012 остаётся), изоляция дисков профилей требует `dedup.scope = drive` —
  doctor это проверяет.

## 08-data-model-and-migration.md — 2. Целевая схема (изменённые таблицы) / `v_blobs`

- `08:d206f4bc`

`storage_id` → `drive_id` (REF null); `tenant_id` → HK null; добавить `scope_key varchar(191) NOT NULL`.
Уникальность: `UNIQUE(scope_key, sha256)`; `UNIQUE(storage_key)` остаётся как защита от двойного объекта;
удалить прежний `…_dedup_uniq` по колонкам области.


## 08-data-model-and-migration.md — 3. Upgrade-миграции 0.2 → 0.3

- `08:e4f63f43`

Если фаза F1 выпускается отдельно как 0.2.1 ([13-workstreams.md](13-workstreams.md)), миграции №3 (типы HK),
№7 (scope_key) и ремонт «вторых корней» из №6 уходят в 0.2.1 с теми же телами; остальные остаются в 0.3.

- `08:bd7ec0a7`

Порядок — отдельные миграции (PostgreSQL выполняет каждую в транзакции; на MySQL DDL не транзакционен, поэтому
каждая миграция идемпотентна: проверяет состояние перед шагом). Все читают **старые** имена таблиц из
устаревших `vaulter.table_names`/`vaulter.table_prefix`, если они заданы.

- `08:c7629c6e`

| # | Файл | Шаги |

- `08:342a7303`

| 7 | `…_000007_blob_scope_key.php` | Добавить `scope_key`, заполнить: `global` → `global`; `tenant` → `tenant:{tenant_id}` или `legacy:tnull`; `storage` → `drive:{drive_id}` или `legacy:snull`; затем `UNIQUE(scope_key, sha256)`, удалить старый dedup-индекс |

- `08:8eb14e07`

После миграций — команды (не миграции, потому что работают с объектным хранилищем):

- `08:57fa9622`

- `vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes --dry-run` → отчёт по `legacy:snull|tnull` blobs и их ссылкам;
  `--execute` → перенос в правильные области (D20); идемпотентен, возобновляем, ведёт журнал в `storage/logs`.

- `08:02425ed3`

Откат: `down()` каждой миграции восстанавливает структуру; данные ремонта N04 и hard-delete grants
необратимы — это отмечено в UPGRADE.md, preflight-миграция требует бэкап (флаг `--force` при наличии данных).


## 09-integrity-and-security.md — 7. CAS-сага и сборка мусора

- `09:30049801`

Порядок commit (без изменений по сути, фиксируется в документации): stage → sha256 → advisory lock
`(scope_key, sha256)` → найти blob (resurrect, если tombstoned) или promote объекта (`ifNoneMatch`) и вставить
строку → edge → SQL commit. Откат SQL после promote оставляет объект-сироту; `vaulter:blobs:reconcile`
удаляет сирот старше `orphan_grace_hours`, перепроверяя отсутствие строки под той же блокировкой. GC:
mark/sweep как сейчас, но все блокировки и транзакции — через `VaulterDatabase` (N01).

- `09:9f12a2c4`

Дополнительно:

- `09:da882554`

- ключ объекта строится из `BlobScope::key()`; reconciler распознаёт старые форматы (`s\d+`, `t\d+`, `snull`,
  `tnull`) и новые (`g`, `t-{urlsafe}`, `d-{urlsafe}`), включая UUID с дефисами;

 ## Unreleased
 
+### Lossless CAS scope and migration №7 (AD20)
+
+Replace `BlobStore::commit($stream, tenantId: ..., storageId: ...)` with
+`commit($stream, BlobScope::forUpload($dedupScope, $tenantId, (string) $storageId), $disk)`.
+`ContentStore::keyFor()` now receives `Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope`.
+Host tenant IDs and drive IDs remain strings without numeric coercion. Tenant scope
+with no tenant uses `BlobScope::global()`. Scope IDs contain 1–96 bytes; IDs outside
+`[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}` are encoded as unpadded base64url in physical paths.
+
+Migration `2026_10_01_000007_blob_scope_key` backfills the NOT NULL `scope_key`
+and replaces the dedup index with `UNIQUE(scope_key, sha256)`. It preserves all
+existing `storage_key` values and objects. A uniqueness conflict stops migration
+before the old index is removed; investigate the conflicting rows before retrying.
+Custom blob schemas must also provide `scope_key`.
+
+After the SQL migrations, inspect and repair mixed legacy areas:
+
+```bash
+php artisan vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes --dry-run
+php artisan vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes --execute
+```
+
+Exactly one mode is required. Review `hash_mismatch` and `unreferenced` reports;
+these blobs remain unchanged. Each target commits independently, so a failed copy
+can be retried. Old objects stay until GC; successful moves are journalled in
+`storage/logs/vaulter-repair-scopes.log`. Target scope comes from revision → file →
+node → storage; no tenant is guessed. A storage with NULL tenant belongs to the
+host-wide global scope. Reads always use the disk and path recorded on the blob.
+Run with operational access to all storages in the affected panel, and pause other
+operators changing tenant assignments during repair.
+
+The migration's `down()` restores the historical structure using the current
+`dedup.scope`; it does not undo object copies or redirected edges. If repaired
+rows conflict with the old index, rollback fails before removing `scope_key`.
+Take a backup before upgrading or repairing data.
+

+
+## Repairing historical mixed scopes
+
+Migration №7 fills `scope_key` from historical IDs without moving any object.
+Blobs previously committed without a numeric storage or tenant ID become
+`legacy:snull` or `legacy:tnull`. Inspect them with
+`php artisan vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes --dry-run`, then apply with `--execute`.
+The report shows blob, digest, source scope, storage, edge count, target and action.
+Unreferenced blobs and SHA-256 mismatches are reported without changing their bytes
+or references. Each target runs in its own `VaulterDatabase` transaction: lock the
+affected trees and both CAS addresses, verify source bytes, copy to the target,
+verify target bytes, redirect revision edges and the legacy `blob_id`, and rebuild
+ref-count caches. Existing target blobs retain their authoritative disk/path.
+Old objects remain until GC. Interrupted repairs resume without duplicate moves;
+successes append JSON to `storage/logs/vaulter-repair-scopes.log`.
+
+The CAS saga is stage → SHA-256 → `cas:{scope_key}:{sha256}` lock → row lookup or
+resurrection → conditional promotion → edge → SQL commit. SQL rollback after
+promotion can leave an orphan. Reconcile recognizes both key layouts, waits for
+`orphan_grace_hours`, and rechecks row absence under the same CAS lock before
+removing bytes. GC uses the blob row's `scope_key` with that identical namespace.

# COMPLETE FILE CHANGELOG.md
```
# Changelog

All notable changes to **Vaulter** will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/),
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

## [Unreleased]

### Added
- Lossless `BlobScope`, `scope_key`, upgrade migration №7 and resumable
  `vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes --dry-run|--execute` (AD20).
- Batch ACL evaluation (`AccessResolver::evaluateMany`, `AclFolder`), `NodePage`,
  `ExactListing`, signed query-bound cursors and `listing.scan_budget` (AD12).
- Independent `ids.host_keys` configuration (default `varchar(64)`), `HostKeyColumns`
  and `VaulterSchema::hostKey()` for host-owned keys (N02, AD25).
- `InvalidDestinationException` (409 `invalid_destination`), `NodeNotTrashedException`
  (409 `node_not_trashed`), `InvalidNodeNameException` (422 `validation_failed`) and
  `Vaulter\Support\NodeName::normalize()` for tree and name invariants (AD13).
- `VaulterDatabase::lockTreeShared()`: the shared mode of the drive tree lock, taken by
  creates, uploads, copies and renames (AD13, D21).

### Changed
- CAS commit and content-key contracts take `BlobScope`; UUID/ULID scopes use
  NULL-free uniqueness and the same `cas:{scope_key}:{sha256}` lock in commit, GC
  and reconcile. Existing blob paths and disks remain authoritative (AD20).
- Children, search, trash, shared, folder children and Filament now display only nodes
  with effective View for every driver. Shared uses cursors instead of page/total;
  REST listings omit totals and reuse batch ACL results for abilities (AD12).
- `vaulter-documents` and `vaulter-filament` require `vaulter-drive` at runtime (AD02).
- HTTP routes use separate `routes.middleware.authenticated`, `public` and `hub`
  stacks; protected routes default to `auth:sanctum`. Legacy `middleware` overrides
  only the authenticated stack, and metrics are appended to all stacks (AD31).
- Domain events carry a readonly `eventId` ULID with an optional final constructor
  argument for replay. Webhook `event_id` now identifies an event occurrence rather
  than its resulting state; retries retain the same id (AD22).
- Domain HTTP errors include a stable `code` through `ProblemException` (AD29).
  Name conflicts now return 409 `node_name_conflict` (previously 422); assembled
  upload MIME mismatches return 422 `upload_rejected` with `reason: mime_mismatch`
  (previously 415). Existing validation fields and tus headers are preserved.
- `vaulter-azgard` now follows the public AzGuard 0.3 line and its widened
  `BackedEnum` panel/role contracts; CI checks out the latest public AzGuard commit
  directly from GitHub without a private repository token.

### Fixed
- A node created with no parent (notably documents) is placed under the drive root
  instead of becoming a hidden second root; upgrade migration №6 moves existing second
  roots under their root (renaming clashes ` (legacy N)`, logged as `node.moved`) and
  adds a unique one-root-per-drive index. The repair is irreversible (N04, AD13).
- Create, upload, move, copy and restore only target live folders of the same drive,
  checked under row locks; the root can no longer be renamed, moved, copied, trashed
  or deleted; moves take the drive tree lock so concurrent moves cannot form a cycle;
  `forceDelete` only purges trashed nodes and node names are normalized (N05, AD13).
- Host owner, subject, author, tenant and actor columns now use `ids.host_keys`
  instead of Vaulter's entity-ID strategy. Upgrade migration №3 preserves existing
  values and restores the strategy's types on rollback (N02, AD25).
- Repository metadata, exception documentation, collaboration errors and install
  star prompt now point to `axiomasoft/vaulter`; package vendor names stay
  `axioma-studio/vaulter-*` (N20/E6, AD02).
- Filament Move filters destinations and checks workspace creation and destination
  Create access at action time, notifying the actor on denial (N14).
- Vaulter resource resolution no longer registers global `node`/`storage` bindings
  that interfere with host routes. Missing resources return their 404 problem code (N10, AD31).
- Outbox deduplication no longer loses repeated rename or trash/restore operations
  returning to the same state; only a replay of the same event id is deduplicated (E8).
- All domain exceptions now return their documented 4xx status instead of HTTP 500
  on JSON requests and Vaulter routes (N11, AD29); configuration and infrastructure
  errors retain Laravel's default handling.
- Vaulter transactions, CAS/tree locks, raw queries and after-commit callbacks now use
  `vaulter.connection` through the new `Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase` singleton,
  preserving atomicity when the host default connection differs (N01, AD25).
  UploadSession, UploadPart, NodeComment and NodeDocumentVersion also resolve that
  connection so their writes cannot autocommit outside those transactions.
- Release workflows now keep the root npm lockfile, test the npm workspace before
  publishing, pass the PHP split token through the action's supported environment,
  reject tags whose exact SHA lacks the ordinary CI checks, and publish the GHCR
  image under the source repository owner.

## [0.2.0] — 2026-07-15

### Changed
- **Mutation testing moved off Infection onto native Pest mutation, with real thresholds
  everywhere** (VLT-CANON Open risk #4 / VLT-REMED D38). Every package lane —
  `documents`, `drive`, `media`, `filament` — carried an Infection step under `|| true`, and
  **none of them had ever executed a single mutant**: Infection 0.33.3 ships no Pest adapter
  (its `testFramework` enum is `phpunit`/`phpspec`/`codeception`/`testo`), so it could not map
  this suite's tests to mutants at all. `|| true` reported that as green. The root lane had
  already moved to `pest --mutate` for `vaulter-core`; the four package lanes now follow.
  Each has its own mutation job with a coverage driver and a **real threshold with no
  `|| true`** — Pest exits non-zero below it. `--min=95` (= `floor(Score) - 5`) from a measured
  baseline: `core` and all four packages score **100.00%** (documents 1472 mutants, drive 1569,
  media 380, filament 189). The four `infection.json5` files are gone, `infection/infection` is
  out of every `require-dev`, and the `mutate` composer scripts plus `make mutate` (which still
  invoked the `vendor/bin/infection` removed in P5.9) now call Pest.
- **Re-platformed into an embeddable virtual document storage** (#20). The document/folder
  model gave way to a **Drive** domain — storages and a recursive node tree (folders/files) —
  backed by a content-addressable blob store (CAS). Collaboration became **node-attached** and
  standardised on **Hocuspocus** (Reverb is now for notifications, not collaboration). The
  release artifacts are the six split Composer packages `vaulter-core`, `vaulter-media`,
  `vaulter-drive`, `vaulter-documents`, `vaulter-filament` and `vaulter-azgard`.
- **`@vaulter/vue` (npm) ships outside this git tag, on its own track** (D3). The monorepo git
  tag `v0.2.0` is a Composer/PHP versioning event covering the six packages above; the Vue client
  (`@vaulter/vue`, `0.0.0`) versions on its independent npm cycle and is not part of this
  release's frozen public surface.
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0, MAJOR): `NodeGrantCreated`, `NodeGrantRevoked`, `NodeShareCreated` and
  `NodeShareRevoked` are no longer deferred to commit** (security remediation P0.2 / F-P1.7-03).
  All four events dropped `ShouldDispatchAfterCommit` and are now dispatched **synchronously
  inside the grant/share mutation's database transaction**, so the outbox and activity rows stage
  atomically with the mutation — closing a crash window where a committed grant/share could lose
  its outbox/activity row. Consequence for listeners: a listener on any of these four events now
  runs **inside the still-open transaction**. It **must be synchronous and idempotent**; a
  `ShouldQueue` listener, or one that assumes an after-commit guarantee, can now run against a
  mutation that is later rolled back, or race the transaction outright. Drive queued or
  after-commit side-effects off the durable outbox/webhook relay instead of off these events
  directly. See `UPGRADE.md` for migration guidance.

### Removed
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0): the CAS engine moved out of `vaulter-core` into the new standalone package
  `axioma-studio/vaulter-media`** (P2 #5, D2/D3). The concrete content-addressed blob engine —
  `Sha256ContentStore`, `FlysystemStorageDriver`, `BlobStore`, `BlobGarbageCollector`, `BlobReconciler`,
  `StagedTempObject` — and the `vaulter:gc-blobs` / `vaulter:reconcile-blobs` commands moved from
  `Vaulter\Cas\*` to `Vaulter\Media\Cas\*` / `Vaulter\Media\Console\*`. Hosts referencing those FQCNs
  directly, or relying on `vaulter-core` alone for Drive file upload/download/versioning, must now also
  `require axioma-studio/vaulter-media`. `Contracts\Cas\*`, the `v_blobs` schema, `Models\Cas\Blob`,
  `Enums\Cas\BlobStatus` and `Repositories\Cas\BlobStoreRepository` remain in core. Config paths
  (`vaulter.cas.*`, `vaulter.dedup.*`) are unchanged. Dependency direction is one-way (media → core; no cycle).
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0): the file/folder tree, storage manager and resumable uploads moved out of
  `vaulter-core` into the new standalone package `axioma-studio/vaulter-drive`** (P2 #5, D1). The
  Drive Actions (`TrashNodeAction`, `UploadFileAction`), `DriveContext`, `DriveManager`,
  `StorageManager`, the `vaulter:purge-trash` / `vaulter:sweep-uploads` commands, Drive events
  (`FileUploaded`, `NodeCopied`, `NodeCreated`, `NodeMoved`, `NodeRenamed`, `NodeTrashed`,
  `NodeVersionCommitted`, `StorageCreated`, …), the Drive/Storage/Upload HTTP controllers, requests
  and resources, and the whole `Uploads\*` subtree (tus + S3-multipart handlers, upload session/part
  models and repositories) moved from `Vaulter\*` to `Vaulter\Drive\*`. Hosts referencing those FQCNs
  directly, or relying on `vaulter-core` alone for `Vaulter::for()`/`forStorage()`, node CRUD, uploads
  or downloads, must now also `require axioma-studio/vaulter-drive`.
  `Contracts\Drive\{DriveHandle,DriveHandleFactory}`, `Support\DriveNameRules`,
  `Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNameConflictException`, `Http\Resources\Drive\NodeResource` and the
  `v_nodes`/`v_upload_sessions`/`v_upload_parts` schemas remain in core.
  `vaulter.models.{upload_session,upload_part}` are no longer defaulted by core and now
  guard-throw when the drive package is absent; other config paths are unchanged. Dependency
  direction is one-way (drive → core+media; no cycle).
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0): document editing, version history, comments and realtime collaboration
  moved out of `vaulter-core` into the new standalone package `axioma-studio/vaulter-documents`**
  (P2 #5, D2). The document Actions (`CreateDocumentNodeAction`, `UpdateDocumentContentAction`,
  `CreateNamedVersionAction`, `RestoreDocumentVersionAction`, `AddNodeCommentAction`,
  `ResolveNodeCommentAction`, `DeleteNodeCommentAction`, `PersistYjsSnapshotAction`), the default
  `Access\HostUserResolver`, the `Collaboration\*` drivers (`HocuspocusNodeCollaborationDriver`,
  `ReverbNodeCollaborationDriver`, `NodeCollaborationDriverManager`, `CollaborationCall`,
  `CollaborationCallResolver`), the document handlers (`TipTapHandler`, `PreviewHandler`,
  `DownloadOnlyHandler`, `UniverHandler`), the `HtmlExporter` / `MarkdownExporter`, the
  `NodeComment` / `NodeDocumentVersion` models and the document/collaboration/comment HTTP
  controllers moved from `Vaulter\*` to `Vaulter\Documents\*`. Hosts referencing those FQCNs
  directly, or relying on `vaulter-core` alone for document editing, versioning, comments or
  collaboration, must now also `require axioma-studio/vaulter-documents`.
  The handler-seam contracts (`FileHandler`, `DocumentContent`, `DocumentExporter`,
  `HandlerRegistry`, `NodeHandlerResolver`), the value objects (`FileDescriptor`,
  `HandlerCapabilities`, `HandlerUi`, `HandlerDescriptor`, `TipTapContent`,
  `OpaqueDocumentContent`) and the `v_node_comments`/`v_node_document_versions` schemas remain
  in core. **Their namespaces were subsequently renamed in the same unreleased cycle** — see the
  kind-neutral-rename entry below for the FQCNs a consumer actually lands on
  (`Contracts\Handlers\*` / `ValueObjects\Handlers\*`).
  `vaulter.models.{node_comment,node_document_version}` are no longer defaulted by core and now
  guard-throw when the documents package is absent; `vaulter.exporters` defaults to `[]` in core
  (`vaulter.handlers` has since gained a core-owned baseline — see VLT-CANON P0.4 below).
  Dependency direction is one-way (documents → core; no cycle) — `vaulter-drive` is not a runtime
  dependency of `vaulter-documents`.
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0): the format-handler contract is kind-neutral now — `DocumentHandler` →
  `FileHandler`, `Contracts\Documents\*` → `Contracts\Handlers\*`** (VLT-CANON P0.1, D7). The
  contract describes ANY file (image/pdf/download-only), not only editable documents, so
  "Engine" / "Document" naming was misleading for the built-in `DownloadOnlyHandler` /
  `PreviewHandler` fallbacks. Renamed: `Contracts\Documents\DocumentHandler` →
  `Contracts\Handlers\FileHandler`; `Contracts\Documents\{HandlerRegistry,NodeHandlerResolver,
  DocumentContent,DocumentExporter}` moved to `Contracts\Handlers\*` (same class names);
  `ValueObjects\Documents\DocumentCapabilities` → `ValueObjects\Handlers\HandlerCapabilities`;
  `ValueObjects\Documents\{HandlerDescriptor,HandlerUi,FileDescriptor}` moved to
  `ValueObjects\Handlers\*` (same class names). `ValueObjects\Documents\{TipTapContent,
  OpaqueDocumentContent,LinkSchemeValidator}` were unaffected by this item — they have since
  moved to `vaulter-documents` in P0.3 (see below). The npm package `@vaulter/vue` renamed the mirrored TS type
  `DocumentCapabilities` → `HandlerCapabilities` (`types.ts`); `HandlerDescriptor`/`HandlerUi`
  are unchanged. `config('vaulter.handlers')` / `config('vaulter.exporters')` keys are
  unaffected. See `UPGRADE.md` for the FQCN migration table.
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0): the `DocumentExporter::export()` parameter WIDENS to the opaque
  `DocumentContent` (was the concrete `TipTapContent`)** (VLT-CANON P0.2). The contract no
  longer assumes TipTap. Callers are unaffected (a `TipTapContent` is a `DocumentContent`);
  **implementations break**: an exporter still declaring `export(TipTapContent $content)` is
  no longer signature-compatible and fatals on class load. A custom exporter must widen its
  parameter to `Vaulter\Contracts\Handlers\DocumentContent` and narrow internally to the
  concrete shape it renders — the built-in `HtmlExporter` / `MarkdownExporter` narrow to
  `TipTapContent` and throw the new
  `Vaulter\Exceptions\Documents\UnsupportedDocumentContentException` otherwise. See
  `UPGRADE.md`.
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0): TipTap's content shapes moved out of `vaulter-core` into
  `vaulter-documents`** (VLT-CANON P0.3). `core` declares only the kind-neutral `DocumentContent`
  contract; it must not know any handler's concrete shape. Moved (same class names, new FQCN
  root `Vaulter\Documents\*`): `ValueObjects\Documents\{TipTapContent,LinkSchemeValidator,
  OpaqueDocumentContent}` → `Documents\ValueObjects\*`; `Attributes\TiptapExtensions` →
  `Documents\Attributes\TiptapExtensions`; `Exceptions\Documents\InvalidTipTapContentException` →
  `Documents\Exceptions\InvalidTipTapContentException`. The store-time allow-list, read-time
  decay and URL-scheme scrub (F-P1.11-01/-02, R-01/R-11/R-12) are unchanged byte-for-byte. A
  core-only host (no `vaulter-documents` installed) is unaffected. `config('vaulter.tiptap.*')`
  stays in core (host-facing config, not a TipTap shape). See `UPGRADE.md`.
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0): the format-handler registry ENGINE moved from `vaulter-documents` to
  core** (VLT-CANON P0.4, D10 — superseding H.5 / the T5 Option-B topology allocation).
  `DocumentHandlerRegistry`, `NodeHandlerResolver`, `DocumentFileDescriptorFactory`, the
  `Capability` enum and the baseline `PreviewHandler`/`DownloadOnlyHandler` moved to
  `Vaulter\Engines\*` / `Vaulter\Enums\Handlers\Capability` (same class names, new FQCN root); a
  drive-only host with no `vaulter-documents` installed now resolves a REAL preview/download
  handler descriptor for every node instead of a silent `null` degrade.
  `Vaulter::registerHandler()` / `Vaulter::handlers()` no longer throw `MissingPackageException`
  when `vaulter-documents` is absent — the seam is core-owned and unconditional now.
  `vaulter-documents` keeps only its own format additions (`TipTapHandler`, the feature-flagged
  `UniverHandler`). The baseline `PreviewHandler`/`DownloadOnlyHandler` are also registered under
  the container tag `'vaulter.handlers'`, so a host carrying a pre-P0.4 published config
  (`'handlers' => []`, which wins the shallow config merge) does not silently lose preview. See
  `UPGRADE.md`.
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0): `v_node_document_versions.format` no longer carries a schema
  `default('tiptap')`; the autosave and Yjs-snapshot writers now stamp the format explicitly**
  (VLT-CANON P0.4). `UpdateDocumentContentAction` and `PersistYjsSnapshotAction` previously let
  the DB default fill `format` when appending a NEW version row to an existing document — so a
  **markdown or univer document's autosave rows were stamped `'tiptap'` regardless of its real
  format**. They now carry the head version's format forward. The value reaches clients through
  `NodeDocumentVersionResource.format` and the version-history API: **a client branching on
  `format === 'tiptap'` must be updated.** `CreateNamedVersionAction`'s no-head fallback likewise
  changed from the literal `'tiptap'` to `DocumentType::RichText->value` (`'richtext'`). No
  backfill ships — pre-existing rows keep their stored value; `format` is an advisory label, the
  handler is always resolved from the node's mime (D4). See `UPGRADE.md`.
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0): `HandlerCapabilities::$richText` now defaults to `false` (was `true`)**
  (VLT-CANON P0.5). A kind-neutral handler makes no rich-text claim unless it opts in. The two
  built-in editable handlers are unaffected in behaviour (`TipTapHandler` now declares
  `richText: true` explicitly; `UniverHandler` already declared `richText: false`); a
  third-party handler relying on the implicit `true` default must now set it explicitly.
- **`NodeExportService::contentOf()` is now kind-neutral** (VLT-CANON P0.5, closing a P0.2/P0.3/
  P0.4 open finding). A non-rich-text node's export now returns its own `OpaqueDocumentContent`
  shape instead of decaying to an empty TipTap document — `HtmlExporter`/`MarkdownExporter`'s
  existing narrowing guard now correctly rejects it with `UnsupportedDocumentContentException`
  instead of silently rendering blank content. `CreateDocumentNodeData::$format`'s default
  changed from the invalid literal `'tiptap'` to `DocumentType::RichText->value` (`'richtext'`).
  See `UPGRADE.md`.
- **The AzGuard bridge is named `axioma-studio/vaulter-azgard`, not
  `axioma-studio/vaulter-permissions-azgard`** (VLT-CANON P2.1, final structural canon). The
  package directory moved `packages/permissions-azgard/` → `packages/azgard/` and the namespace
  `Vaulter\Permissions\Azgard\*` → `Vaulter\Azgard\*`. The core-owned contract seam
  `Vaulter\Contracts\Access\AzgardGuard` is unaffected — only the bridge package's own name and
  namespace changed. **Note this is not a released-package break:** the bridge has never been
  published (the repo carries no tag, and the split/Packagist pipeline is tag-triggered), so
  `vaulter-permissions-azgard` never existed on Packagist and the `Added` entry below lists the
  bridge under its final name. Only a host tracking this monorepo's dev branch — requiring the old
  package name via a path/VCS repo, or importing `Vaulter\Permissions\Azgard\*` FQCNs — has to
  update. See `UPGRADE.md`.
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0): catch-all folders resolved by role** (VLT-CANON P2.3, final structural
  canon). `documents/src/Documents/` (tautological name), the TipTap-only slice of
  `documents/src/Support/`, and the pure-helper slice of `drive/src/Uploads/` were dissolved into
  the domain-service folder/layer matching each class's actual role: `TipTapHandler`/`UniverHandler`
  → `Vaulter\Documents\Engines\Handlers`; `NodeExportService` → `Vaulter\Documents\Export`;
  `CollaborationSessionService` → `Vaulter\Documents\Collaboration`; `TipTapRenderer`/
  `HasTiptapExtensions` → `Vaulter\Documents\Support\TipTap(\Concerns)`; `MagicByteSniffer`/
  `StagedUpload`/`UploadModels` → `Vaulter\Drive\Support\Uploads`. Pure move, zero behaviour
  change. See `UPGRADE.md`.
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0): `documents/src/Access/HostUserResolver` moved to
  `Vaulter\Documents\Collaboration\HostUserResolver`** (VLT-CANON P2.4, final structural canon).
  `Access/` held a single class that wasn't an access-control class — it implements the
  collaboration webhook's `HostUserResolver` contract, not anything ACL-shaped — so it now sits
  next to the collaboration drivers it serves. core's `Access/` and `Webhooks/` domain-service
  folders were audited against ADR-0017 in the same pass and left unchanged (they conform). Pure
  move, zero behaviour change. See `UPGRADE.md`.
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0): the legacy `document_id`-bound DocVault path was excised**
  (ADR-0014 step 2), leaving a single canonical `v_nodes` document path (kind=document
  nodes with node-attached comments and versions). Removed: the `Document`,
  `DocumentFolder`, `DocumentVersion`, `DocumentPermission`, `DocumentComment` and
  `DocumentShare` models and their `v_document*` tables and migrations; the legacy
  document/folder/version/permission/comment/share repositories, controllers, Actions,
  events, resources and requests; the `Document`-bound collaboration driver
  (`Contracts\Collaboration\CollaborationDriver` + its Reverb/Hocuspocus implementations
  and `Vaulter::driver()`); the `Contracts\Access\PermissionResolver` seam
  (`Vaulter::resolvePermissionsWith()` / `permissionResolver()` and the
  `vaulter.permission_resolver` config key); and the
  `vaulter.models.{document,version,folder,permission,comment,share}` config entries with
  their table names. A dev-only, idempotent `drop_legacy_docvault_tables` migration drops
  any pre-existing `v_document*` tables. Use `Vaulter::for()` and the node/document
  endpoints instead.
- **BREAKING (pre-1.0): `@vaulter/vue` dropped the legacy document-centric surface**
  (ADR-0014 step 4), which called the removed `/documents`, `/folders`, `/documents/*`
  endpoints. Removed: the client namespaces `documents`, `versions`, `folders`,
  `permissions`, `shares` and legacy `collaboration`; the composables `useDocuments`,
  `usePermissions`, `useShares`, `useDocumentExport`, `useCollaboration`, `useVaulterEditor`;
  the components `VaulterFileManager`, `VaulterEditor`, `VaulterVersionHistory`,
  `VaulterPermissionsPanel`, `VaulterSharePanel`, `VaulterBreadcrumb`; and the legacy
  `createCollaborationSession` transport. Use the Drive surface instead — `client.nodes`/
  `useNodes`/`VaulterDrive`, `client.grants`/`useNodeGrants`, `client.nodeShares`/
  `useNodeShares`, `useDocumentNode`, `useNodeCollaboration`. `useComments` /
  `VaulterCommentsPanel` were repointed to the node comment API (`/nodes/{node}/comments`,
  prop `nodeId` instead of `documentId`).

### Added
- **`@vaulter/vue` explicit engine-component registry** (VLT-CANON P1.2) —
  `registerEngineComponent(id, component)` / `unregisterEngineComponent(id)` /
  `resolveEngineComponent(id)` / `registeredEngineComponentIds()` / `clearEngineComponents()`,
  formalising `VaulterDocumentNode`'s `ui.component`→editor mapping as a public, explicitly
  imported API (not an ambient global). `componentMap` stays fully supported and takes
  precedence; the registry is an additional global fallback for hosts with many mount points.
  In development builds, an unmapped handler now logs one `console.warn` per unique
  `ui.component`/`id`. Purely additive — no existing prop or behaviour changed.
- **Config-first Storage Panels** — `Vaulter::for()`, per-panel table prefixes and id strategy
  (`bigint` | `ulid` | `uuid7`), with a per-panel schema/migration generator.
- **Content-addressable blob store (CAS)** — scoped dedup (global/tenant/storage), race-safe
  commit (advisory-lock on Postgres), two-phase garbage collection and a reconcile command.
- **Composite ACL** — `AccessResolver` over node grants, host-role drivers and share links, with
  access-aware listing; **four permission drivers**: Database (default), Gate, Spatie and AzGuard.
- **Real-time collaboration (node-attached)** — Hocuspocus driver, Yjs snapshot persistence and a
  TipTap projection; JWT-authenticated rooms bound to a single node.
- **Resumable uploads** — tus + S3-multipart, server-recomputed content hashes, a sweep command.
- **Append-only activity feed** and **transactional-outbox webhooks** with a single relay-sweep
  owner and idempotent delivery.
- **Handler-aware documents** — rich-text (TipTap) and opaque snapshots (e.g. the Univer
  spreadsheet handler), plus a handler-aware create-path (`mime` resolves the node's handler).
- **AzGuard bridge** (`axioma-studio/vaulter-azgard`) — a real `AzgardGuard` adapter
  delegating to AzGuard scoped roles and workspace-context checks, keeping core AzGuard-free.
- **Vue client** (`@vaulter/vue`) — Drive components (folder-picker, no `window.prompt`),
  shared-with-me view, node-collaboration composables and the headless spreadsheet shell.
- **Full Postgres portability lane** running the schema-portable suite against real Postgres
  alongside the default in-memory SQLite.
- **Pluggable export formats**: register a `\Vaulter\Contracts\DocumentExporter` via
  `config('vaulter.exporters')` or `Vaulter::registerExporter()` to export documents to custom
  formats (PDF, DOCX, …) alongside the built-in HTML and Markdown. The export response now also
  returns the exporter's `mime_type`; binary exporters return base64-encoded `content`.
- **Swappable share-link token generator**: `config('vaulter.share_token_generator')` — implement
  `\Vaulter\Contracts\ShareTokenGenerator` to mint short, hashed or prefixed share tokens (the
  default stays a 48-char random string).
- **Configurable table prefix**: `config('vaulter.table_prefix')` (env `VAULTER_TABLE_PREFIX`,
  default `v_`) prefixes every Vaulter table. Published migrations and models resolve table names
  through it, so the package coexists with host tables and naming conventions.

## [0.1.0] - 2026-06-14

First public release. Vaulter is a complete, embeddable document-management package for Laravel
with a TypeScript front-end and an optional Filament admin.

### Added
- **Filament admin** (`axioma-studio/vaulter-filament`): an optional Filament 5 plugin
  (`VaulterPlugin`) adding **Documents** (searchable table, edit form, trash filter, delete/restore/
  force-delete), **Folders** (CRUD) and **Share-links** (list + revoke) resources, each resolving the
  configured model. Self-contained package with an isolated CI workflow so Filament never constrains
  the core Laravel 12/13 matrix.
- Initial monorepo scaffold: `packages/core` (Laravel package), `packages/vue`
  (`@vaulter/vue` components), `hocuspocus-server/` (collaboration server) and
  VitePress documentation under `docs/`.
- Development tooling: Pint, PHPStan (**level max**) + Larastan, Rector, Pest + Orchestra
  Testbench + type-coverage, Infection, GitHub Actions CI and a `Makefile`.
- **M1 core domain** (`packages/core`):
  - Enums with attribute metadata (`DocumentType`, `PermissionLevel`, `CollaborationDriverType`),
    value objects (`TipTapContent`, `WorkspaceRef`), DTOs, domain events and a typed exception
    hierarchy.
  - Final readonly Actions (documents, versions, folders, permissions, collaboration token) over
    read/store repositories with a `DocumentVisibilityService`.
  - Swappable models with dual key mode (`id` | `ulid`) and optional `tenant_id` via
    `config('vaulter.models'|'id_type'|'tenant')`.
  - REST API (documents, versions, folders, permissions, collab token) with `JsonResource`
    responses that embed computed `abilities` (`AbilitiesDto`).
  - Collaboration driver abstraction: `ReverbCollaborationDriver` (default) and
    `HocuspocusCollaborationDriver` (optional), plus broadcast-channel authorization.
  - `PermissionResolver`, `StorageAdapter`, `CollaborationDriver` contracts; `VaulterManager`
    + facade.
- **M2 frontend** (`@vaulter/vue`): TypeScript-first rewrite.
  - Typed API client (`createVaulterClient`) covering documents, versions, folders, permissions
    and the collaboration token, with `VaulterApiError` and full request/response types.
  - Composables `useVaulterClient`, `useDocuments`, `useVaulterEditor`, `useCollaboration`,
    `usePermissions`.
  - Slot-driven components wired to the client and consuming computed `abilities`.
  - `VaulterPlugin` provides the client app-wide; Vite library build emits ES + UMD + `.d.ts`.
  - `Frontend` CI workflow (Node 20/22: type-check, Vitest, build).
- **M3 collaboration runtime** (backend + transport):
  - Server-side persistence of merged content: `POST /documents/{id}/collab/sync`
    (`SyncCollaborationStateAction`) updates the document and dispatches the broadcastable
    `DocumentContentSynced` event on the private channel `vaulter.documents.{id}`.
  - `@vaulter/vue`: `createCollaborationSession` (Hocuspocus out of the box, injectable Reverb
    bridge) and a `useCollaboration` that exposes a `Y.Doc` plus `connect`/`disconnect`.
- **Comments**: threaded, optionally anchored document comments.
  - `v_document_comments` model (swappable), `AddComment`/`ResolveComment`/`DeleteComment`
    actions, REST API under `/documents/{id}/comments` gated by the `comment` / `manage`
    abilities, and a broadcast `CommentAdded` (`comment.added`) + `CommentResolved` events.
  - `@vaulter/vue`: `useComments` composable, `VaulterCommentsPanel` component, client methods
    and types.
- **Public share links**: tokenized, level-scoped, optionally expiring document shares.
  - `v_document_shares` model (swappable), `CreateShareLink` / `RevokeShareLink` actions, managed
    under `/documents/{id}/shares` (gated by `manage`) with a **public** `GET /shares/{token}`
    that returns the document with abilities derived from the share level; `ShareLinkCreated` /
    `ShareLinkRevoked` events; `DocumentAbilities::fromLevel()`.
  - `@vaulter/vue`: `useShares` composable, `VaulterSharePanel` component, client methods (incl.
    `shares.resolve(token)`) and types.
- **Export**: render documents to HTML or Markdown.
  - `TipTapRenderer` (common nodes/marks, escaped HTML) + `ExportFormat` enum and a
    `GET /documents/{id}/export?format=` endpoint (gated by `view`) returning
    `{ format, content, filename }`.
  - `@vaulter/vue`: `useDocumentExport` composable (with a browser `download` helper) and a
    `documents.export(id, format)` client method.
- **Bulk operations**: `POST /documents/bulk` applies one action (`delete` / `restore` / `move` /
  `force-delete`) to many documents, authorizing each individually and returning `processed` vs
  `skipped` ids (`BulkAction` enum, `BulkDocumentController`). `@vaulter/vue`: `documents.bulk`
  client method and `useDocuments.bulk`.
- **Document operations**: move & duplicate.
  - `PATCH /documents/{id}/move` (`MoveDocumentAction`, gated by `edit`) re-files a document into a
    folder of the same workspace (cross-workspace → `422`) and dispatches `DocumentMoved`;
    `POST /documents/{id}/duplicate` (`DuplicateDocumentAction`, gated by `view` + `create`) clones it.
  - `@vaulter/vue`: `documents.move` / `documents.duplicate` client methods and `useDocuments`
    `move` / `duplicate`.
- **Search & trash**: document discovery and lifecycle.
  - The document list accepts a `q` term (title + content `LIKE`, workspace-scoped). A trash
    listing (`GET /documents/trash`) and permanent `DELETE /documents/{id}/force` (gated by
    `manage`) round out soft-delete/restore; `DocumentPurged` event on hard delete.
  - `@vaulter/vue`: `useDocuments` gains `listTrashed` / `forceDelete` (and `q` on `list`); client
    `documents.trashed` / `documents.forceDelete`.
- Documentation: architecture, embedding, search & trash, permission resolver, abilities,
  collaboration, comments, sharing, export, error reference, the TypeScript frontend guide, and
  ADRs 0001–0004.

[Unreleased]: https://github.com/axioma-studio/vaulter/compare/v0.2.0...HEAD
[0.2.0]: https://github.com/axioma-studio/vaulter/releases/tag/v0.2.0

# Upgrade Guide

This file documents breaking changes and how to migrate through them. Entries are ordered
newest-first. See `CHANGELOG.md` for the full list of changes per release; this file only covers
changes that require action from consumers of the package.

## Unreleased

### Lossless CAS scope and migration №7 (AD20)

Replace `BlobStore::commit($stream, tenantId: ..., storageId: ...)` with
`commit($stream, BlobScope::forUpload($dedupScope, $tenantId, (string) $storageId), $disk)`.
`ContentStore::keyFor()` now receives `Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope`.
Host tenant IDs and drive IDs remain strings without numeric coercion. Tenant scope
with no tenant uses `BlobScope::global()`. Scope IDs contain 1–96 bytes; IDs outside
`[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}` are encoded as unpadded base64url in physical paths.

Migration `2026_10_01_000007_blob_scope_key` backfills the NOT NULL `scope_key`
and replaces the dedup index with `UNIQUE(scope_key, sha256)`. It preserves all
existing `storage_key` values and objects. A uniqueness conflict stops migration
before the old index is removed; investigate the conflicting rows before retrying.
Custom blob schemas must also provide `scope_key`.

After the SQL migrations, inspect and repair mixed legacy areas:

```bash
php artisan vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes --dry-run
php artisan vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes --execute
```

Exactly one mode is required. Review `hash_mismatch` and `unreferenced` reports;
these blobs remain unchanged. Each target commits independently, so a failed copy
can be retried. Old objects stay until GC; successful moves are journalled in
`storage/logs/vaulter-repair-scopes.log`. Target scope comes from revision → file →
node → storage; no tenant is guessed. A storage with NULL tenant belongs to the
host-wide global scope. Reads always use the disk and path recorded on the blob.
Run with operational access to all storages in the affected panel, and pause other
operators changing tenant assignments during repair.

The migration's `down()` restores the historical structure using the current
`dedup.scope`; it does not undo object copies or redirected edges. If repaired
rows conflict with the old index, rollback fails before removing `scope_key`.
Take a backup before upgrading or repairing data.

### Exact listing and shared cursors (AD12)

Before: children, search, trash and Filament exposed grant-reachable candidates,
including denied nodes; `shared` used `page`, `current_page` and `total`.
After: all these surfaces and folder `show` children apply effective `View` for every
driver. REST listings return `meta.next_cursor`, `per_page`, `scanned`, `path` and a
null `prev_cursor`; `total` and numbered page links are omitted. Replace shared
page-number iteration with `cursor` iteration, and continue through empty pages
while `next_cursor` is non-null. `vaulter.listing.scan_budget` defaults to 1000;
raise it to inspect more candidates per request. Invalid or cross-query cursors
return 422 `validation_failed` with `errors.cursor`.

New PHP surface: `AccessResolver::evaluateMany()`, `AclFolder`, `NodePage` and
`ExactListing`. Listing resource abilities use the batch grant sets.

### Tree invariants, second-root repair and migration №6 (AD13)

Before: Actions accepted any node of the drive as a parent, so a folder, upload, move,
copy or document could land inside a file, a document or a trashed folder; a `null`
parent in `CreateDocumentNodeAction` (and in Drive Actions called directly) wrote a
second parentless node that the root listing never showed; the root could be renamed,
moved or trashed; two concurrent moves could form a cycle; `forceDelete` purged live
nodes; restoring or trashing twice was a silent no-op. After:

- A parent or destination must be a live folder of the same drive, checked under row
  locks inside the transaction: otherwise `InvalidDestinationException`
  (409, `invalid_destination`); a folder of another drive raises
  `CrossStorageMoveException` (409, `cross_drive_operation`) from Drive Actions and
  `InvalidDestinationException` from `CreateDocumentNodeAction`.
- A `null` destination always means the drive root (`root_node_id`).
- The root cannot be renamed, moved, copied, trashed or force-deleted (409, `invalid_destination`).
- Trashing a node already in the trash raises `InvalidDestinationException`; restoring or
  force-deleting a node that is not in the trash raises `NodeNotTrashedException`
  (409, `node_not_trashed`). Delete a live node by trashing it first.
- Every tree write takes the drive tree lock as its first statement: moves, restores, trashes
  and force deletes exclusively (`VaulterDatabase::lockTree()`), creates, uploads, copies and
  renames shared (`VaulterDatabase::lockTreeShared()`, new). Concurrent moves can no longer form a
  cycle (`CycleException`, 409, `node_move_cycle`) and a create cannot slip under a folder being
  trashed. On PostgreSQL the shared lock keeps uploads concurrent with each other but a structural
  operation waits for in-flight uploads of its drive; on MySQL/MariaDB and SQLite the lock is the
  storage row and tree writes of one drive run one at a time. Custom Actions that change the
  tree should take the same locks before locking node rows.
- Names written by create, upload, rename, copy (explicit name) and document creation are
  trimmed, NFC-normalized when ext-intl is installed, and rejected with
  `InvalidNodeNameException` (422, `validation_failed`, `errors.name`) when empty, `.`/`..`,
  longer than `vaulter.drive.name_rules.max_length` UTF-8 bytes, or containing `/`, `\`
  or a control character.

Run `php artisan migrate --force` after a database backup. Migration
`2026_10_01_000006_nodes_cleanup` moves every parentless node other than a drive's
`root_node_id` under that root, renaming it `"<base> (legacy N)<.ext>"` when a live root
child already owns the name, writes a `node.moved` activity row (`actor_type`
`vaulter:system`, `context.reason` `n04_repair`, `context.renamed_from`) per move, and then
adds the unique one-root-per-drive index (`{nodes}_root_uniq`; a generated `root_storage`
column on MySQL/MariaDB). It stops without changes when a drive has several parentless
nodes and no `root_node_id`: set the real root first. The repair is irreversible —
rollback only drops the index. Re-running the migration changes nothing.

### Independent host keys and migration №3 (AD25)

Before: host owner, subject, author, tenant and actor columns inherited Vaulter's
entity-ID strategy (`char(26)` for ULID or bigint). After: all 27 columns use
`vaulter.ids.host_keys`, default `string` (`varchar(64)`), so UUID users and bigint
projects/roles can coexist with ULID Vaulter IDs. `VaulterSchema::hostKey()` is new;
`actor()` and `tenant()` use the host setting. Entity keys and references keep their strategy.

Run `php artisan migrate --force` in a maintenance window after a database backup.
Migration `2026_10_01_000003_host_key_columns` preserves existing values, column
nullability and indexes, uses `vaulter.connection` and legacy table names, and is
idempotent. PostgreSQL casts values with `USING`; MySQL/MariaDB modifies column types;
SQLite requires no type upgrade. The migration does not rename the tenant column.

Set `VAULTER_HOST_KEYS` to `bigint`, `uuid` or `ulid` only when every existing host
key fits that type. Incompatible data raises the database driver's error. Rollback
restores the current Vaulter ID strategy; host keys inserted after upgrading (for
example UUIDs into an old ULID installation) can prevent rollback. Do not assume a
structural rollback can accept every new host key.

### Runtime Drive dependencies (AD02)

Documents and Filament now declare `axioma-studio/vaulter-drive` in `require`
rather than `require-dev`; installing either also installs Drive and its Media
backend. Update your Composer lock accordingly. Filament Move now requires
workspace creation access and Create on the destination, including the storage
root. A denied move leaves the node in place and shows "Move not allowed".

### HTTP middleware and route parameters (AD31)

Replace `vaulter.middleware` with `vaulter.routes.middleware.authenticated` and
remove the legacy key to adopt the default `['api', 'auth:sanctum']`. Install and
configure Sanctum or choose your own host guard. A non-null legacy list still
wins for protected routes, including an empty list. Public shares and signed hub
callbacks use their own `public` and `hub` stacks; metrics are appended once to
all stacks. Replace PHP calls to `Config::middleware()` with the corresponding
`authenticatedMiddleware()`, `publicMiddleware()` or `hubMiddleware()`.

Global route bindings for `node` and `storage` have been removed. Vaulter's middleware
resolves its own parameters through swappable repositories before Laravel's
`SubstituteBindings`; host routes retain their own model bindings. Controller
model signatures and existing route paths are preserved.

### Webhook event occurrence ids (AD22, E8)

Webhook `event_id` values are now 26-character ULIDs generated when a domain event
is constructed. Consumers should deduplicate by the complete `event_id`, without
parsing a topic or node id from it. Repeated operations producing the same state
have different ids; retries of one occurrence keep its original id. Existing queued
rows retain their stored ids, so accept both formats while the backlog drains.

All domain events expose readonly `eventId` and accept an optional final
`?string $eventId = null` constructor argument. Preserve this value when reconstructing
an event for replay. Custom callers of `OutboxRecorder::stage()` using named arguments
must replace `key:` with `eventId:` and pass the originating event's id.

### Domain HTTP error codes and statuses (AD29)

Domain exceptions now implement `Vaulter\Exceptions\Contracts\ProblemException`
with `problemCode(): string` and `httpStatus(): int`. JSON requests and paths under
`vaulter.route_prefix` receive `{message, code}` plus the existing error fields.
PHP callers continue receiving the same exception classes and messages. Host HTML
routes and configuration/infrastructure failures retain Laravel's default handling.

Update HTTP clients for these changes:

- `NodeNameConflictException`: 422 → 409, `code: node_name_conflict`; `errors.name` remains.
- `UploadContentMismatchException`: 415 → 422, `code: upload_rejected`, `reason: mime_mismatch`.
- Document, move and lookup exceptions that previously returned 500 now return the
  4xx statuses listed in [the error reference](docs/guide/errors.md#domain-http-errors).

`OffsetMismatchException` still returns 409 and the authoritative `Upload-Offset`
plus `Tus-Resumable: 1.0.0`. New `NodeNotFoundException::forId()` and
`StorageNotFoundException::forId()` provide 404 codes for resource resolvers.
Custom domain exceptions should implement `ProblemException`; additional JSON fields
can be returned from `protected renderExtras(): array`. RFC 9457 formatting follows
in a later release.

### Dedicated database transactions (AD25)

Transactions, locks and after-commit callbacks previously used the host default connection;
they now follow `vaulter.connection` via `Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase`. The config
key is unchanged. Actions, upload repositories, CAS services and webhook/activity subscribers
now receive this singleton through constructor injection. Resolve them through Laravel's
container, or provide `database: app(VaulterDatabase::class)` when constructing them directly.
Custom transaction code can use the same singleton; acquire tree/CAS locks inside its
`transaction()` callback. The shipped UploadSession, UploadPart, NodeComment and NodeDocumentVersion models
now also follow this key, so their Eloquent writes participate in the same transaction.
A custom replacement model must resolve the same connection to preserve atomicity.

### CI: mutation testing runs on native Pest, Infection is gone (VLT-CANON Open risk #4)

Not consumer-facing — a contributor/CI note; no published API, config key or schema changes.

Every package lane (`documents`, `drive`, `media`, `filament`) ran Infection under `|| true` and
**never executed a mutant**: Infection 0.33.3 has no Pest adapter, so it could not map this
suite's tests to mutants, and `|| true` reported the failure as green. The four lanes now use
the same tool as the root `vaulter-core` lane (`pest --mutate`, VLT-REMED D38/P5.9), each in its
own job with a coverage driver and a real `--min=95` threshold and **no** `|| true`.

What this changes for a contributor:

- `composer mutate` in `packages/{documents,drive,media,filament}` and `make mutate` at the root
  now invoke `vendor/bin/pest --mutate …`, not `vendor/bin/infection` (the root binary has not
  existed since P5.9 — `make mutate` was broken).
- Running mutation testing locally needs a coverage driver (`pcov` or `xdebug`); without one Pest
  reports that coverage is unavailable.
- `infection/infection` is no longer a dev dependency and the four `infection.json5` files are
  removed. If you had local tooling pointing at them, point it at the Pest invocation instead.

Baseline at the time of the switch: `--min=95` = `floor(Score) - 5`, measured 2026-07-13 —
`core`, `documents`, `drive`, `media` and `filament` all score **100.00%**.

### `documents/src/Access/HostUserResolver` moves into `Collaboration/` (VLT-CANON P2.4)

**Why.** `Access/` in `vaulter-documents` held exactly one class, and it was not an access-control
class: `HostUserResolver` implements `Vaulter\Documents\Contracts\Collaboration\HostUserResolver`
and exists purely to let the M4b collaboration webhook materialise an `Authorizable` from a bare
user id (see its own docblock: "purely collaboration-webhook machinery"). Its folder disagreed
with its contract. It now sits next to the collaboration drivers it serves
(`NodeCollaborationDriverManager`, `HocuspocusNodeCollaborationDriver`, …). core's `Access/` and
`Webhooks/` domain-service folders were also audited against ADR-0017 in this pass — both conform
(they are in fact ADR-0017's own reference shape for the pattern) and needed no change.

| Old | New |
|---|---|
| `Vaulter\Documents\Access\HostUserResolver` | `Vaulter\Documents\Collaboration\HostUserResolver` |

Only hosts that bind or reference the *default* implementation directly are affected — the
contract FQCN (`Vaulter\Documents\Contracts\Collaboration\HostUserResolver`) that hosts normally
type-hint or swap is unchanged. Pre-1.0 — hard rename, no alias phase. Pure move: zero behavior
change.

### Catch-all folders resolved by role: `documents/src/Documents`, `documents/src/Support`, `drive/src/Uploads` (VLT-CANON P2.3)

**Why.** Final structural canon (Part 3): `documents/src/Documents/` was a tautologically-named
grab-bag mixing the (already core-owned, VLT-CANON P0.4) engine registry remnants with TipTap/Univer
drivers and two read-only evaluator services; `documents/src/Support/` mixed engine-neutral helpers
with TipTap-only ones; `drive/src/Uploads/` mixed orchestrator classes with pure helpers. Each moved
class now sits in the domain-service folder or layer matching its actual role — the registry/driver
classes join `Engines/Handlers` (mirrors core's `Vaulter\Engines\Handlers`), the two evaluators join
the existing `Export`/`Collaboration` domain-service folders (ADR-0017) alongside their siblings
(`HtmlExporter`/`MarkdownExporter`; `NodeCollaborationDriverManager`/drivers), the TipTap-only
Support helpers get a `TipTap` domain sub-namespace, and the Uploads helpers join a new
`Support/Uploads`. Pre-1.0 — hard rename, no alias phase. Pure move: zero behavior change.

**What moved (documents):**

| Old | New |
|---|---|
| `Vaulter\Documents\Documents\Handlers\TipTapHandler` | `Vaulter\Documents\Engines\Handlers\TipTapHandler` |
| `Vaulter\Documents\Documents\Handlers\UniverHandler` | `Vaulter\Documents\Engines\Handlers\UniverHandler` |
| `Vaulter\Documents\Documents\NodeExportService` | `Vaulter\Documents\Export\NodeExportService` |
| `Vaulter\Documents\Documents\CollaborationSessionService` | `Vaulter\Documents\Collaboration\CollaborationSessionService` |
| `Vaulter\Documents\Support\TipTapRenderer` | `Vaulter\Documents\Support\TipTap\TipTapRenderer` |
| `Vaulter\Documents\Support\Concerns\HasTiptapExtensions` | `Vaulter\Documents\Support\TipTap\Concerns\HasTiptapExtensions` |

`Vaulter\Documents\Support\DocumentContentHasher` is engine-neutral and did NOT move.

**What moved (drive):**

| Old | New |
|---|---|
| `Vaulter\Drive\Uploads\MagicByteSniffer` | `Vaulter\Drive\Support\Uploads\MagicByteSniffer` |
| `Vaulter\Drive\Uploads\StagedUpload` | `Vaulter\Drive\Support\Uploads\StagedUpload` |
| `Vaulter\Drive\Uploads\UploadModels` | `Vaulter\Drive\Support\Uploads\UploadModels` |

`Vaulter\Drive\Uploads\{UploadSessionManager,S3MultipartHandler,TusUploadHandler}` are orchestrators
(`*Manager`/`*Handler`) and stay in `Vaulter\Drive\Uploads` — the domain-service folder shape itself
did not change, only the pure-helper classes it also held moved out.

**Not renamed:** `Vaulter\Contracts\Handlers\*` (the public contract interfaces), `Vaulter\Engines\*`
(the core-owned registry, moved earlier in P0.4), and the `config('vaulter.handlers')`/
`config('vaulter-documents.handlers')` KEYS — only the FQCN *values* configured under them changed.

**Who breaks.** A host that directly references any of the old FQCNs above (a manual `use`, an
`instanceof` check, `app(OldFqcn::class)`, or a published `config/vaulter-documents.php` copy still
listing `TipTapHandler::class` under its old FQCN) must update to the new FQCN.

### Package renamed: `axioma-studio/vaulter-permissions-azgard` → `axioma-studio/vaulter-azgard` (VLT-CANON P2.1)

**Why.** Final structural canon (Part 3): the AzGuard bridge package's `permissions-` prefix was
the only package in the monorepo naming a *layer* rather than the *thing it bridges* (`vaulter-core`,
`vaulter-drive`, `vaulter-media`, `vaulter-documents`, `vaulter-filament` all name a domain/framework,
not a role). Pre-1.0, so the rename ships without an alias/deprecation phase.

**What moved:**

| Old | New |
|---|---|
| `packages/permissions-azgard/` (dir) | `packages/azgard/` |
| `axioma-studio/vaulter-permissions-azgard` (Composer package) | `axioma-studio/vaulter-azgard` |
| `Vaulter\Permissions\Azgard\*` (namespace) | `Vaulter\Azgard\*` |
| `Vaulter\Permissions\Azgard\VaulterAzgardServiceProvider` | `Vaulter\Azgard\VaulterAzgardServiceProvider` |
| `Vaulter\Permissions\Azgard\AzgardGuardAdapter` | `Vaulter\Azgard\AzgardGuardAdapter` |
| `Vaulter\Permissions\Azgard\NullAzgardGuard` | `Vaulter\Azgard\NullAzgardGuard` |
| `Vaulter\Permissions\Azgard\Contracts\AzgardBackedUser` | `Vaulter\Azgard\Contracts\AzgardBackedUser` |

**Not renamed — the core-owned contract seam is untouched:** `Vaulter\Contracts\Access\AzgardGuard`
(the narrow interface `AzgardPermissionResolver` calls through) stays in `vaulter-core` under its
existing FQCN. Only the bridge/adapter package that implements it moved.

**Who breaks — and who does not.** The bridge has **never been published**: this repo carries no
tag, and the split-to-Packagist pipeline (`monorepo-split.yml`) is tag-triggered, so
`axioma-studio/vaulter-permissions-azgard` never existed on Packagist. **A host installing from
Packagist therefore has nothing to migrate — it only ever sees `axioma-studio/vaulter-azgard`.**

Only a host tracking this monorepo's dev branch directly is affected. Such a host must:
- change a `require` of `axioma-studio/vaulter-permissions-azgard` (via a path/VCS repository) to
  `axioma-studio/vaulter-azgard`, and update the repo path if it points at
  `packages/permissions-azgard`;
- update any direct import of `Vaulter\Permissions\Azgard\*` (service-provider registration, the
  `AzgardGuardAdapter`/`NullAzgardGuard`/`AzgardBackedUser` FQCNs) to `Vaulter\Azgard\*`.

The published config file name (`config/vaulter-azgard.php`, tag `vaulter-azgard-config`) and its
contents are unchanged — only the package/namespace names moved.

### `HandlerCapabilities::$richText` now defaults to `false`; the export read path is kind-neutral (VLT-CANON P0.5)

**`richText` default flipped from `true` to `false`.** `Vaulter\ValueObjects\Handlers\
HandlerCapabilities` no longer assumes rich text (TipTap) is the default shape for editable
content — a handler that doesn't explicitly declare `richText: true` is now treated as
non-rich-text (opaque content), not rich-text. **Who breaks:** any THIRD-PARTY `FileHandler`
that relies on omitting `richText` to mean "rich text" — it must now set `richText: true`
explicitly. The two built-in editable handlers are unaffected: `TipTapHandler` now sets
`richText: true` explicitly (it always behaved as rich-text; only the *declaration* changed from
implicit to explicit); `UniverHandler` already set `richText: false` explicitly. A request with
NO bound node (`UpdateDocumentContentRequest::isRichText()`'s no-node fallback) now defaults to
`false` (kind-neutral — no claim without a resolved handler) instead of `true`; this path is only
reachable outside normal route-model-binding and has no known consumer.

**`CreateDocumentNodeData::$format` default changed from the literal `'tiptap'` to
`DocumentType::RichText->value` (`'richtext'`).** `'tiptap'` was never a valid `DocumentType`
value (see the P0.4 value-space note above) — only direct construction of this DTO (bypassing the
HTTP create endpoint, which always supplies `format` explicitly via
`CreateDocumentNodeRequest::versionFormat()`) is affected.

**The export read path (`NodeExportService::contentOf()`) is now kind-neutral**, closing the gap
P0.2/P0.3/P0.4 tracked as open: it used to resolve EVERY node's head through `TipTapContent`
regardless of the node's actual handler, so a non-rich-text node (e.g. a `univer` spreadsheet)
decayed to an EMPTY TipTap document and exported as blank content instead of being refused. It
now resolves the node's handler and branches on `richText`: a rich-text node's head still decays
through `TipTapContent` exactly as before (no change for TipTap documents); a non-rich-text
node's head is now returned as its own `OpaqueDocumentContent` snapshot, which the existing
`HtmlExporter`/`MarkdownExporter` narrowing guard rejects with
`UnsupportedDocumentContentException` instead of silently rendering nothing. **Who breaks:**
any client exporting a non-rich-text (e.g. Univer) document to HTML/Markdown — it now gets a 4xx
`UnsupportedDocumentContentException` instead of a 200 with blank/empty content. This is a
correctness fix, not a new restriction: the blank export was never useful output.

**Frontend:** `HandlerCapabilities` in `@vaulter/vue` now carries `rich_text: boolean` (was
missing from the TS type entirely, even though the backend always serialised it) plus an open
index signature (`[capability: string]: boolean | undefined`) — capabilities are an additive axis
set, and future new flags won't break existing client code that destructures a known subset.

### The format-handler contract is kind-neutral now: `DocumentHandler` → `FileHandler` (VLT-CANON P0.1 / D7)

**Why.** The contract describes ANY file a host might route to a handler — image/pdf preview,
download-only fallback — not only editable documents. "Document" naming was misleading for the
built-in `DownloadOnlyHandler`/`PreviewHandler` fallbacks, which are not editors. VLT-CANON P0.1
renames the core seam to a kind-neutral vocabulary ahead of pluggable non-editor handlers. This is
a **breaking change to public FQCNs only** — no behaviour, schema, config-key, or route changes.
`config('vaulter.handlers')` / `config('vaulter.exporters')` keys are unaffected.

| Old FQCN | New FQCN |
|:--|:--|
| `Vaulter\Contracts\Documents\DocumentHandler` | `Vaulter\Contracts\Handlers\FileHandler` |
| `Vaulter\Contracts\Documents\HandlerRegistry` | `Vaulter\Contracts\Handlers\HandlerRegistry` |
| `Vaulter\Contracts\Documents\NodeHandlerResolver` | `Vaulter\Contracts\Handlers\NodeHandlerResolver` |
| `Vaulter\Contracts\Documents\DocumentContent` | `Vaulter\Contracts\Handlers\DocumentContent` |
| `Vaulter\Contracts\Documents\DocumentExporter` | `Vaulter\Contracts\Handlers\DocumentExporter` |
| `Vaulter\ValueObjects\Documents\DocumentCapabilities` | `Vaulter\ValueObjects\Handlers\HandlerCapabilities` |
| `Vaulter\ValueObjects\Documents\HandlerDescriptor` | `Vaulter\ValueObjects\Handlers\HandlerDescriptor` |
| `Vaulter\ValueObjects\Documents\HandlerUi` | `Vaulter\ValueObjects\Handlers\HandlerUi` |
| `Vaulter\ValueObjects\Documents\FileDescriptor` | `Vaulter\ValueObjects\Handlers\FileDescriptor` |

`Vaulter\ValueObjects\Documents\{TipTapContent,OpaqueDocumentContent,LinkSchemeValidator}` were
unaffected by P0.1 — they have since moved to `vaulter-documents` in P0.3 (see below). Any
`FileHandler` implementation (host-registered or third-party) must update its `implements`
clause and constructor/method type-hints to the new FQCNs; a class still implementing the old
`Vaulter\Contracts\Documents\DocumentHandler` interface no longer satisfies `HandlerRegistry`.

**`@vaulter/vue`.** The TS type mirroring `DocumentCapabilities` in `types.ts` is renamed to
`HandlerCapabilities`. `HandlerDescriptor`/`HandlerUi` are unchanged.

### `DocumentExporter::export()` now takes the opaque `DocumentContent`, not `TipTapContent` (VLT-CANON P0.2)

**Why.** The exporter contract assumed every document is TipTap; that stopped being kind-neutral
once `DocumentContent` (the same opaque interface the write path already stores) exists. A
non-TipTap handler's content should be exportable through the same seam without the contract
forcing a TipTap shape on it.

**Who breaks.** Callers are fine — a `TipTapContent` *is* a `DocumentContent`. **Implementations
break**: the parameter widened, so an exporter still declaring `export(TipTapContent $content)` is
no longer signature-compatible with the interface and fatals on class load.

```diff
 interface DocumentExporter
 {
-    public function export(TipTapContent $content): string;
+    public function export(DocumentContent $content): string;
 }
```

Any custom `DocumentExporter` (host-registered or third-party) must update its `export()`
parameter type to `Vaulter\Contracts\Handlers\DocumentContent` and narrow internally to the
concrete shape it renders, e.g.:

```php
use Vaulter\Contracts\Handlers\DocumentContent;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Documents\UnsupportedDocumentContentException;
use Vaulter\Documents\ValueObjects\TipTapContent;

public function export(DocumentContent $content): string
{
    if (!$content instanceof TipTapContent) {
        throw UnsupportedDocumentContentException::forExporter(
            $this->format(), TipTapContent::class, $content::class,
        );
    }

    // ... render $content as before
}
```

`UnsupportedDocumentContentException` is new and lives in **core**
(`Vaulter\Exceptions\Documents\`), next to `UnknownExportFormatException` — a core-only host that
registers its own exporter can throw it without requiring `vaulter-documents`. The built-in
`HtmlExporter`/`MarkdownExporter` guard exactly as above.

Note: the package's own node-export path does not yet hand a non-TipTap `DocumentContent` to an
exporter — `NodeExportService::contentOf()` still resolves every version through `TipTapContent`.
The guard is what makes a custom exporter safe today and what makes the seam correct once a
kind-neutral read path lands; it is not yet reachable through `GET .../export`.

`config('vaulter.exporters')` keys and every exporter's `format()`/`mimeType()`/`extension()` are
unaffected.

### TipTap's content shapes moved out of core into `vaulter-documents` (VLT-CANON P0.3)
