<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas;

use InvalidArgumentException;

final readonly class BlobScope
{
    private function __construct(private string $kind, private ?string $id = null, private ?string $legacySegment = null) {}

    public static function global(): self
    {
        return new self('global');
    }

    public static function tenant(string $id): self
    {
        return self::identified('tenant', $id);
    }

    public static function drive(string $id): self
    {
        return self::identified('drive', $id);
    }

    public static function legacy(string $segment): self
    {
        if ($segment === 'global') {
            return new self('global', legacySegment: $segment);
        }

        // Historical releases also emitted ULID segments; preserve their layout.
        if (preg_match('/^([st])(null|\d+|[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26})$/D', $segment, $matches) !== 1) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('Invalid legacy CAS scope segment.');
        }

        return new self($matches[1] === 's' ? 'drive' : 'tenant', $matches[2] === 'null' ? null : $matches[2], $segment);
    }

    public static function forUpload(string $dedupScope, ?string $tenantId, string $storageId): self
    {
        return match ($dedupScope) {
            'global' => self::global(),
            'tenant' => $tenantId === null ? self::global() : self::tenant($tenantId),
            'storage' => self::drive($storageId),
            default => throw new InvalidArgumentException('Unknown CAS dedup scope.'),
        };
    }

    public function key(): string
    {
        if ($this->kind === 'global') {
            return 'global';
        }

        return $this->id === null ? 'legacy:'.$this->legacySegment : $this->kind.':'.$this->id;
    }

    public function segment(): string
    {
        if ($this->legacySegment !== null) {
            return $this->legacySegment;
        }

        if ($this->kind === 'global') {
            return 'g';
        }

        $id = $this->id ?? throw new InvalidArgumentException('Missing CAS scope identifier.');
        $encoded = preg_match('/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/D', $id) === 1
            ? $id : '~'.rtrim(strtr(base64_encode($id), '+/', '-_'), '=');

        return ($this->kind === 'drive' ? 'd-' : 't-').$encoded;
    }

    public static function fromSegment(string $segment): self
    {
        if ($segment === 'g') {
            return self::global();
        }

        if (preg_match('/^([td])-(~?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128})$/D', $segment, $matches) !== 1) {
            return self::legacy($segment);
        }

        $encoded = $matches[2];
        $id = str_starts_with($encoded, '~') ? base64_decode(strtr(substr($encoded, 1), '-_', '+/'), true) : $encoded;
        if ($id === false) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('Invalid encoded CAS scope identifier.');
        }

        $scope = $matches[1] === 'd' ? self::drive($id) : self::tenant($id);
        if ($scope->segment() !== $segment) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('Noncanonical CAS scope segment.');
        }

        return $scope;
    }

    public function isLegacy(): bool
    {
        return $this->legacySegment !== null;
    }

    public function dedupScope(): string
    {
        return $this->kind === 'drive' ? 'storage' : $this->kind;
    }

    public function tenantId(): ?string
    {
        return $this->kind === 'tenant' ? $this->id : null;
    }

    public function storageId(): ?string
    {
        return $this->kind === 'drive' ? $this->id : null;
    }

    private static function identified(string $kind, string $id): self
    {
        // Keep the canonical path within the reconciler's bounded segment grammar.
        if ($id === '' || strlen($id) > 96) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('CAS scope identifiers must contain 1–96 bytes.');
        }

        return new self($kind, $id);
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/core/tests/Arch/PublicApiSnapshotTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Vaulter\Access\AccessResolver;
use Vaulter\Access\AclFolder;
use Vaulter\Access\ExactListing;
use Vaulter\Data\Drive\NodePage;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Contracts\ProblemException;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNotFoundException;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\StorageNotFoundException;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\VaulterException;
use Vaulter\Facades\Vaulter;
use Vaulter\Http\Support\HttpResourceResolver;
use Vaulter\Schema\HostKeyColumns;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\Support\VaulterSchema;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * VLT-REL P2.2 — executable freeze of the public composer surface for the `0.1.0`
 * tag. The human-readable SSOT is `plans/archive/VLT-REL/root/contracts/public-api-surface.md`
 * (P2.1); THIS test is its enforceable counterpart: it reflects the LIVE signatures of
 * every `Vaulter\Contracts\**` interface plus the `Vaulter\Facades\Vaulter` facade,
 * canonicalises them into a stable manifest, and compares against a committed golden.
 * Any unplanned change to the surface (a removed/renamed method, a narrowed parameter
 * type, a new contract, a dropped facade `@method`) reddens the build.
 *
 * WHY reflection, not the doc: the doc can drift silently; this test can't — the golden
 * is generated from real signatures, so a `public-api.md:19` breaking change (removal /
 * rename / type narrowing) is caught mechanically, not by review vigilance.
 *
 * Canonicalisation (D5 — signatures only; config/resources/bindings live in the doc):
 *   • Contracts are DISCOVERED by scanning `src/Contracts/**` — so a NEW contract is
 *     frozen automatically (its absence from the golden reddens the build), not just the
 *     ones enumerated today.
 *   • Full signature: parameter name + type (union/intersection/nullable normalised),
 *     by-ref, variadic, default value; return type. Named-argument discipline
 *     (`php:named-arguments`) makes parameter NAMES part of the contract, so they are
 *     frozen too.
 *   • `@internal`-marked interfaces/methods are EXCLUDED (outside the BC promise,
 *     `public-api.md:22`).
 *   • Deterministic order: contracts sorted by FQCN, methods sorted by name, union/
 *     intersection members sorted — so a harmless reorder never reddens the build while
 *     a real signature diff reads cleanly.
 *   • The facade is frozen by its `@method` docblock (it stands over the resolved
 *     `VaulterManager`, Laravel Manager idiom — SSOT §2), not by reflecting the thin
 *     Facade class (whose only real method is the protected accessor).
 *
 * AMEND (adding surface legally after the tag): regenerate the golden with
 * `UPDATE_SNAPSHOT=1`, add a new `D#`, sync `plan.md` §7 Contracts, and ship a CHANGELOG
 * MINOR — see `plans/archive/VLT-REL/root/contracts/AMEND.md`. Regenerating the golden OUTSIDE
 * that procedure (no `D#`) is a `[POSSIBLE-DEFECT]` per `.claude/rules/tests.md`.
 * `UPDATE_SNAPSHOT` is env-gated and is NEVER set by any CI workflow.
 */
if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotType')) {
    /**
     * Normalise a reflection type to a canonical, deterministic string. Union and
     * intersection members are sorted so a source-level reorder (not a BC change) does
     * not redden the build. Non-builtin types carry a leading `\` for an unambiguous FQCN.
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotType(?ReflectionType $type): string
    {
        if ($type === null) {
            return '';
        }

        if ($type instanceof ReflectionUnionType) {
            $parts = array_map('vaulterSnapshotType', $type->getTypes());
            sort($parts);

            return implode('|', $parts);
        }

        if ($type instanceof ReflectionIntersectionType) {
            $parts = array_map('vaulterSnapshotType', $type->getTypes());
            sort($parts);

            return implode('&', $parts);
        }

        /** @var ReflectionNamedType $type */
        $name = $type->getName();
        $lower = strtolower($name);
        $prefix = ($type->allowsNull() && $lower !== 'null' && $lower !== 'mixed') ? '?' : '';

        return $prefix.($type->isBuiltin() ? '' : '\\').$name;
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotValue')) {
    /**
     * Compact, deterministic rendering of a parameter default value (scalars, null,
     * shallow arrays — the only shapes contract defaults take).
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotValue(mixed $value): string
    {
        if ($value === null) {
            return 'null';
        }

        if (is_bool($value)) {
            return $value ? 'true' : 'false';
        }

        if (is_string($value)) {
            return "'".$value."'";
        }

        if (is_array($value)) {
            return '['.implode(', ', array_map('vaulterSnapshotValue', $value)).']';
        }

        if (is_int($value) || is_float($value)) {
            return (string) $value;
        }

        return get_debug_type($value);
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotMethod')) {
    /** Serialise one public method to `name(type $p = default, …): return`. */
    function vaulterSnapshotMethod(ReflectionMethod $method): string
    {
        $params = [];

        foreach ($method->getParameters() as $param) {
            $piece = vaulterSnapshotType($param->getType());
            $piece .= ' ';
            $piece .= $param->isPassedByReference() ? '&' : '';
            $piece .= $param->isVariadic() ? '...' : '';
            $piece .= '$'.$param->getName();

            if ($param->isDefaultValueAvailable()) {
                $piece .= ' = '.($param->isDefaultValueConstant()
                    ? (string) $param->getDefaultValueConstantName()
                    : vaulterSnapshotValue($param->getDefaultValue()));
            }

            $params[] = trim($piece);
        }

        $return = vaulterSnapshotType($method->getReturnType());

        return $method->getName().'('.implode(', ', $params).')'.($return !== '' ? ': '.$return : '');
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotInterface')) {
    /**
     * Serialise one contract: FQCN header + its public, non-`@internal` methods (sorted).
     *
     * @param class-string $fqcn
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotInterface(string $fqcn): string
    {
        $reflection = new ReflectionClass($fqcn);
        $lines = [];

        foreach ($reflection->getMethods(ReflectionMethod::IS_PUBLIC) as $method) {
            $doc = $method->getDocComment();

            if ($doc !== false && str_contains($doc, '@internal')) {
                continue;
            }

            $lines[] = '  '.vaulterSnapshotMethod($method);
        }

        sort($lines);

        return $fqcn."\n".implode("\n", $lines);
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotContracts')) {
    /**
     * Discover every `*\Contracts\**` interface under a package src tree and map each
     * file to its FQCN (PSR-4: basename === declared type). `@internal`-marked types are
     * dropped from the frozen set.
     *
     * @return list<class-string> sorted FQCNs
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotContracts(string $contractsDir, string $namespaceRoot): array
    {
        if (!is_dir($contractsDir)) {
            return [];
        }

        /** @var Iterator<SplFileInfo> $files */
        $files = new RegexIterator(
            new RecursiveIteratorIterator(new RecursiveDirectoryIterator($contractsDir, FilesystemIterator::SKIP_DOTS)),
            '/\.php$/',
        );

        $fqcns = [];

        foreach ($files as $file) {
            $relative = substr(str_replace($contractsDir.'/', '', $file->getPathname()), 0, -4);
            $fqcn = $namespaceRoot.'\\'.str_replace('/', '\\', $relative);

            // Per-branch interface_exists()/class_exists() narrow $fqcn to class-string
            // (an OR of the two does not), so ReflectionClass type-checks without a
            // suppression. Contracts are interfaces by invariant (arch 'contracts are
            // interfaces'); the class branch keeps discovery honest if that ever slips.
            if (interface_exists($fqcn)) {
                $reflection = new ReflectionClass($fqcn);
            } elseif (class_exists($fqcn)) {
                $reflection = new ReflectionClass($fqcn);
            } else {
                continue;
            }

            $doc = $reflection->getDocComment();

            if ($doc !== false && str_contains($doc, '@internal')) {
                continue;
            }

            $fqcns[] = $fqcn;
        }

        sort($fqcns);

        return array_values(array_unique($fqcns));
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotFacade')) {
    /** Freeze the facade by its `@method` docblock (surface over the resolved manager). */
    function vaulterSnapshotFacade(string $facadeFqcn): string
    {
        $doc = (new ReflectionClass($facadeFqcn))->getDocComment() ?: '';
        preg_match_all('/@method\s+(.+)$/m', $doc, $matches);

        $methods = array_map(
            static fn (string $line): string => trim((string) preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', $line)),
            $matches[1],
        );

        sort($methods);

        return 'FACADE '.$facadeFqcn."\n".implode("\n", array_map(
            static fn (string $m): string => '  '.$m,
            $methods,
        ));
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotAssert')) {
    /**
     * Shared gate body: compare the freshly reflected manifest against the committed
     * golden, or regenerate it under the env-gated `UPDATE_SNAPSHOT` escape hatch.
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotAssert(string $manifest, string $goldenPath): void
    {
        if (getenv('UPDATE_SNAPSHOT') === '1') {
            @mkdir(dirname($goldenPath), 0o755, true);
            file_put_contents($goldenPath, $manifest);
            expect(is_file($goldenPath))->toBeTrue();

            return;
        }

        expect(is_file($goldenPath))->toBeTrue();

        expect($manifest)->toBe(
            file_get_contents($goldenPath),
            'Public API surface changed. If this is INTENTIONAL and ADDITIVE, follow AMEND '
            .'(plans/archive/VLT-REL/root/contracts/AMEND.md): regenerate the golden with UPDATE_SNAPSHOT=1, '
            .'add a D#, sync plan.md §7 Contracts, ship a CHANGELOG MINOR. If it is BREAKING '
            .'(removed/renamed method, narrowed type), that is a SemVer decision — escalate to the owner.',
        );
    }
}

test('vaulter-core public API surface matches the frozen golden (VLT-REL P2.2)', function (): void {
    $contracts = vaulterSnapshotContracts(__DIR__.'/../../src/Contracts', 'Vaulter\Contracts');

    // Discovery must find the enumerated surface — a scan that silently finds nothing
    // would make the whole freeze an inert no-op.
    expect($contracts)->not->toBe([]);

    $blocks = array_map('vaulterSnapshotInterface', $contracts);
    foreach ([AccessResolver::class, AclFolder::class, ExactListing::class, NodePage::class] as $class) {
        $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface($class);
    }
    $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface(VaulterDatabase::class);
    $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface(BlobScope::class);
    $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface(Config::class);
    $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface(HostKeyColumns::class);
    $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface(VaulterSchema::class);
    $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface(HttpResourceResolver::class);
    $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface(ProblemException::class);
    foreach ([NodeNotFoundException::class => 'forId', StorageNotFoundException::class => 'forId', VaulterException::class => 'render'] as $class => $method) {
        $blocks[] = $class."\n  ".vaulterSnapshotMethod(new ReflectionMethod($class, $method));
    }
    $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotFacade(Vaulter::class);

    foreach (vaulterSnapshotContracts(__DIR__.'/../../src/Events', 'Vaulter\Events') as $eventClass) {
        $reflection = new ReflectionClass($eventClass);
        $properties = [];
        foreach ($reflection->getProperties(ReflectionProperty::IS_PUBLIC) as $property) {
            $properties[] = '  '.($property->isReadOnly() ? 'readonly ' : '')
                .vaulterSnapshotType($property->getType()).' $'.$property->getName();
        }
        sort($properties);
        $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface($eventClass)."\n".implode("\n", $properties);
    }

    vaulterSnapshotAssert(implode("\n\n", $blocks)."\n", __DIR__.'/__snapshots__/public-api.txt');
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/core/tests/Schema/BlobDedupIndexTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Vaulter\Panels\Panel;
use Vaulter\Schema\PanelTablesBuilder;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;

/** Every configured scope uses the same NULL-free scope_key index. */
function dedupIndexColumns(): array
{
    $table = Config::table('blobs');
    $name = "{$table}_scope_sha_uniq";

    /** @var list<object{name: string}> $columns */
    $columns = DB::select("PRAGMA index_info('{$name}')");

    return array_map(static fn (object $column): string => $column->name, $columns);
}

function rebuildBlobsTable(): void
{
    $builder = new PanelTablesBuilder;
    $panel = (new Panel)->id('default');

    $builder->dropTable(panel: $panel, entity: 'blobs');
    $builder->createTable(panel: $panel, entity: 'blobs');
}

function storageKeyIndexColumns(): array
{
    $table = Config::table('blobs');
    $name = "{$table}_storage_key_uniq";

    /** @var list<object{name: string}> $columns */
    $columns = DB::select("PRAGMA index_info('{$name}')");

    return array_map(static fn (object $column): string => $column->name, $columns);
}

it('global scope → UNIQUE (scope_key, sha256)', function (): void {
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'global');
    rebuildBlobsTable();

    expect(dedupIndexColumns())->toBe(['scope_key', 'sha256']);
})->skip(
    fn (): bool => DB::connection()->getDriverName() !== 'sqlite',
    'PRAGMA index introspection + dropTable(no CASCADE) are SQLite-only; the dedup UNIQUE is exercised live on PG by the Cas suite.',
);

it('tenant scope → UNIQUE (scope_key, sha256)', function (): void {
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
    rebuildBlobsTable();

    expect(dedupIndexColumns())->toBe(['scope_key', 'sha256']);
})->skip(
    fn (): bool => DB::connection()->getDriverName() !== 'sqlite',
    'PRAGMA index introspection + dropTable(no CASCADE) are SQLite-only; the dedup UNIQUE is exercised live on PG by the Cas suite.',
);

it('storage scope → UNIQUE (scope_key, sha256)', function (): void {
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'storage');
    rebuildBlobsTable();

    expect(dedupIndexColumns())->toBe(['scope_key', 'sha256']);
})->skip(
    fn (): bool => DB::connection()->getDriverName() !== 'sqlite',
    'PRAGMA index introspection + dropTable(no CASCADE) are SQLite-only; the dedup UNIQUE is exercised live on PG by the Cas suite.',
);

// The physical object address remains independently unique across disks.
it('storage_key_uniq stays single-column (the F-P1.5-03 NULL-tenant dedup backbone)', function (): void {
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
    rebuildBlobsTable();

    expect(storageKeyIndexColumns())->toBe(['storage_key']);
})->skip(
    fn (): bool => DB::connection()->getDriverName() !== 'sqlite',
    'PRAGMA index introspection + dropTable(no CASCADE) are SQLite-only; the storage_key UNIQUE is exercised live on PG by the Cas suite.',
);

# COMPLETE FILE packages/core/tests/Schema/BlobDedupScopeTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Database\QueryException;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;

/**
 * Blob dedup-uniqueness is scoped (D18 / H.3): the same sha256 is unique only
 * within (tenant_id, storage_id), so identical bytes in two scopes are two rows
 * and storage_key is scope-qualified so they never share one physical object.
 */
function makeBlob(array $overrides = []): Blob
{
    // Blob is fully guarded (P1.4/F-P1.5-04) — forceFill bypasses it for this
    // trusted test fixture builder, exactly like the production writer
    // (BlobStoreRepository) does.
    $blob = new Blob;
    $blob->forceFill([
        'sha256' => str_repeat('a', 64),
        'size_bytes' => 10,
        'scope_key' => isset($overrides['tenant_id']) ? 'tenant:'.$overrides['tenant_id'] : 'global',
        'storage_key' => 'global/aa/aa/'.str_repeat('a', 64),
        ...$overrides,
    ]);
    $blob->save();

    return $blob;
}

it('отвергает одинаковый sha256 в одном scope', function (): void {
    makeBlob(['tenant_id' => 1, 'storage_id' => 1, 'storage_key' => 't1/aa/aa/x']);

    // Run the violating insert inside a SAVEPOINT (nested DB::transaction): on PostgreSQL
    // a UNIQUE violation aborts the WHOLE enclosing transaction — here the RefreshDatabase
    // wrapper — so a raw insert would poison the connection for the rest of the suite
    // ("current transaction is aborted"). The savepoint rolls back just the failed insert,
    // exactly as the production BlobStore commit does. SQLite tolerates either form.
    expect(fn () => DB::transaction(
        fn () => makeBlob(['tenant_id' => 1, 'storage_id' => 1, 'storage_key' => 't1/aa/aa/y']),
    ))->toThrow(QueryException::class);
});

it('допускает одинаковый sha256 в разных scope', function (): void {
    $a = makeBlob(['tenant_id' => 1, 'storage_id' => null, 'storage_key' => 't1/aa/aa/a']);
    $b = makeBlob(['tenant_id' => 2, 'storage_id' => null, 'storage_key' => 't2/aa/aa/b']);

    expect($a->exists)->toBeTrue()
        ->and($b->exists)->toBeTrue()
        ->and($a->sha256)->toBe($b->sha256)
        ->and($a->storage_key)->not->toBe($b->storage_key);
});

it('хранит state-колонки блоба для CAS-логики M1b', function (): void {
    // Read back from the DB so the schema-level column defaults are asserted.
    $blob = makeBlob(['tenant_id' => 9])->fresh();

    expect($blob->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Pending)
        ->and($blob->ref_count)->toBe(0)
        ->and($blob->dedup_scope)->toBe('tenant')
        ->and($blob->delete_after_at)->toBeNull();
});

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/core/tests/Schema/FileVersionBlobTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Database\QueryException;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Vaulter\Enums\Drive\NodeKind;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\File;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersion;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Storage;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileVersionBlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Tests\Schema\ForceCreates;

/**
 * The file ↔ version ↔ blob chain and the v_file_version_blobs edge (the GC
 * truth, §2.4): relations resolve through the swappable model map.
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    $storage = ForceCreates::make(Storage::class, [
        'owner_type' => 'team', 'owner_id' => 1, 'name' => 'Disk',
    ]);

    $node = ForceCreates::make(Node::class, [
        'storage_id' => $storage->getKey(), 'kind' => NodeKind::File, 'name' => 'a.pdf',
    ]);

    $this->file = ForceCreates::make(File::class, [
        'node_id' => $node->getKey(),
        'mime_type' => 'application/pdf',
        'size_bytes' => 100,
    ]);

    $this->blob = ForceCreates::make(Blob::class, [
        'scope_key' => 'global',
        'sha256' => str_repeat('b', 64),
        'size_bytes' => 100,
        'storage_key' => 'global/bb/bb/'.str_repeat('b', 64),
        'status' => 'committed',
    ]);

    $this->version = ForceCreates::make(FileVersion::class, [
        'file_id' => $this->file->getKey(),
        'version_no' => 1,
        'blob_id' => $this->blob->getKey(),
        'content_sha256' => str_repeat('b', 64),
        'size_bytes' => 100,
        'original_name' => 'a.pdf',
        'status' => 'committed',
    ]);

    $this->file->forceFill(['current_version_id' => $this->version->getKey()])->save();
});

it('file -> versions -> blob связаны', function (): void {
    expect($this->file->versions()->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and($this->file->currentVersion->getKey())->toBe($this->version->getKey())
        ->and($this->version->blob->getKey())->toBe($this->blob->getKey());
});

it('ребро version<->blob — это GC-истина через pivot', function (): void {
    // FileVersionBlob is fully guarded (P1.4/F-P1.8-02) — the production write
    // path is the query-builder repository, not Eloquent's own relation
    // attach() (which routes pivot attributes through fill()/mass-assignment
    // and would now throw MassAssignmentException).
    app(FileVersionBlobStoreRepository::class)->attach($this->version, $this->blob, [
        'role' => 'data',
        'ordinal' => 0,
    ]);

    expect($this->version->blobs()->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and($this->blob->versions()->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and($this->version->blobs()->first()->pivot->role)->toBe('data');
});

it('version_no монотонно уникален на файл', function (): void {
    // SAVEPOINT the violating insert: on PostgreSQL a UNIQUE violation aborts the whole
    // enclosing RefreshDatabase transaction, poisoning the connection for the rest of the
    // suite; the nested DB::transaction rolls back only the failed insert. SQLite tolerates
    // either form (see BlobDedupScopeTest for the reference rationale).
    expect(fn () => DB::transaction(fn () => ForceCreates::make(FileVersion::class, [
        'file_id' => $this->file->getKey(),
        'version_no' => 1,
        'content_sha256' => str_repeat('c', 64),
        'size_bytes' => 1,
        'original_name' => 'a.pdf',
    ])))->toThrow(QueryException::class);
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/core/tests/Unit/BlobScopeTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

it('round trips lossless CAS scope identifiers', function (string $id): void {
    foreach ([BlobScope::drive($id), BlobScope::tenant($id)] as $scope) {
        expect(BlobScope::fromSegment($scope->segment())->key())->toBe($scope->key());
    }
})->with(['001', '01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV', '019a0000-1234-7000-8000-123456789abc', 'host/project', 'équipe']);

it('retains legacy paths while sharing their numeric scope keys', function (string $segment, string $key): void {
    $scope = BlobScope::fromSegment($segment);
    expect($scope->key())->toBe($key)->and($scope->segment())->toBe($segment);
})->with([
    ['global', 'global'], ['s001', 'drive:001'], ['t12', 'tenant:12'],
    ['snull', 'legacy:snull'], ['tnull', 'legacy:tnull'],
]);

it('selects the host scope without coercing identifiers', function (): void {
    expect(BlobScope::forUpload('tenant', null, 'drive')->key())->toBe('global')
        ->and(BlobScope::forUpload('storage', null, 'drive')->key())->toBe('drive:drive')
        ->and(BlobScope::forUpload('tenant', '001', 'drive')->key())->toBe('tenant:001')
        ->and(BlobScope::forUpload('global', 'tenant', 'drive')->segment())->toBe('g');
});

it('rejects invalid and noncanonical scope segments', function (string $segment): void {
    expect(fn () => BlobScope::fromSegment($segment))->toThrow(InvalidArgumentException::class);
})->with(['', '../g', 'd-', 'd-~', 'd-~YQ', 't-~bad=', 'unknown']);

# COMPLETE FILE packages/core/tests/Upgrade/BlobScopeKeyMigrationTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabaseState;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\Tests\Upgrade\LoadsV02Fixture;

uses(LoadsV02Fixture::class);

afterEach(function (): void {
    RefreshDatabaseState::$migrated = false;
});

it('backfills historical blob scopes without moving their objects', function (string $strategy): void {
    if (app(VaulterDatabase::class)->driver() !== 'pgsql') {
        $this->markTestSkipped('Historical fixtures require PostgreSQL.');
    }
    $this->loadV02Fixture($strategy);
    $db = app(VaulterDatabase::class)->connection();
    $table = Config::table('blobs');
    $before = $db->table($table)->orderBy('id')->get()->keyBy('id');
    $this->artisan('migrate', ['--force' => true])->assertSuccessful();
    foreach ($db->table($table)->get() as $blob) {
        $original = $before[$blob->id];
        $expected = match ($original->dedup_scope) {
            'storage' => $original->storage_id === null ? 'legacy:snull' : 'drive:'.$original->storage_id,
            'tenant' => $original->tenant_id === null ? 'legacy:tnull' : 'tenant:'.$original->tenant_id,
            default => 'global',
        };
        expect($blob->scope_key)->toBe($expected)->and($blob->storage_key)->toBe($original->storage_key);
    }
    $schema = $db->getSchemaBuilder();
    expect($schema->hasIndex($table, $table.'_scope_sha_uniq'))->toBeTrue()
        ->and($schema->hasIndex($table, $table.'_dedup_uniq'))->toBeFalse();
    $this->artisan('migrate', ['--force' => true])->assertSuccessful();
    $migration = require __DIR__.'/../../database/migrations/2026_10_01_000007_blob_scope_key.php';
    $migration->up();
    expect($db->table($table)->whereNull('scope_key')->count())->toBe(0);
    $migration->down();
    expect($schema->hasColumn($table, 'scope_key'))->toBeFalse()
        ->and($schema->hasIndex($table, $table.'_dedup_uniq'))->toBeTrue();
    $migration->up();
})->with(['bigint', 'ulid']);

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/src/Actions/UploadFileAction.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Actions;

use LogicException;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Data\UploadFileData;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\FileUploaded;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeVersionCommitted;
use Vaulter\Drive\Exceptions\CrossStorageMoveException;
use Vaulter\Drive\Exceptions\QuotaExceededException;
use Vaulter\Enums\Drive\NodeKind;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNameConflictException;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\File;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersion;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileVersionStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeReadRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\StorageStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\Support\LoadTier;
use Vaulter\Support\NodeName;
use Vaulter\Support\TreeGuard;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Commit an upload into the Drive (§2.4 commit flow): stage → BlobStore.commit
 * (content-addressed dedup) → File + FileVersion(version_no) + version↔blob edge
 * → file node (current_version_id, mime, size, extension).
 *
 * Quota (§1.5 eager): the incoming size is checked against the storage's
 * quota_bytes BEFORE the blob is committed (we never store bytes we must reject),
 * and used_bytes is incremented WITHIN the transaction.
 *
 * Two paths share one Action:
 *   - new file → create Node(kind=file) + File + FileVersion(version_no=1);
 *   - new version (upload onto an existing file node) → bump version_no, repoint
 *     current_version_id, mark the prior head superseded, and adjust used_bytes by
 *     the SIZE DELTA (new size − previous head size), not the full size.
 *
 * Authorization (M2a / H.2) is enforced upstream at the HTTP controller
 * boundary (NodeController + AccessResolver); this Action stays pure.
 */
final readonly class UploadFileAction
{
    public function __construct(
        private BlobStore $blobs,
        private FileStoreRepository $files,
        private FileVersionStoreRepository $versions,
        private NodeStoreRepository $nodeStore,
        private NodeReadRepository $nodeRead,
        private StorageStoreRepository $storageStore,
        private MetricsRecorder $metrics,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
        private TreeGuard $tree,
    ) {}

    public function execute(UploadFileData $data): Node
    {
        return $this->metrics->timing(
            name: 'vaulter_upload_chunk_duration_seconds',
            operation: fn (): Node => $this->executeUpload($data),
            labels: [
                'protocol' => 'single',
                'tier' => LoadTier::label(),
            ],
        );
    }

    private function executeUpload(UploadFileData $data): Node
    {
        $probedSize = $this->probeSize($data->stream);
        $previousSize = $this->previousHeadSize($data);

        // Eager quota — the delta a new version adds is (new − old); a brand-new
        // file adds its full size. Checked BEFORE we commit any bytes (§1.5). The
        // probe is best-effort for non-seekable streams; the real used_bytes
        // adjustment below uses the committed blob's authoritative size.
        $this->assertWithinQuota(data: $data, delta: $probedSize - $previousSize);

        // A new file gets a normalized name under a resolved folder (the root when no
        // destination is given); a new version keeps the node's place and name.
        $name = $data->isNewVersion() ? null : NodeName::normalize($data->resolvedName());
        $parentId = $data->isNewVersion() ? null : $this->tree->destinationId(storage: $data->storage, into: $data->into);

        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($data, $previousSize, $name, $parentId): Node {
            $this->database->lockTreeShared($data->storage->id);

            if ($name !== null && $parentId !== null) {
                $this->assertDestination(data: $data, parentId: $parentId, name: $name);
            }

            // A new version locks its target before the CAS lock, as every writer takes its
            // node row before CAS, and measures the delta from the locked row; a target
            // deleted meanwhile is not found rather than given new blob edges.
            $target = $data->targetNode;

            if ($target instanceof Node) {
                $this->tree->sync($target, $this->tree->locked($this->tree->lock([$target->id]), $target->id));
                $previousSize = $target->size_bytes;
            }

            $storageId = $data->storage->getKey();
            if (!is_int($storageId) && !is_string($storageId)) {
                throw new LogicException('An upload requires a persisted storage identifier.');
            }

            $blob = $this->blobs->commit(
                stream: $data->stream,
                scope: BlobScope::forUpload(
                    Config::dedupScope(),
                    $data->storage->tenant_id === null ? null : (string) $data->storage->tenant_id,
                    (string) $storageId,
                ),
                disk: $data->storage->disk,
            );

            $size = $blob->size_bytes;

            $this->metrics->counter(
                name: 'vaulter_upload_bytes_total',
                amount: $size,
                labels: ['protocol' => 'single'],
            );

            $node = $name === null || $parentId === null
                ? $this->commitNewVersion(data: $data, blob: $blob, size: $size)
                : $this->commitNewFile(data: $data, blob: $blob, size: $size, name: $name, parentId: $parentId);

            $delta = $size - $previousSize;

            if ($delta !== 0) {
                $this->storageStore->adjustUsedBytes(storage: $data->storage, delta: $delta);
            }

            return $node;
        });
    }

    /**
     * New-file path (§2.4): node(kind=file) → file → version_no=1 → edge → repoint.
     */
    private function commitNewFile(UploadFileData $data, Blob $blob, int $size, string $name, int|string $parentId): Node
    {
        $node = $this->nodeStore->create([
            'storage_id' => $data->storage->id,
            'parent_id' => $parentId,
            'kind' => NodeKind::File,
            'name' => $name,
            'mime_type' => $data->mimeType,
            'extension' => $this->extension($data->originalName),
            'size_bytes' => $size,
            'owner_id' => $data->actorId,
            'created_by' => $data->actorId,
        ]);

        $file = $this->files->create([
            'node_id' => $node->id,
            'mime_type' => $data->mimeType,
            'size_bytes' => $size,
            'version_count' => 0,
        ]);

        $version = $this->makeVersion(data: $data, file: $file, blob: $blob, size: $size, versionNo: 1);

        $this->blobs->attach($version, $blob);

        $this->files->repointHead(
            file: $file,
            currentVersionId: $version->id,
            mimeType: $data->mimeType,
            sizeBytes: $size,
        );

        $this->nodeStore->update($node, [
            'file_id' => $file->id,
            'current_version_id' => $version->id,
        ]);

        event(new FileUploaded(node: $node->refresh(), file: $file, version: $version, actorId: $data->actorId));

        return $node;
    }

    /**
     * New-version path (§2.4): bump version_no, new version + edge against the
     * (deduped) blob, mark the prior head superseded, repoint file + node heads.
     */
    private function commitNewVersion(UploadFileData $data, Blob $blob, int $size): Node
    {
        $node = $data->targetNode;

        if (!$node instanceof Node) {
            // Defensive: isNewVersion() already guaranteed this; keeps PHPStan honest.
            throw new LogicException('[Vaulter] A new-version upload requires a target node.');
        }

        /** @var File $file */
        $file = $node->file()->firstOrFail();

        $previousHead = $file->currentVersion()->first();

        $version = $this->makeVersion(
            data: $data,
            file: $file,
            blob: $blob,
            size: $size,
            versionNo: $this->versions->nextVersionNo($file),
        );

        $this->blobs->attach($version, $blob);

        if ($previousHead instanceof FileVersion) {
            $this->versions->markSuperseded($previousHead);
        }

        $this->files->repointHead(
            file: $file,
            currentVersionId: $version->id,
            mimeType: $data->mimeType ?? $file->mime_type,
            sizeBytes: $size,
        );

        $this->nodeStore->repointHead(
            node: $node,
            currentVersionId: $version->id,
            mimeType: $data->mimeType ?? $node->mime_type,
            extension: $node->extension,
            sizeBytes: $size,
        );

        event(new NodeVersionCommitted(
            node: $node->refresh(),
            file: $file,
            version: $version,
            versionNo: $version->version_no,
            actorId: $data->actorId,
        ));

        return $node;
    }

    private function makeVersion(
        UploadFileData $data,
        File $file,
        Blob $blob,
        int $size,
        int $versionNo,
    ): FileVersion {
        return $this->versions->create([
            'file_id' => $file->getKey(),
            'version_no' => $versionNo,
            'blob_id' => $blob->getKey(),
            'content_sha256' => $blob->sha256,
            'size_bytes' => $size,
            'mime_type' => $data->mimeType,
            'original_name' => $data->originalName,
            'status' => 'committed',
            'created_by' => $data->actorId,
            'committed_at' => now(),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Reject the upload BEFORE committing bytes when it would push used_bytes past
     * quota_bytes (§1.5). A null quota is unlimited; a non-positive delta (a
     * smaller new version) never trips the check.
     */
    private function assertWithinQuota(UploadFileData $data, int $delta): void
    {
        $quota = $data->storage->quota_bytes;

        if ($quota === null || $delta <= 0) {
            return;
        }

        if ($data->storage->used_bytes + $delta > $quota) {
            throw QuotaExceededException::forStorage(
                storageId: $data->storage->id,
                quotaBytes: $quota,
                usedBytes: $data->storage->used_bytes,
                incomingBytes: $delta,
            );
        }
    }

    /**
     * The destination folder is locked before the bytes are committed, so it cannot
     * be trashed or replaced by a file between the check and the insert.
     */
    private function assertDestination(UploadFileData $data, int|string $parentId, string $name): void
    {
        $parent = $this->tree->locked($this->tree->lock([$parentId]), $parentId);
        if (!$this->tree->sameStorage($parent, $data->storage->id)) {
            throw CrossStorageMoveException::between(fromStorageId: $data->storage->id, toStorageId: $parent->storage_id);
        }

        $this->tree->assertActiveFolder($parent);

        if ($this->nodeRead->siblingNameExists(storage: $data->storage, parentId: $parentId, name: $name)) {
            throw NodeNameConflictException::forName(name: $name, parentId: $parentId);
        }
    }

    private function previousHeadSize(UploadFileData $data): int
    {
        $target = $data->targetNode;

        return $target instanceof Node ? $target->size_bytes : 0;
    }

    /**
     * Probe the staged stream's byte length without consuming it (the ContentStore
     * re-reads it from the start). Seekable streams report fstat size; a
     * non-seekable stream falls back to 0 here and the committed blob's size is the
     * truth (used for accounting after commit).
     */
    private function probeSize(mixed $stream): int
    {
        if (!is_resource($stream)) {
            return 0;
        }

        $stats = fstat($stream);

        return is_array($stats) ? (int) $stats['size'] : 0;
    }

    private function extension(string $name): ?string
    {
        $ext = pathinfo($name, PATHINFO_EXTENSION);

        return $ext === '' ? null : mb_strtolower($ext);
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/src/Cas/BlobGarbageCollector.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Cas;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Collection;
use Illuminate\Database\QueryException;
use Throwable;
use Vaulter\Cas\BlobLock;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Cas\BlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileVersionBlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;

/**
 * Two-phase, race-safe blob GC (H.3). It is ACL-blind — liveness ≠ the viewer's
 * rights, so a node a user cannot see still pins its blob; GC runs as the system
 * with no panel/workspace context.
 *
 *  PHASE 1 (mark): scan committed ∧ ref_count(cache)=0 (reconciliation rebuilds
 *  that cache from the edge table, so a blob qualifies once its last edge is
 *  detached and reconciled), then under the per-(scope,sha256) lock RE-VERIFY no
 *  live edge exists in v_file_version_blobs before tombstoning (grace deadline =
 *  now + grace, armed here so the sweep cannot delete within the grace window).
 *
 *  PHASE 2 (sweep): take tombstoned blobs past grace, RE-VERIFY under the lock
 *  (a resurrection may have flipped them back to committed), → deleting →
 *  physical delete (scope-qualified key, no cross-scope deletion) → deleted
 *  (idempotent tombstone). On PostgreSQL the sweep runs SERIALIZABLE with
 *  retry-on-40001.
 *
 * The advisory lock + UNIQUE (scope, sha256) + resurrection are what make this
 * safe against a concurrent upload-commit: whichever transaction wins the lock
 * acts on rows the other will then re-read; a commit resurrects before a sweep
 * can delete, a sweep that wins re-checks edges before deleting.
 */
final readonly class BlobGarbageCollector
{
    private const int BATCH_SIZE = 1000;

    private const int MAX_SERIALIZATION_RETRIES = 5;

    public function __construct(
        private ContentStore $content,
        private BlobLock $lock,
        private BlobStoreRepository $blobs,
        private FileVersionBlobStoreRepository $edges,
        private MetricsRecorder $metrics,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    /**
     * Run both phases and return a per-phase tally.
     *
     * @return array{tombstoned: int, swept: int, skipped: int}
     */
    public function collect(int $graceHours = 0): array
    {
        $grace = Config::gcGraceHours($graceHours);

        $mark = $this->mark(graceHours: $grace);
        $sweep = $this->sweep();

        return [
            'tombstoned' => $mark['tombstoned'],
            'swept' => $sweep['swept'],
            'skipped' => $mark['skipped'] + $sweep['skipped'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * PHASE 1 — tombstone committed blobs that are genuinely unreferenced.
     *
     * @return array{tombstoned: int, skipped: int}
     */
    public function mark(int $graceHours): array
    {
        $tombstoned = 0;
        $skipped = 0;

        $this->metrics->timing(
            name: 'vaulter_gc_duration_seconds',
            operation: function () use ($graceHours, &$tombstoned, &$skipped): void {
                foreach ($this->candidateBatches(static fn (Builder $q): Builder => $q->garbageCollectable()) as $batch) {
                    $marked = $this->markBatch($batch, $graceHours);
                    $tombstoned += $marked;
                    $skipped += $batch->count() - $marked;

                    $this->metrics->histogram(name: 'vaulter_gc_batch_rows', value: $batch->count());
                    $this->metrics->counter(name: 'vaulter_gc_rows_scanned_total', amount: $batch->count());
                }
            },
            labels: ['phase' => 'mark'],
        );

        return ['tombstoned' => $tombstoned, 'skipped' => $skipped];
    }

    /**
     * PHASE 2 — sweep tombstoned blobs past their grace and physically delete.
     *
     * @return array{swept: int, skipped: int}
     */
    public function sweep(): array
    {
        $swept = 0;
        $skipped = 0;
        $hadBatch = false;

        $this->metrics->timing(
            name: 'vaulter_gc_duration_seconds',
            operation: function () use (&$swept, &$skipped, &$hadBatch): void {
                foreach ($this->candidateBatches(static fn (Builder $q): Builder => $q->sweepable()) as $batch) {
                    $hadBatch = true;
                    $batchSwept = 0;

                    foreach ($batch as $candidate) {
                        $deleted = $this->serializable(fn (): bool => $this->lockedTransaction($candidate, function (Blob $blob): bool {
                            // (the body runs inside a SERIALIZABLE transaction on PG — see
                            // serializable()/lockedTransaction(): the isolation level is set as
                            // the FIRST statement inside the transaction, before the lock.)
                            // A resurrection between scan and lock flips it back to committed —
                            // never delete then.
                            if ($blob->status !== BlobStatus::Tombstoned) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            if ($this->edges->hasLiveEdge($blob)) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            if ($blob->delete_after_at === null || $blob->delete_after_at->isFuture()) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            // Claim it, delete the scope-qualified object, finalise the
                            // idempotent tombstone. A missing object is fine — markDeleted is
                            // idempotent.
                            $this->blobs->markDeleting($blob);
                            $this->content->delete($blob->storage_key, $blob->disk);
                            $this->blobs->markDeleted($blob);

                            return true;
                        }, serializable: true));

                        $deleted ? $batchSwept++ : $skipped++;
                    }

                    $swept += $batchSwept;
                    $this->metrics->histogram(name: 'vaulter_gc_batch_rows', value: $batch->count());
                    $this->metrics->counter(name: 'vaulter_gc_objects_deleted_total', amount: $batchSwept);
                }
            },
            labels: ['phase' => 'sweep'],
        );

        if (!$hadBatch) {
            $this->metrics->counter(name: 'vaulter_gc_objects_deleted_total', amount: 0);
        }

        return ['swept' => $swept, 'skipped' => $skipped];
    }

    /**
     * Sweep PENDING blobs from a crashed staging (H.3): a pending row older than
     * the grace window whose commit never finished and that carries no edge. Its
     * (possibly partial) object is deleted and the row is finalised as a deleted
     * tombstone, all under the lock so a slow-but-live commit is not stolen.
     *
     * @return array{swept: int, skipped: int}
     */
    public function sweepPending(int $graceHours = 0): array
    {
        $grace = Config::gcGraceHours($graceHours);
        $cutoff = now()->subHours($grace);
        $swept = 0;
        $skipped = 0;

        $batches = $this->candidateBatches(static fn (Builder $q): Builder => $q
            ->where('status', BlobStatus::Pending->value)
            ->where('first_seen_at', '<', $cutoff));

        foreach ($batches as $batch) {
            foreach ($batch as $candidate) {
                // Symmetry with sweep() (F-P1.5-02): the pending-sweep runs its unit
                // SERIALIZABLE on PostgreSQL with retry-on-40001, so a transient
                // serialization failure under concurrent GC is retried rather than
                // propagated as a QueryException that aborts the whole pass.
                $acted = $this->serializable(fn (): bool => $this->lockedTransaction($candidate, function (Blob $blob) use ($cutoff): bool {
                    if ($blob->status !== BlobStatus::Pending) {
                        return false;
                    }

                    // A pending row that grew an edge (a commit landing right now) is
                    // live — leave it.
                    if ($this->edges->hasLiveEdge($blob)) {
                        return false;
                    }

                    if ($blob->first_seen_at !== null && $blob->first_seen_at->greaterThanOrEqualTo($cutoff)) {
                        return false;
                    }

                    $this->blobs->markDeleting($blob);
                    $this->content->delete($blob->storage_key, $blob->disk);
                    $this->blobs->markDeleted($blob);

                    return true;
                }, serializable: true));

                $acted ? $swept++ : $skipped++;
            }

            $this->metrics->histogram(name: 'vaulter_gc_batch_rows', value: $batch->count());
        }

        return ['swept' => $swept, 'skipped' => $skipped];
    }

    /**
     * Run a single-blob critical section inside a transaction whose first
     * statement is the per-(scope,sha256) lock, re-reading the row FOR UPDATE on
     * MySQL. The callback receives the locked, fresh row and returns whether it
     * acted.
     *
     * When $serializable is set on PostgreSQL the isolation level is raised as the
     * VERY FIRST statement inside the open transaction block — the only place
     * `SET TRANSACTION` takes effect on PG (issued outside a block it warns and is
     * ignored). It precedes even the advisory lock, which is allowed: SET
     * TRANSACTION only forbids a prior data statement, not a prior SET.
     *
     * The raise is skipped when this is NOT a top-level transaction (transactionLevel
     * > 1) — i.e. the GC unit runs nested inside a caller's open transaction (a host
     * wrapping the sweep, or RefreshDatabase in tests). PostgreSQL forbids changing the
     * isolation level once a transaction has issued a data statement, so SET would 25001;
     * the only valid behaviour is to inherit the enclosing transaction's isolation. The
     * advisory lock + FOR UPDATE still serialise the critical section either way.
     *
     * @param \Closure(Blob): bool $work
     */
    private function lockedTransaction(Blob $candidate, \Closure $work, bool $serializable = false): bool
    {
        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($candidate, $work, $serializable): bool {
            if ($serializable && $this->lock->isPostgres() && $this->database->connection()->transactionLevel() <= 1) {
                $this->database->connection()->statement('SET TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL SERIALIZABLE');
            }

            $this->lock->acquire(scope: $this->scopeFor($candidate), sha256: $candidate->sha256);

            $fresh = $this->blobs->lockForUpdate($candidate);

            return $work($fresh);
        });
    }

    /**
     * On PostgreSQL run the unit SERIALIZABLE and retry on a 40001 serialization
     * failure (H.3) — the isolation level itself is raised INSIDE the transaction
     * (see lockedTransaction), this only drives the retry loop. On MySQL/SQLite the
     * FOR-UPDATE/single-writer path already serialises, so run once.
     *
     * @param \Closure(): bool $unit
     */
    private function serializable(\Closure $unit): bool
    {
        if (!$this->lock->isPostgres()) {
            return $unit();
        }

        $attempt = 0;

        while (true) {
            try {
                return $unit();
            } catch (QueryException $exception) {
                $attempt++;

                if (!$this->isSerializationFailure($exception) || $attempt >= self::MAX_SERIALIZATION_RETRIES) {
                    throw $exception;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private function isSerializationFailure(Throwable $exception): bool
    {
        // PostgreSQL serialization_failure → SQLSTATE 40001.
        return str_contains($exception->getMessage(), '40001');
    }

    /**
     * The scope discriminator for a blob's advisory lock — the same segment baked
     * into its storage_key, so GC and commit lock the same liveness unit.
     */
    private function scopeFor(Blob $blob): string
    {
        return $blob->scope_key;
    }

    /**
     * Lock before reading state/liveness, but amortise the transaction and reads
     * over a bounded batch. Keep repository writes so model events still fire.
     *
     * @param Collection<int, Blob> $batch
     */
    private function markBatch(Collection $batch, int $graceHours): int
    {
        $model = Config::blobModel();

        // Custom models/observers may change another candidate while saving.
        // Preserve the per-row fresh liveness check for those extension points.
        if ($model !== Blob::class || $this->hasBlobListeners()) {
            $marked = 0;

            foreach ($batch as $candidate) {
                $acted = $this->lockedTransaction($candidate, function (Blob $blob) use ($graceHours): bool {
                    if ($blob->status !== BlobStatus::Committed || $this->edges->hasLiveEdge($blob)) {
                        return false;
                    }

                    $this->blobs->tombstone(blob: $blob, graceHours: $graceHours);

                    return true;
                });

                $marked += (int) $acted;
            }

            return $marked;
        }

        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($batch, $graceHours): int {
            if ($this->lock->isPostgres()) {
                $keys = $batch->map(fn (Blob $blob): string => 'cas:'.$this->scopeFor($blob).':'.$blob->sha256)->all();

                // The same hashtext namespace as BlobLock. Acquire distinct locks
                // in hash order (including collisions), before any data read.
                $this->database->connection()->select(
                    'SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(lock_key) FROM '
                    .'(SELECT DISTINCT hashtext(value) AS lock_key '
                    .'FROM jsonb_array_elements_text(CAST(? AS jsonb)) ORDER BY lock_key) AS locks',
                    [json_encode($keys, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR)],
                );
            }

            $model = Config::blobModel();
            $query = $model::query()->whereKey($batch->modelKeys())->orderBy((new $model)->getKeyName());

            if ($this->lock->usesRowLock()) {
                $query->lockForUpdate();
            }

            $fresh = $query->get();
            $edgeModel = Config::fileVersionBlobModel();
            /** @var \Illuminate\Support\Collection<int, int|string> $liveIds */
            $liveIds = $edgeModel::query()->whereIn('blob_id', $batch->modelKeys())->distinct()->pluck('blob_id');
            $live = array_fill_keys($liveIds->all(), true);
            $marked = 0;

            foreach ($fresh as $blob) {
                if ($blob->status !== BlobStatus::Committed) {
                    continue;
                }

                if (isset($live[$blob->id])) {
                    continue;
                }

                $this->blobs->tombstone(blob: $blob, graceHours: $graceHours);
                $marked++;
            }

            return $marked;
        });
    }

    private function hasBlobListeners(): bool
    {
        $dispatcher = Blob::getEventDispatcher();

        return array_any(
            ['retrieved', 'saving', 'saved', 'updating', 'updated'],
            static fn (string $event): bool => $dispatcher?->hasListeners('eloquent.'.$event.': '.Blob::class) ?? false,
        );
    }

    /**
     * Scan by key, never offset: processed and skipped rows both advance the
     * checkpoint even though status changes remove rows from the candidate set.
     * A phase-start high-water key keeps inserts from extending a pass forever.
     * Queries and materialised batches hold at most BATCH_SIZE rows, as the
     * system (no panel/workspace — GC is ACL-blind).
     *
     * @param  \Closure(Builder<Blob>): Builder<Blob> $scope
     * @return iterable<Collection<int, Blob>>
     */
    private function candidateBatches(\Closure $scope): iterable
    {
        $model = Config::blobModel();
        $query = $scope($model::query());
        $key = $query->getModel()->getKeyName();
        $upper = (clone $query)->reorder($key, 'desc')->value($key);

        if ($upper === null) {
            return;
        }

        $last = null;

        do {
            $page = (clone $query)->where($key, '<=', $upper)->reorder($key)->limit(self::BATCH_SIZE);

            if ($last !== null) {
                $page->where($key, '>', $last);
            }

            $batch = $page->get();

            if ($batch->isEmpty()) {
                return;
            }

            $last = $batch->last()->getKey();

            yield $batch;
        } while ($batch->count() === self::BATCH_SIZE);
    }
}
