# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/src/Cas/BlobReconciler.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Cas;

use Vaulter\Cas\BlobLock;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ListableContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Cas\BlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileVersionBlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\Support\Identity;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Blob reconciliation (H.3): ref_count is a CACHE, not the truth, so it drifts and
 * must be rebuilt from the edge table — the real liveness source. Reconciliation
 * also surfaces the two integrity hazards a CAS must watch, the inverse images of
 * each other:
 *
 *  - DANGLING ROW   — a committed blob row whose canonical object is missing on
 *    disk (a half-promoted commit, a botched manual delete). Found by reconcile()
 *    walking the ROW side.
 *  - ORPHAN OBJECT  — bytes on disk with NO blob row at all (a commit promoted its
 *    bytes then its transaction rolled the row back; or leaked bytes). Found by
 *    sweepOrphans() walking the DISK side.
 *
 * reconcile() never deletes — it rewrites the ref_count cache and reports.
 * sweepOrphans() is report-only by default; with $prune it physically deletes true
 * orphans under the SAME per-(scope,sha256) lock + grace discipline the GC uses, so
 * a concurrent commit can never have its in-flight bytes swept out from under it.
 *
 * Two capability gates make the destructive path safe and BC-clean:
 *  - LISTING. The disk-walk needs the CAS driver to enumerate the disk. That seam
 *    lives on the optional {@see ListableContentStore} capability (NOT the base
 *    ContentStore contract — adding to it would break every external driver). When
 *    the active store is not listable, sweepOrphans() refuses cleanly (reports
 *    listingSupported=false) instead of failing; this also surfaces the S3 /
 *    non-FilesystemAdapter "cannot enumerate" case rather than silently skipping.
 *  - PRUNE SERIALISATION. The prune re-reads under the lock then deletes BYTES with
 *    NO row write, so it is ordered against a concurrent commit ONLY on drivers
 *    whose lock serialises a write-less section (PG advisory xact lock; MySQL FOR
 *    UPDATE). On SQLite that ordering never engages (acquire() is a no-op, FOR
 *    UPDATE is ignored) so a concurrent commit could INSERT the owning row between
 *    the recheck and the delete and we would delete LIVE bytes — therefore prune is
 *    REFUSED on SQLite (pruneSupported=false): detection still runs, deletion does
 *    not. Report-only works on EVERY driver. (The GC is immune: it writes a real
 *    markDeleting() that forces SQLite's write lock; the prune deliberately writes
 *    nothing, so it cannot lean on that.)
 */
final readonly class BlobReconciler
{
    /** Both historical two-shard keys and lossless single-shard keys. */
    private const string KEY_PATTERN = '#^(?<scope>global|[ts](?:\d+|null|[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26})|g|[td]-~?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128})/[0-9a-f]{2}/(?:[0-9a-f]{2}/)?(?<sha256>[0-9a-f]{64})$#';

    public function __construct(
        private ContentStore $content,
        private BlobStoreRepository $blobs,
        private FileVersionBlobStoreRepository $edges,
        private BlobLock $lock,
        private MetricsRecorder $metrics,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    /**
     * Rebuild ref_count for every blob from its live edge count and collect the
     * dangling rows. Returns a report tally.
     *
     * @return array{reconciled: int, changed: int, dangling: list<string>}
     */
    public function reconcile(): array
    {
        $reconciled = 0;
        $changed = 0;
        $dangling = [];

        $this->metrics->timing(
            name: 'vaulter_reconcile_duration_seconds',
            operation: function () use (&$reconciled, &$changed, &$dangling): void {
                foreach ($this->blobModel()::query()->cursor() as $blob) {
                    /** @var Blob $blob */
                    $reconciled++;

                    $count = $this->edges->countEdges($blob);

                    if ($blob->ref_count !== $count) {
                        $this->blobs->setRefCount(blob: $blob, count: $count);
                        $changed++;
                    }

                    // A committed blob must have its bytes on disk; a missing object is a
                    // dangling row the operator should investigate (re-upload or purge).
                    if ($blob->status === BlobStatus::Committed && !$this->content->exists($blob->storage_key, $blob->disk)) {
                        $dangling[] = Identity::stringify($blob->getKey());
                    }
                }
            },
        );

        if ($reconciled > 0) {
            $this->metrics->counter(name: 'vaulter_reconcile_objects_total', amount: $reconciled);
        }

        return [
            'reconciled' => $reconciled,
            'changed' => $changed,
            'dangling' => $dangling,
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Walk the DISK for orphan objects — bytes that match the committed CAS key
     * layout but have NO blob row — and, when $prune AND the driver serialises the
     * write-less prune section, physically delete the true orphans (H.3).
     * Report-only by default (a safe dry run on EVERY driver).
     *
     * CAPABILITY GATES:
     *  - If the active store cannot enumerate the disk (not a {@see ListableContentStore},
     *    e.g. a streaming-only or non-FilesystemAdapter driver) the walk is REFUSED:
     *    returns listingSupported=false, scanned 0, no deletion — the command reports
     *    "listing unsupported", not a fatal error.
     *  - If $prune is requested but the driver does NOT serialise a write-less
     *    critical section (SQLite — see {@see BlobLock::serializesCriticalSection()}),
     *    pruning is REFUSED: detection still runs and orphans are reported, but
     *    pruneSupported=false and nothing is deleted (deleting under SQLite would
     *    race a concurrent commit and could destroy live bytes). Run report-only, or
     *    prune on the production PostgreSQL/MySQL.
     *
     * SAFETY (this deletes bytes when it does prune):
     *  1. A path is a candidate ONLY if it EXACTLY matches the committed key layout
     *     (KEY_PATTERN) AND keyFor(scope, sha256) re-derives the SAME path — so the
     *     shard bytes are the real shard of that sha256. Staging/tmp, resumable
     *     parts and foreign files never match and are skipped untouched.
     *  2. A matching path with ANY blob row (looked up DISK-AGNOSTICALLY, since
     *     storage_key is globally unique) is owned, not an orphan.
     *  3. A row-less object is a deletable orphan only once it is OLDER than the
     *     orphan grace — a just-promoted object may be a commit whose row is not yet
     *     durable; a fresher one is skipped.
     *  4. Pruning happens under the per-(scope,sha256) lock inside a transaction,
     *     RE-checking both the row-absence (under the MySQL row lock) and the grace
     *     AFTER taking the lock (a concurrent commit may have just created the row /
     *     re-touched the bytes), then deletes idempotently.
     *
     * @return array{scanned: int, orphans: list<string>, pruned: int, skipped: int, listingSupported: bool, pruneSupported: bool}
     */
    public function sweepOrphans(?string $disk = null, int $graceHours = 0, bool $prune = false): array
    {
        // The disk-walk seam is an OPTIONAL capability, not the base ContentStore
        // contract: a non-listable store cannot be walked, so refuse cleanly.
        $content = $this->content;

        if (!$content instanceof ListableContentStore) {
            return $this->unsupportedListing();
        }

        $disk ??= Config::defaultDisk();
        $grace = Config::orphanGraceHours($graceHours);

        // The prune deletes bytes with NO row write, so it is only safe on a driver
        // whose lock serialises a write-less section. On SQLite we still DETECT and
        // REPORT, but never delete.
        $pruneSupported = $this->lock->serializesCriticalSection();
        $doPrune = $prune && $pruneSupported;

        $scanned = 0;
        $orphans = [];
        $pruned = 0;
        $skipped = 0;

        foreach ($content->listKeys($disk) as $path) {
            $scanned++;

            $parsed = $this->parseManagedKey($path);

            // Not a committed CAS object (staging/tmp, resumable part, foreign file,
            // or a mis-sharded path that does not round-trip) — never touch it.
            if ($parsed === null) {
                $skipped++;

                continue;
            }

            // An object owned by a blob row (any status, ANY disk — storage_key is
            // globally unique) is not an orphan.
            if ($this->blobs->findByStorageKey(storageKey: $path) !== null) {
                $skipped++;

                continue;
            }

            // A row-less object younger than the grace may be a commit-in-progress
            // whose row is not yet durable — leave it for a later pass.
            if (!$this->isPastGrace(content: $content, path: $path, disk: $disk, graceHours: $grace)) {
                $skipped++;

                continue;
            }

            $orphans[] = $path;

            if (!$doPrune) {
                continue;
            }

            $this->pruneOrphan(content: $content, path: $path, disk: $disk, scope: $parsed['scope'], sha256: $parsed['sha256'], graceHours: $grace)
                ? $pruned++
                : $skipped++;
        }

        return [
            'scanned' => $scanned,
            'orphans' => $orphans,
            'pruned' => $pruned,
            'skipped' => $skipped,
            'listingSupported' => true,
            'pruneSupported' => $pruneSupported,
        ];
    }

    /**
     * The walk-refused result for a store that cannot enumerate the disk: nothing
     * scanned, nothing pruned, both capabilities reported off.
     *
     * @return array{scanned: int, orphans: list<string>, pruned: int, skipped: int, listingSupported: bool, pruneSupported: bool}
     */
    private function unsupportedListing(): array
    {
        return [
            'scanned' => 0,
            'orphans' => [],
            'pruned' => 0,
            'skipped' => 0,
            'listingSupported' => false,
            'pruneSupported' => false,
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Delete one orphan under the per-(scope,sha256) advisory lock (mirrors
     * BlobGarbageCollector::lockedTransaction): take the lock as the first statement,
     * then RE-CHECK that no row appeared and the object is still past grace before
     * the idempotent physical delete. Returns whether the object was deleted (false
     * ⇒ a concurrent commit won the race / the bytes were re-touched / already gone).
     *
     * Only ever reached on a driver whose lock serialises this write-less section
     * (sweepOrphans gates on {@see BlobLock::serializesCriticalSection()}); the
     * row-absence recheck takes the MySQL row lock so a concurrent INSERT contends.
     *
     * @param ContentStore&ListableContentStore $content the active store, already
     *                                                   confirmed listable by the caller so its age probe is usable here
     */
    private function pruneOrphan(ContentStore&ListableContentStore $content, string $path, string $disk, string $scope, string $sha256, int $graceHours): bool
    {
        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($content, $path, $disk, $scope, $sha256, $graceHours): bool {
            $this->lock->acquire(scope: $scope, sha256: $sha256);

            // A commit that won the lock between the cheap scan and here has now
            // created the row — the bytes are owned, never delete them. Disk-agnostic
            // (storage_key is globally unique); lock: true takes the MySQL row lock.
            if ($this->blobs->findByStorageKey(storageKey: $path, lock: true) !== null) {
                return false;
            }

            // The same commit may have re-promoted (re-touched) the bytes; re-apply
            // the grace under the lock so a fresh object is never swept.
            if (!$this->isPastGrace(content: $content, path: $path, disk: $disk, graceHours: $graceHours)) {
                return false;
            }

            // Idempotent: a missing object is fine.
            $content->delete($path, $disk);

            return true;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Whether the object is older than the orphan grace. A null mtime (absent or the
     * disk cannot report it) is treated as NOT past grace, so an object whose age we
     * cannot establish is skipped, never deleted.
     */
    private function isPastGrace(ContentStore&ListableContentStore $content, string $path, string $disk, int $graceHours): bool
    {
        $lastModified = $content->lastModified($path, $disk);

        if ($lastModified === null) {
            return false;
        }

        return $lastModified < now()->subHours($graceHours)->getTimestamp();
    }

    /**
     * Classify a disk path as a committed CAS object and extract its (scope, sha256),
     * or null when it is not one. The match is exact AND verified by round-tripping
     * keyFor(scope, sha256) === $path, so the shard bytes in the path are guaranteed
     * to be the real shard of that sha256 (a mis-sharded or scope-spoofed path is
     * rejected). This is the primary guard that the sweep only ever touches genuine
     * canonical objects.
     *
     * @return array{scope: string, sha256: string}|null
     */
    private function parseManagedKey(string $path): ?array
    {
        if (preg_match(self::KEY_PATTERN, $path, $matches) !== 1) {
            return null;
        }

        try {
            $scope = BlobScope::fromSegment($matches['scope']);
        } catch (\InvalidArgumentException) {
            return null;
        }
        $sha256 = $matches['sha256'];

        // Re-derive the canonical key from the parsed parts; the shard bytes must be
        // the real shard of this sha256 for the path to round-trip.
        if ($this->content->keyFor(scope: $scope, sha256: $sha256) !== $path) {
            return null;
        }

        return ['scope' => $scope->key(), 'sha256' => $sha256];
    }

    /**
     * @return class-string<Blob>
     */
    private function blobModel(): string
    {
        return Config::blobModel();
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/src/Cas/BlobStore.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Cas;

use Vaulter\Cas\BlobLock;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore as BlobStoreContract;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersion;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Cas\BlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileVersionBlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Stage and hash outside the transaction, then lock (scope_key, sha256) before
 * row lookup, resurrection and promotion. SQL rollback can leave orphan bytes;
 * the reconciler uses this same lock before collecting them.
 */
final readonly class BlobStore implements BlobStoreContract
{
    public function __construct(
        private ContentStore $content,
        private BlobLock $lock,
        private BlobStoreRepository $blobs,
        private FileVersionBlobStoreRepository $edges,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    /**
     * @param resource $stream
     */
    public function commit(mixed $stream, BlobScope $scope, ?string $disk = null): Blob
    {
        $disk ??= Config::defaultDisk();

        // Stage + hash OUTSIDE the transaction: hashing a multi-GB upload must not
        // hold the row/advisory lock. The lock guards only the liveness decision.
        $staged = $this->content->stage($stream);
        $storageKey = $this->content->keyFor(scope: $scope, sha256: $staged->sha256());

        try {
            return $this->database->transaction(function () use (
                $staged, $scope, $disk, $storageKey
            ): Blob {
                // H.3 invariant: the lock is the FIRST statement; every liveness
                // decision below reads rows acquired after it.
                $this->lock->acquire(scope: $scope->key(), sha256: $staged->sha256());

                $result = $this->blobs->findOrCreate(
                    sha256: $staged->sha256(),
                    sizeBytes: $staged->sizeBytes(),
                    storageKey: $storageKey,
                    disk: $disk,
                    scope: $scope,
                    tenantId: $scope->tenantId(),
                    storageId: $scope->storageId(),
                );

                // Promote bytes only when the row is newly created (dedup hit ⇒
                // skip the physical write). promote() is itself idempotent and
                // conditional, so a resurrected row whose object may be gone is
                // re-promoted safely.
                if ($result['created'] || !$this->content->exists($result['blob']->storage_key, $result['blob']->disk)) {
                    $this->content->promote(staged: $staged, key: $result['blob']->storage_key, disk: $result['blob']->disk);
                }

                return $result['blob'];
            });
        } finally {
            $staged->discard();
        }
    }

    /**
     * @param array{role?: string, ordinal?: int, offset_bytes?: int|null, length_bytes?: int|null} $attributes
     */
    public function attach(FileVersion $version, Blob $blob, array $attributes = []): void
    {
        $this->edges->attach(version: $version, blob: $blob, attributes: $attributes);
    }

    public function detach(FileVersion $version, Blob $blob, string $role = 'data', int $ordinal = 0): void
    {
        $this->edges->detach(version: $version, blob: $blob, role: $role, ordinal: $ordinal);
    }

    /**
     * @return resource|null
     */
    public function read(Blob $blob): mixed
    {
        return $this->content->get($blob->storage_key, $blob->disk);
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/src/Cas/Sha256ContentStore.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Cas;

use RuntimeException;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ListableContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ListableStorageDriver;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StagedObject;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StorageDriver;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * The default ContentStore (H.3): hash the LOCAL staged temp file, never the live
 * stream. An incoming stream is drained into a seekable temp file; the sha256 is
 * computed from those durable bytes (a lying client sha256 or an S3 multipart
 * ETag is irrelevant). Promotion writes the staged bytes to the canonical
 * scope-qualified key with a conditional create so a concurrent identical upload
 * cannot clobber, and on S3 attaches x-amz-checksum-sha256 for server-side
 * verification (config-aware; ignored by the local/sqlite test disk).
 */
final readonly class Sha256ContentStore implements ContentStore, ListableContentStore
{
    /**
     * Read buffer for streaming the upload into the staged temp file.
     */
    private const int CHUNK_BYTES = 1_048_576;

    public function __construct(
        private readonly StorageDriver $driver,
    ) {}

    /**
     * @param resource $stream
     */
    public function stage(mixed $stream): StagedObject
    {
        if (!is_resource($stream)) {
            throw new RuntimeException('ContentStore::stage() expects an open stream resource.');
        }

        $path = $this->newTempPath();
        $target = @fopen($path, 'wb');

        if ($target === false) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Unable to open staging temp file [{$path}].");
        }

        $hash = hash_init('sha256');
        $size = 0;

        // Rewind the source where possible so a partially consumed stream still
        // stages whole; non-seekable streams (php://input) are read as-is.
        if (stream_get_meta_data($stream)['seekable']) {
            @rewind($stream);
        }

        while (!feof($stream)) {
            $buffer = fread($stream, self::CHUNK_BYTES);

            if ($buffer === false) {
                break;
            }

            hash_update($hash, $buffer);
            $size += strlen($buffer);
            fwrite($target, $buffer);
        }

        fclose($target);

        return new StagedTempObject(
            path: $path,
            sha256: hash_final($hash),
            sizeBytes: $size,
        );
    }

    public function promote(StagedObject $staged, string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        // Already at the canonical key (a dedup hit promoted by a concurrent
        // uploader, or an idempotent re-commit) — nothing to write.
        if ($this->driver->exists($key, $disk)) {
            return;
        }

        $stream = $staged->readStream();

        try {
            // If-None-Match: * — a 412/409 means we were overtaken; the driver
            // returns false and we treat that as "already there" (H.3), not fatal.
            $this->driver->put(
                key: $key,
                stream: $stream,
                disk: $disk,
                ifNoneMatch: true,
                options: ['ChecksumSHA256' => $this->base64Sha256($staged->sha256())],
            );
        } finally {
            if (is_resource($stream)) {
                fclose($stream);
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * @return resource|null
     */
    public function get(string $key, ?string $disk = null): mixed
    {
        return $this->driver->get($key, $disk);
    }

    public function exists(string $key, ?string $disk = null): bool
    {
        return $this->driver->exists($key, $disk);
    }

    public function delete(string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        $this->driver->delete($key, $disk);
    }

    public function lastModified(string $key, ?string $disk = null): ?int
    {
        // The listing capability lives on the DRIVER, not the base StorageDriver
        // contract; a non-listable driver cannot report age, so we degrade to null
        // (age unknown ⇒ the orphan walk skips rather than deletes).
        if (!$this->driver instanceof ListableStorageDriver) {
            return null;
        }

        return $this->driver->lastModified($key, $disk);
    }

    /**
     * @return iterable<string>
     */
    public function listKeys(?string $disk = null): iterable
    {
        // Only delegate when the underlying driver can actually enumerate the disk;
        // otherwise yield nothing so this store reports no objects rather than
        // calling a method the base StorageDriver contract no longer carries.
        if (!$this->driver instanceof ListableStorageDriver) {
            return;
        }

        yield from $this->driver->listContents($disk);
    }

    public function keyFor(BlobScope $scope, string $sha256): string
    {
        $segment = $scope->segment();
        $a = substr($sha256, 0, 2);
        $legacyShard = $scope->isLegacy() ? substr($sha256, 2, 2).'/' : '';

        return "{$segment}/{$a}/{$legacyShard}{$sha256}";
    }

    /**
     * S3 expects the checksum as base64 of the raw digest, not the hex string.
     */
    private function base64Sha256(string $hexSha256): string
    {
        $raw = hex2bin($hexSha256);

        return $raw === false ? '' : base64_encode($raw);
    }

    private function newTempPath(): string
    {
        $path = tempnam(sys_get_temp_dir(), 'vaulter_cas_');

        if ($path === false) {
            throw new RuntimeException('Unable to allocate a staging temp file.');
        }

        return $path;
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/src/Console/RepairBlobScopesCommand.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Console;

use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Illuminate\Database\Query\Builder;
use Illuminate\Support\Collection;
use RuntimeException;
use Throwable;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Cas\BlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

final class RepairBlobScopesCommand extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes {--dry-run} {--execute}';

    protected $description = 'Copy legacy mixed-scope blobs to their lossless scopes and atomically redirect revision edges';

    public function handle(VaulterDatabase $database, ContentStore $content, BlobStoreRepository $blobs): int
    {
        if ((bool) $this->option('dry-run') === (bool) $this->option('execute')) {
            $this->error('Select exactly one of --dry-run or --execute.');

            return self::INVALID;
        }

        $repaired = 0;
        $skipped = 0;
        $failed = false;
        $model = Config::blobModel();
        $model::query()->whereIn('scope_key', ['legacy:snull', 'legacy:tnull'])->chunkById(100, function ($batch) use ($database, $content, $blobs, &$repaired, &$skipped, &$failed): void {
            $rows = [];
            foreach ($batch as $blob) {
                $references = $this->referenceRows($database, $blob);
                $groups = $references->groupBy(function ($ref) use ($blob): string {
                    return $this->target($blob, (string) $ref->storage_id, $ref->tenant_id === null ? null : (string) $ref->tenant_id)->key();
                });
                if ($groups->isEmpty()) {
                    $rows[] = [$blob->getKey(), $blob->sha256, $blob->scope_key, '—', 0, '—', 'unreferenced'];
                    $skipped++;
                }

                foreach ($groups as $refs) {
                    $first = $refs->first() ?? throw new RuntimeException('Missing scope repair reference.');
                    $scope = $this->target($blob, (string) $first->storage_id, $first->tenant_id === null ? null : (string) $first->tenant_id);
                    $action = 'move';
                    try {
                        if ($this->option('execute')) {
                            $action = $this->repair($database, $content, $blobs, $blob, $scope, array_values(array_unique($refs->map(static fn ($ref): string => (string) $ref->storage_id)->all())));
                            $action === 'move' ? $repaired++ : $skipped++;
                        } elseif (!$this->validObject($content, $blob->storage_key, $blob->disk, $blob->sha256)) {
                            $action = 'hash_mismatch';
                            $skipped++;
                        }
                    } catch (Throwable $exception) {
                        $action = 'error';
                        $failed = true;
                        $skipped++;
                        $this->error($exception->getMessage());
                    }
                    $rows[] = [$blob->getKey(), $blob->sha256, $blob->scope_key, $refs->pluck('storage_id')->unique()->implode(','), $refs->count(), $scope->key(), $action];
                }
            }
            $this->table(['blob_id', 'sha256', 'scope_key', 'storage_id', 'refs', 'target', 'action'], $rows);
        });
        $this->info("Repaired: {$repaired}, skipped: {$skipped}");

        return $failed ? self::FAILURE : self::SUCCESS;
    }

    /**
     * @param  list<string>|null                                                                                   $storageIds
     * @return Collection<int, object{version_id: int|string, storage_id: int|string, tenant_id: int|string|null}>
     */
    private function referenceRows(VaulterDatabase $database, Blob $blob, ?array $storageIds = null): Collection
    {
        $query = $this->references($database, $blob);
        if ($storageIds !== null) {
            $query->whereIn('storages.id', $storageIds);
        }
        /** @var Collection<int, object{version_id: int|string, storage_id: int|string, tenant_id: int|string|null}> $rows */
        $rows = $query->get();

        return $rows;
    }

    private function references(VaulterDatabase $database, Blob $blob): Builder
    {
        return $database->connection()->table(Config::table('file_version_blobs').' as edges')
            ->join(Config::table('file_versions').' as versions', 'versions.id', '=', 'edges.file_version_id')
            ->join(Config::table('files').' as files', 'files.id', '=', 'versions.file_id')
            ->join(Config::table('nodes').' as nodes', 'nodes.id', '=', 'files.node_id')
            ->join(Config::table('storages').' as storages', 'storages.id', '=', 'nodes.storage_id')
            ->where('edges.blob_id', $blob->getKey())
            ->select(['versions.id as version_id', 'storages.id as storage_id', 'storages.tenant_id']);
    }

    private function target(Blob $blob, string $storageId, ?string $tenantId): BlobScope
    {
        return $blob->scope_key === 'legacy:snull' ? BlobScope::drive($storageId)
            : ($tenantId === null ? BlobScope::global() : BlobScope::tenant($tenantId));
    }

    /** @param list<string> $storageIds */
    private function repair(VaulterDatabase $database, ContentStore $content, BlobStoreRepository $blobs, Blob $candidate, BlobScope $scope, array $storageIds): string
    {
        return $database->transaction(function () use ($database, $content, $blobs, $candidate, $scope, $storageIds): string {
            // Follow the tree-before-CAS order so uploads and purge cannot change
            // this revision chain while the repair redirects its edges.
            sort($storageIds, SORT_STRING);
            foreach ($storageIds as $id) {
                $database->lockTree($id);
            }
            $keys = [$candidate->scope_key, $scope->key()];
            sort($keys, SORT_STRING);
            foreach ($keys as $key) {
                $database->advisoryLock('cas:'.$key.':'.$candidate->sha256);
            }
            $old = $blobs->lockForUpdate($candidate);
            $refs = $this->referenceRows($database, $old, $storageIds)
                ->filter(fn ($ref): bool => $this->target($old, (string) $ref->storage_id, $ref->tenant_id === null ? null : (string) $ref->tenant_id)->key() === $scope->key());
            if ($refs->isEmpty()) {
                return 'unreferenced';
            }

            $stream = $content->get($old->storage_key, $old->disk);
            if (!is_resource($stream)) {
                return 'hash_mismatch';
            }
            try {
                $staged = $content->stage($stream);
            } finally {
                fclose($stream);
            }
            try {
                if (!hash_equals($old->sha256, $staged->sha256())) {
                    return 'hash_mismatch';
                }
                $result = $blobs->findOrCreate($old->sha256, $staged->sizeBytes(), $content->keyFor($scope, $old->sha256), $old->disk, $scope, $scope->tenantId(), $scope->storageId());
                $target = $result['blob'];
                $content->promote($staged, $target->storage_key, $target->disk);
                if (!$this->validObject($content, $target->storage_key, $target->disk, $old->sha256)) {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Repair target bytes failed SHA-256 verification.');
                }
                $db = $database->connection();
                $edges = $db->table(Config::table('file_version_blobs'));
                $count = $edges->whereIn('file_version_id', $refs->pluck('version_id')->unique()->all())->where('blob_id', $old->getKey())->update(['blob_id' => $target->getKey()]);
                $db->table(Config::table('file_versions'))->whereIn('id', $refs->pluck('version_id')->unique()->all())
                    ->where('blob_id', $old->getKey())->update(['blob_id' => $target->getKey()]);
                // ref_count is a cache: repair from live edges rather than decrement
                // a possibly stale cache below zero.
                foreach ([$old, $target] as $blob) {
                    $blobs->setRefCount($blob, $db->table(Config::table('file_version_blobs'))->where('blob_id', $blob->getKey())->count());
                }
                $entry = json_encode(['ts' => now()->toIso8601String(), 'blob_id' => $old->getKey(), 'target' => $scope->key(), 'action' => 'move', 'edges' => $count], JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR)."\n";
                $database->afterCommit(static function () use ($entry): void {
                    $dir = storage_path('logs');
                    if (!is_dir($dir) && !mkdir($dir, 0o755, true) && !is_dir($dir)) {
                        throw new RuntimeException('Cannot create scope repair log directory.');
                    }
                    if (file_put_contents($dir.'/vaulter-repair-scopes.log', $entry, FILE_APPEND | LOCK_EX) === false) {
                        throw new RuntimeException('Cannot append scope repair log.');
                    }
                });

                return 'move';
            } finally {
                $staged->discard();
            }
        });
    }

    private function validObject(ContentStore $content, string $key, string $disk, string $sha256): bool
    {
        $stream = $content->get($key, $disk);
        if (!is_resource($stream)) {
            return false;
        }
        try {
            $hash = hash_init('sha256');
            while (!feof($stream)) {
                $buffer = fread($stream, 1_048_576);
                if ($buffer === false) {
                    return false;
                }
                hash_update($hash, $buffer);
            }

            return hash_equals($sha256, hash_final($hash));
        } finally {
            fclose($stream);
        }
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/src/VaulterMediaServiceProvider.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media;

use Spatie\LaravelPackageTools\Package;
use Spatie\LaravelPackageTools\PackageServiceProvider;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StorageDriver;
use Vaulter\Media\Console\GcBlobsCommand;
use Vaulter\Media\Console\ReconcileBlobsCommand;
use Vaulter\Media\Console\RepairBlobScopesCommand;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;

/**
 * Vaulter Media — the content-addressed file/blob layer (CAS), extracted from
 * vaulter-core (P2 #5, ADR decisions D2/D3). This package OWNS the concrete CAS
 * engine (ContentStore / StorageDriver / BlobStore implementations, the two-phase
 * GC + reconciler and their commands); the seam contracts, the Blob model/schema
 * and the per-file ACL stay in core (ACL-agnostic CAS — the media layer never
 * touches AccessResolver).
 *
 * Dependency direction is one-way: media requires core (for the Contracts\Cas\*
 * seam, the Blob/FileVersion models and Support\Config), core does NOT require
 * media at runtime — a host that wants the CAS file layer installs this package
 * and Laravel auto-discovers this provider, which binds the concrete engine.
 */
final class VaulterMediaServiceProvider extends PackageServiceProvider
{
    public function configurePackage(Package $package): void
    {
        $package
            ->name('vaulter-media')
            ->hasCommand(GcBlobsCommand::class)
            ->hasCommand(ReconcileBlobsCommand::class)
            ->hasCommand(RepairBlobScopesCommand::class);
    }

    public function packageRegistered(): void
    {
        // Own the CAS engine defaults WITHOUT changing the config path hosts use:
        // merge the swappable-class defaults under the existing `vaulter.cas` key.
        // Core no longer ships these three keys, so this merge is authoritative and
        // resolves the class strings Support\Config reads below.
        $this->mergeConfigFrom(__DIR__.'/../config/vaulter-cas.php', 'vaulter.cas');

        // CAS services (H.3) — swappable via config('vaulter.cas.*'). StorageDriver
        // and ContentStore are stateless wrappers (singletons); BlobStore is the
        // race-free lifecycle orchestrator. These bindings moved out of core with
        // the engine; core code resolves them through the Contracts\Cas\* seam.
        $this->app->singleton(StorageDriver::class, Config::storageDriverClass());
        $this->app->singleton(ContentStore::class, Config::contentStoreClass());
        $this->app->bind(BlobStore::class, Config::blobStoreClass());
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobCommandsTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * The CAS console commands wire the collector / reconciler to artisan (H.3): the
 * GC tombstones-then-sweeps and reconcile rebuilds ref_count and reports.
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
    config()->set('vaulter.gc.grace_hours', 1);
});

it('vaulter:gc-blobs прогоняет обе фазы', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('via command'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    // Already tombstoned past grace → the sweep phase removes it.
    $blob->forceFill(['status' => BlobStatus::Tombstoned, 'delete_after_at' => now()->subDay()])->save();

    $this->artisan('vaulter:gc-blobs')
        ->assertSuccessful();

    expect($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Deleted);
});

it('vaulter:gc-blobs --pending подметает упавший staging', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('half upload'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    // A crashed staging: pending, old, no edge.
    $blob->forceFill(['status' => BlobStatus::Pending, 'first_seen_at' => now()->subDay()])->save();

    $this->artisan('vaulter:gc-blobs', ['--pending' => true])
        ->assertSuccessful();

    expect($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Deleted);
});

it('vaulter:reconcile-blobs корректирует ref_count и сообщает', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('reconcile cmd'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $store->attach($version, $blob);

    $this->artisan('vaulter:reconcile-blobs')
        ->assertSuccessful();

    expect($blob->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(1);
});

it('vaulter:reconcile-blobs падает, когда есть dangling-строка', function (): void {
    $blob = app(BlobStore::class)->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('dangling cmd'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    Storage::disk('local')->delete($blob->storage_key);

    $this->artisan('vaulter:reconcile-blobs')
        ->assertFailed();
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobCommitAtomicityTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Cas\BlobLock;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StagedObject;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\FlysystemStorageDriver;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\Sha256ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Cas\BlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileVersionBlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Commit atomicity (H.3): if the transaction rolls back, no blob row is left
 * behind. The staged temp file is always discarded. (The physical object is only
 * promoted for a newly created row, so a roll back before promotion leaves the
 * disk clean too.)
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
});

it('rollback не оставляет висящей строки blob', function (): void {
    // A ContentStore whose promote() blows up mid-transaction forces a rollback
    // after the row INSERT — proving the INSERT is inside the tx and reverts.
    $exploding = new class implements ContentStore
    {
        public function __construct(private readonly ContentStore $inner = new Sha256ContentStore(new FlysystemStorageDriver)) {}

        public function stage(mixed $stream): StagedObject
        {
            return $this->inner->stage($stream);
        }

        public function promote(StagedObject $staged, string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
        {
            throw new RuntimeException('promote boom');
        }

        public function get(string $key, ?string $disk = null): mixed
        {
            return $this->inner->get($key, $disk);
        }

        public function exists(string $key, ?string $disk = null): bool
        {
            return false; // force the promote branch
        }

        public function delete(string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
        {
            $this->inner->delete($key, $disk);
        }

        public function keyFor(BlobScope $scope, string $sha256): string
        {
            return $this->inner->keyFor($scope, $sha256);
        }
    };

    $store = new BlobStore(
        content: $exploding,
        lock: app(BlobLock::class),
        blobs: app(BlobStoreRepository::class),
        edges: app(FileVersionBlobStoreRepository::class),
        database: app(VaulterDatabase::class),
    );

    expect(fn () => $store->commit(stringStream('boom bytes'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused')))
        ->toThrow(RuntimeException::class, 'promote boom');

    // The transaction rolled back: zero rows, zero objects.
    expect(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(0)
        ->and(Storage::disk('local')->allFiles())->toBe([]);
});

it('staged temp-файл удаляется после успешного коммита', function (): void {
    $before = glob(sys_get_temp_dir().'/vaulter_cas_*') ?: [];

    app(BlobStore::class)->commit(stringStream('temp cleanup'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    $after = glob(sys_get_temp_dir().'/vaulter_cas_*') ?: [];

    expect(count($after))->toBeLessThanOrEqual(count($before));
});

function stringStream(string $bytes)
{
    $stream = fopen('php://temp', 'r+b');
    fwrite($stream, $bytes);
    rewind($stream);

    return $stream;
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobCommitTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobReconciler;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * The content-addressed commit flow (H.3): stage → hash the staged file →
 * find-or-create by the per-scope dedup key → promote bytes only on a newly
 * created row. Server-recompute beats any client claim; dedup yields ONE row and
 * ONE physical object for identical bytes.
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
});

it('коммитит блоб: одна строка, объект на диске, статус committed', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('hello cas'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    expect($blob->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed)
        ->and($blob->sha256)->toBe(hash('sha256', 'hello cas'))
        ->and($blob->size_bytes)->toBe(9)
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1);

    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($blob->storage_key);
});

it('сервер пересчитывает sha256 — врущий клиент игнорируется', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    // The client could send any sha256 they like; commit() never takes it — the
    // hash is recomputed from the staged temp file. We prove it by checking the
    // stored hash matches the REAL bytes, not a lie.
    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('real bytes'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    expect($blob->sha256)->toBe(hash('sha256', 'real bytes'))
        ->and($blob->sha256)->not->toBe(hash('sha256', 'a lie'));
});

it('дедуп: одинаковые байты дважды → ОДНА строка, ОДИН объект, два ребра', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $first = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('same bytes'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $second = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('same bytes'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    expect($second->getKey())->toBe($first->getKey())
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1);

    // Two distinct versions reference the one blob — two edges, one physical object.
    $v1 = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion(versionNo: 1, name: 'a.bin');
    $v2 = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion(versionNo: 1, name: 'b.bin');
    $store->attach($v1, $first);
    $store->attach($v2, $second);

    expect(Storage::disk('local')->allFiles())->toHaveCount(1);

    // ref_count is NOT bumped synchronously (H.3) — it stays 0 until reconciliation.
    expect($first->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(0);

    // Reconciliation rebuilds ref_count to the edge total (2).
    app(BlobReconciler::class)->reconcile();
    expect($first->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(2);
});

it('повторный коммит идемпотентен и не пишет второй объект', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('idem'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('idem'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    expect(Storage::disk('local')->allFiles())->toHaveCount(1)
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1);
});

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobCrossScopeGcTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobGarbageCollector;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Cross-scope deletion safety (H.3 / H.6): under per-tenant dedup the SAME bytes
 * in two tenants are two rows at two scope-qualified keys. Sweeping tenant 1's
 * blob must never delete tenant 2's object — the scope segment in storage_key is
 * what guarantees it.
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
});

it('sweep одного scope не трогает байты другого scope с теми же байтами', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $gc = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);

    $a = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('cross-scope'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $b = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('cross-scope'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 2, 'unused'));

    // Two distinct physical objects despite identical bytes.
    expect($a->storage_key)->not->toBe($b->storage_key);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($a->storage_key);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($b->storage_key);

    // Tenant 1's blob becomes a sweep candidate; tenant 2's stays committed.
    $a->forceFill(['status' => BlobStatus::Tombstoned, 'delete_after_at' => now()->subMinute()])->save();

    $gc->sweep();

    // A's bytes gone, B's bytes intact — no cross-scope deletion.
    Storage::disk('local')->assertMissing($a->storage_key);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($b->storage_key);
    expect($b->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed);
});

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobDedupScopeBehaviourTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * The dedup scope (D18 / H.3) decides what shares one physical object: global
 * (sha256 alone), tenant (tenant_id + sha256) or storage (storage_id + sha256).
 * The scope-qualified storage_key keeps two scopes from ever colliding on one
 * object, so one scope's GC can never touch another's bytes.
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
});

it('global: одинаковые байты в разных тенантах → ОДИН блоб', function (): void {
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'global');
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $a = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('shared'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $b = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('shared'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 2, 'unused'));

    expect($b->getKey())->toBe($a->getKey())
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and($a->storage_key)->toStartWith('g/');
});

it('tenant: одинаковые байты в разных тенантах → ДВА блоба, два объекта', function (): void {
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $a = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('shared'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $b = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('shared'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 2, 'unused'));

    expect($b->getKey())->not->toBe($a->getKey())
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(2)
        ->and($a->sha256)->toBe($b->sha256)
        ->and($a->storage_key)->not->toBe($b->storage_key)
        ->and($a->storage_key)->toStartWith('t-1/')
        ->and($b->storage_key)->toStartWith('t-2/')
        ->and(Storage::disk('local')->allFiles())->toHaveCount(2);
});

it('storage: одинаковые байты в разных хранилищах → ДВА блоба', function (): void {
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'storage');
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $a = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('shared'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), null, (string) 10));
    $b = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('shared'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), null, (string) 20));

    expect($b->getKey())->not->toBe($a->getKey())
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(2)
        ->and($a->storage_key)->toStartWith('d-10/')
        ->and($b->storage_key)->toStartWith('d-20/')
        ->and($a->dedup_scope)->toBe('storage');
});

it('storage-scope ключит на storage_id, не на tenant_id', function (): void {
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'storage');
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    // Same storage, different tenants → still ONE blob (tenant is irrelevant in
    // storage scope).
    $a = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('shared'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, (string) 10));
    $b = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('shared'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 2, (string) 10));

    expect($b->getKey())->toBe($a->getKey())
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1);
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobFindOrCreateTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Cas\BlobLock;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Cas\BlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * find-or-create regressions (H.3): the row id is always real (the ON CONFLICT
 * RETURNING null-id gotcha is avoided), the INSERT-race loser re-SELECTs and
 * reuses, and a tombstoned match is resurrected — all inside one locked
 * transaction.
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
});

it('возвращает РЕАЛЬНЫЙ id (регрессия на null-id из ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING)', function (): void {
    $repo = app(BlobStoreRepository::class);
    $sha = hash('sha256', 'real id');

    $result = DB::transaction(fn (): array => $repo->findOrCreate(
        sha256: $sha,
        sizeBytes: 7,
        storageKey: "t1/{$sha[0]}{$sha[1]}/{$sha[2]}{$sha[3]}/{$sha}",
        disk: 'local',
        scope: BlobScope::tenant('1'),
        tenantId: '1',
        storageId: null,
    ));

    expect($result['created'])->toBeTrue()
        ->and($result['blob']->getKey())->not->toBeNull()
        ->and($result['blob']->exists)->toBeTrue()
        ->and($result['blob']->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed);
});

it('второй find-or-create тех же байт → created=false, тот же id', function (): void {
    $repo = app(BlobStoreRepository::class);
    $sha = hash('sha256', 'second hit');
    $key = "t1/{$sha[0]}{$sha[1]}/{$sha[2]}{$sha[3]}/{$sha}";

    $first = DB::transaction(fn (): array => $repo->findOrCreate(
        sha256: $sha, sizeBytes: 10, storageKey: $key, disk: 'local',
        scope: BlobScope::tenant('1'), tenantId: '1', storageId: null,
    ));

    $second = DB::transaction(fn (): array => $repo->findOrCreate(
        sha256: $sha, sizeBytes: 10, storageKey: $key, disk: 'local',
        scope: BlobScope::tenant('1'), tenantId: '1', storageId: null,
    ));

    expect($second['created'])->toBeFalse()
        ->and($second['blob']->getKey())->toBe($first['blob']->getKey())
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1);
});

it('find-or-create на tombstoned-строке воскрешает её', function (): void {
    $repo = app(BlobStoreRepository::class);
    $sha = hash('sha256', 'resurrect-me');
    $key = "t1/{$sha[0]}{$sha[1]}/{$sha[2]}{$sha[3]}/{$sha}";

    $created = DB::transaction(fn (): array => $repo->findOrCreate(
        sha256: $sha, sizeBytes: 5, storageKey: $key, disk: 'local',
        scope: BlobScope::tenant('1'), tenantId: '1', storageId: null,
    ));

    Blob::query()->whereKey($created['blob']->getKey())->update([
        'status' => BlobStatus::Tombstoned->value,
        'delete_after_at' => now()->addHour(),
    ]);

    $again = DB::transaction(fn (): array => $repo->findOrCreate(
        sha256: $sha, sizeBytes: 5, storageKey: $key, disk: 'local',
        scope: BlobScope::tenant('1'), tenantId: '1', storageId: null,
    ));

    expect($again['created'])->toBeFalse()
        ->and($again['blob']->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed)
        ->and($again['blob']->delete_after_at)->toBeNull();
});

it('BlobLock на sqlite: advisory-lock — no-op, single-writer сериализует', function (): void {
    $lock = app(BlobLock::class);

    // SQLite test connection: no advisory lock, no FOR UPDATE — the unique
    // constraint + single-writer transaction is the backbone here.
    expect($lock->isPostgres())->toBeFalse()
        ->and($lock->usesRowLock())->toBeFalse();

    // acquire() is a safe no-op on sqlite (does not throw); we assert it returns
    // without error by reaching the post-transaction expectation.
    $reached = false;

    DB::transaction(function () use ($lock, &$reached): void {
        $lock->acquire(scope: 't1', sha256: str_repeat('a', 64));
        $reached = true;
    });

    expect($reached)->toBeTrue();
})->skip(
    fn (): bool => DB::connection()->getDriverName() !== 'sqlite',
    'asserts the SQLite no-op lock premise; on PG/MySQL the lock is a real mutex.',
);

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobGarbageCollectorTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobGarbageCollector;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Two-phase, race-safe GC (H.3): mark unreferenced committed blobs as tombstoned
 * (grace deadline), then sweep tombstoned blobs past grace to a physical delete.
 * A referenced blob is never touched; a concurrent re-commit resurrects a
 * tombstone before the sweep can delete it.
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
});

it('фаза 1: помечает unreferenced committed-блоб tombstoned с grace-дедлайном', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $gc = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('garbage'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    // The mark scan keys on committed ∧ ref_count(cache)<=0 — exactly what
    // reconciliation leaves on a blob whose last edge was detached. No grace is
    // pre-armed; tombstone() sets delete_after_at when it marks.
    $blob->forceFill(['ref_count' => 0])->save();

    $result = $gc->mark(graceHours: 24);

    expect($result['tombstoned'])->toBe(1)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Tombstoned)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->delete_after_at)->not->toBeNull();
});

it('фаза 1: НЕ трогает блоб с живым ребром (liveness = ребро, не ref_count)', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $gc = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('referenced'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $store->attach($version, $blob);

    // ref_count cache is stale (0) but the EDGE exists — the blob enters the scan
    // on ref_count<=0, and GC must re-verify under the lock and skip.
    $blob->forceFill(['ref_count' => 0])->save();

    $result = $gc->mark(graceHours: 24);

    expect($result['tombstoned'])->toBe(0)
        ->and($result['skipped'])->toBe(1)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed);
});

it('фаза 2: sweep удаляет байты только у tombstoned past-grace, статус deleted', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $gc = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('sweep me'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $key = $blob->storage_key;
    $blob->forceFill([
        'status' => BlobStatus::Tombstoned,
        'delete_after_at' => now()->subMinute(),
    ])->save();

    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($key);

    $result = $gc->sweep();

    expect($result['swept'])->toBe(1)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Deleted)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->delete_after_at)->toBeNull();

    Storage::disk('local')->assertMissing($key);
});

it('фаза 2: НЕ удаляет tombstoned внутри grace-окна', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $gc = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('still in grace'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $blob->forceFill([
        'status' => BlobStatus::Tombstoned,
        'delete_after_at' => now()->addHour(),
    ])->save();

    $result = $gc->sweep();

    expect($result['swept'])->toBe(0)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Tombstoned);

    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($blob->storage_key);
});

it('воскрешение: коммит на tombstoned-блоб разтомбствляет его (status→committed)', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    // A blob got tombstoned (GC phase 1) — bytes still on disk during grace.
    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('phoenix'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $blob->forceFill([
        'status' => BlobStatus::Tombstoned,
        'delete_after_at' => now()->addHour(),
    ])->save();

    // A concurrent upload of the SAME bytes arrives. It must un-tombstone, not
    // create a second row (this is what makes online GC safe — H.3).
    $resurrected = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('phoenix'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    expect($resurrected->getKey())->toBe($blob->getKey())
        ->and($resurrected->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed)
        ->and($resurrected->delete_after_at)->toBeNull()
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1);
});

it('воскрешение перед sweep спасает байты: после resurrection sweep ничего не трогает', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $gc = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);

    // Tombstoned past grace — a sweep candidate.
    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('saved'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $blob->forceFill([
        'status' => BlobStatus::Tombstoned,
        'delete_after_at' => now()->subMinute(),
    ])->save();

    // Ordering we simulate (SQLite single-writer): the commit WINS first and
    // resurrects the blob...
    $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('saved'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    // ...so when the sweep then runs, it re-reads under the lock, sees committed,
    // and refuses to delete — the bytes survive.
    $result = $gc->sweep();

    expect($result['swept'])->toBe(0)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed);

    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($blob->storage_key);
});

it('идемпотентность: повторный sweep по deleted-блобу — no-op', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $gc = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('twice swept'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $blob->forceFill(['status' => BlobStatus::Tombstoned, 'delete_after_at' => now()->subMinute()])->save();

    $gc->sweep();
    $second = $gc->sweep();

    expect($second['swept'])->toBe(0)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Deleted);
});

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobGcBatchTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Database\Events\QueryExecuted;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobGarbageCollector;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersionBlob;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\Testing\FakeMetricsRecorder;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    $this->freezeSecond();
    $this->metrics = new FakeMetricsRecorder;
    $this->app->instance(MetricsRecorder::class, $this->metrics);
});

/** @return list<Blob> */
function r21SeedCandidates(int $count, BlobStatus $status = BlobStatus::Committed): array
{
    $blobs = [];

    for ($i = 0; $i < $count; $i++) {
        $blob = CasTestHelpers::seedBlobRow('r21 candidate '.$i, $status);

        if ($status !== BlobStatus::Committed) {
            $blob->forceFill([
                'delete_after_at' => now()->subHour(),
                'first_seen_at' => now()->subDays(2),
            ])->save();
        }

        $blobs[] = $blob;
    }

    return $blobs;
}

/** @return list<int|float> */
function r21MetricValues(FakeMetricsRecorder $metrics, string $name): array
{
    return array_values(array_map(
        static fn (array $emission): int|float => $emission['value'],
        array_filter($metrics->emissions(), static fn (array $emission): bool => $emission['name'] === $name),
    ));
}

it('caps mark batches and advances past live candidates without per-row liveness reads', function (): void {
    $blobs = r21SeedCandidates(2105);
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    // Stale ref_count pins candidates in the scan; both full batches contain
    // skipped rows, including the checkpoint boundary.
    foreach ([0, 999, 1000, 1999, 2104] as $index) {
        $store->attach($version, $blobs[$index]);
    }

    $reads = [];
    DB::listen(function (QueryExecuted $query) use (&$reads): void {
        if (str_starts_with(strtolower($query->sql), 'select')) {
            $reads[] = $query->sql;
        }
    });

    expect(app(BlobGarbageCollector::class)->mark(graceHours: 24))->toBe([
        'tombstoned' => 2100,
        'skipped' => 5,
    ])->and(count($reads))->toBeLessThan(20)
        ->and(r21MetricValues($this->metrics, 'vaulter_gc_batch_rows'))->toBe([1000, 1000, 105])
        ->and(r21MetricValues($this->metrics, 'vaulter_gc_rows_scanned_total'))->toBe([1000, 1000, 105]);

    expect(Blob::query()->where('status', BlobStatus::Tombstoned)->count())->toBe(2100)
        ->and($blobs[999]->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed)
        ->and($blobs[2000]->fresh()->delete_after_at->equalTo(now()->addHours(24)))->toBeTrue();
});

it('caps sweep and pending batches while skipped rows stay eligible', function (BlobStatus $status): void {
    $blobs = r21SeedCandidates(1005, $status);
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    app(BlobStore::class)->attach($version, $blobs[999]);

    $collector = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);
    $result = $status === BlobStatus::Pending ? $collector->sweepPending(graceHours: 24) : $collector->sweep();

    expect($result)->toBe(['swept' => 1004, 'skipped' => 1])
        ->and(r21MetricValues($this->metrics, 'vaulter_gc_batch_rows'))->toBe([1000, 5])
        ->and($blobs[999]->fresh()->status)->toBe($status)
        ->and($blobs[1004]->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Deleted);

    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($blobs[999]->storage_key);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertMissing($blobs[1004]->storage_key);

    if ($status === BlobStatus::Tombstoned) {
        expect(r21MetricValues($this->metrics, 'vaulter_gc_objects_deleted_total'))->toBe([999, 5]);
    }
})->with([BlobStatus::Tombstoned, BlobStatus::Pending]);

it('does not extend the current pass for candidates inserted after its high-water key', function (): void {
    r21SeedCandidates(1001);
    $inserted = null;
    Blob::updated(function (Blob $blob) use (&$inserted): void {
        if ($inserted === null && $blob->status === BlobStatus::Tombstoned) {
            $inserted = CasTestHelpers::seedBlobRow('inserted during mark', BlobStatus::Committed);
        }
    });

    $collector = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);
    expect($collector->mark(graceHours: 24))->toBe(['tombstoned' => 1001, 'skipped' => 0])
        ->and($inserted->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed)
        ->and(r21MetricValues($this->metrics, 'vaulter_gc_batch_rows'))->toBe([1000, 1])
        ->and($collector->mark(graceHours: 24))->toBe(['tombstoned' => 1, 'skipped' => 0]);
});

it('rechecks a later candidate whose live edge is added by an earlier model observer', function (): void {
    [$first, $later] = r21SeedCandidates(2);
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    Blob::updated(function (Blob $blob) use ($first, $later, $version, $store): void {
        if ($blob->is($first)) {
            $store->attach($version, $later);
        }
    });

    expect(app(BlobGarbageCollector::class)->mark(graceHours: 24))->toBe(['tombstoned' => 1, 'skipped' => 1])
        ->and($later->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($later->storage_key);
});

it('rechecks live edges and status after the cheap scan in the optimized mark path', function (): void {
    [$live, $changed, $orphan] = r21SeedCandidates(3);
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $injected = false;
    DB::listen(function (QueryExecuted $query) use (&$injected, $live, $changed, $version): void {
        if (!$injected && str_contains($query->sql, 'limit 1000')) {
            $injected = true;
            app(BlobStore::class)->attach($version, $live);
            $changed->forceFill(['status' => BlobStatus::Pending])->save();
        }
    });

    expect(app(BlobGarbageCollector::class)->mark(graceHours: 24))->toBe(['tombstoned' => 1, 'skipped' => 2])
        ->and($live->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed)
        ->and($changed->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Pending)
        ->and($orphan->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Tombstoned);
});

it('rechecks status liveness and grace for a materialized deletion batch', function (BlobStatus $status): void {
    [$live, $changed, $recent, $orphan] = r21SeedCandidates(4, $status);
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $injected = false;
    DB::listen(function (QueryExecuted $query) use (&$injected, $live, $changed, $recent, $version, $status): void {
        if (!$injected && str_contains($query->sql, 'limit 1000')) {
            $injected = true;
            app(BlobStore::class)->attach($version, $live);
            $changed->forceFill(['status' => BlobStatus::Committed])->save();
            $recent->forceFill($status === BlobStatus::Pending
                ? ['first_seen_at' => now()]
                : ['delete_after_at' => now()->addHour()])->save();
        }
    });

    $collector = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);
    $result = $status === BlobStatus::Pending ? $collector->sweepPending(graceHours: 24) : $collector->sweep();
    expect($result)->toBe(['swept' => 1, 'skipped' => 3]);

    foreach ([$live, $changed, $recent] as $blob) {
        Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($blob->storage_key);
    }
    Storage::disk('local')->assertMissing($orphan->storage_key);
})->with([BlobStatus::Tombstoned, BlobStatus::Pending]);

it('retains completed checkpoints when a later optimized mark batch rolls back', function (): void {
    $blobs = r21SeedCandidates(1005);
    $updates = 0;
    DB::listen(function (QueryExecuted $query) use (&$updates): void {
        if (str_starts_with(strtolower($query->sql), 'update') && str_contains($query->sql, Config::table('blobs'))) {
            if (++$updates === 1002) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Later batch failed');
            }
        }
    });

    expect(fn () => app(BlobGarbageCollector::class)->mark(graceHours: 24))->toThrow(RuntimeException::class, 'Later batch failed');
    expect(Blob::query()->where('status', BlobStatus::Tombstoned)->count())->toBe(1000)
        ->and($blobs[1000]->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed)
        ->and(r21MetricValues($this->metrics, 'vaulter_gc_batch_rows'))->toBe([1000])
        ->and(r21MetricValues($this->metrics, 'vaulter_gc_rows_scanned_total'))->toBe([1000]);
});

it('advances sparse string and native UUID keys with configured models', function (string $keyType): void {
    Schema::create('r21_string_blobs', function (Blueprint $table) use ($keyType): void {
        $table->{$keyType}('blob_key')->primary();
        $table->string('sha256');
        $table->string('dedup_scope');
        $table->string('scope_key');
        $table->string('status');
        $table->unsignedInteger('ref_count');
        $table->timestamp('delete_after_at')->nullable();
    });
    Schema::create('r21_string_edges', function (Blueprint $table) use ($keyType): void {
        $table->{$keyType}('blob_id');
    });
    $model = new class extends Blob
    {
        protected $primaryKey = 'blob_key';

        protected $keyType = 'string';

        public $incrementing = false;

        public function getTable(): string
        {
            return 'r21_string_blobs';
        }
    };
    $edgeModel = new class extends FileVersionBlob
    {
        public function getTable(): string
        {
            return 'r21_string_edges';
        }
    };
    config()->set('vaulter.models.blob', $model::class);
    config()->set('vaulter.models.file_version_blob', $edgeModel::class);
    $keyFor = static fn (int $index): string => $keyType === 'uuid'
        ? sprintf('00000000-0000-7000-8000-%012d', $index * 3)
        : sprintf('blob-%08d', $index * 3);

    for ($i = 1004; $i >= 0; $i--) {
        (new ($model::class))->forceFill([
            'blob_key' => $keyFor($i),
            'sha256' => hash('sha256', 'string blob '.$i),
            'dedup_scope' => 'global',
            'scope_key' => 'global',
            'status' => BlobStatus::Committed,
            'ref_count' => 0,
        ])->save();
    }

    (new ($edgeModel::class))->forceFill(['blob_id' => $keyFor(999)])->save();

    expect(app(BlobGarbageCollector::class)->mark(graceHours: 24))->toBe(['tombstoned' => 1004, 'skipped' => 1])
        ->and(r21MetricValues($this->metrics, 'vaulter_gc_batch_rows'))->toBe([1000, 5])
        ->and($model::query()->where('status', BlobStatus::Tombstoned)->count())->toBe(1004)
        ->and($model::query()->find($keyFor(999))->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed);
})->with(['string', 'uuid']);

it('uses the same PostgreSQL lock namespace for every batched mark candidate before liveness reads', function (): void {
    if (DB::connection()->getDriverName() !== 'pgsql') {
        $this->markTestSkipped('PostgreSQL advisory transaction locks require the package PG lane.');
    }

    $blobs = r21SeedCandidates(3);
    $queries = [];
    DB::listen(function (QueryExecuted $query) use (&$queries): void {
        $queries[] = $query->sql;
    });

    expect(app(BlobGarbageCollector::class)->mark(graceHours: 24)['tombstoned'])->toBe(3);
    $lockIndex = array_find_key($queries, static fn (string $sql): bool => str_contains($sql, 'pg_advisory_xact_lock'));
    $edgeIndex = array_find_key($queries, static fn (string $sql): bool => str_contains($sql, Config::table('file_version_blobs')));
    expect($lockIndex)->not->toBeNull()->and($lockIndex)->toBeLessThan($edgeIndex);

    config()->set('database.connections.r21_probe', config('database.connections.testing'));
    $probe = DB::connection('r21_probe');

    try {
        foreach ($blobs as $blob) {
            $key = 'cas:'.$blob->scope_key.':'.$blob->sha256;
            $acquired = $probe->selectOne('SELECT pg_try_advisory_lock(hashtext(?)) AS acquired', [$key])->acquired;

            if ($acquired) {
                $probe->selectOne('SELECT pg_advisory_unlock(hashtext(?))', [$key]);
            }

            expect($acquired)->toBeFalse();
        }
    } finally {
        DB::purge('r21_probe');
    }
});
