# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobGcPipelineTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobGarbageCollector;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobReconciler;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Сквозной GC-конвейер БЕЗ ручного стампинга delete_after_at (H.3). Раньше mark-фаза
 * сканировала по `delete_after_at < now`, который ни один путь коммита/детача не
 * взводит на committed-блобе, — значит реально осиротевший блоб не попадал в скан
 * и байты текли навсегда. Эти тесты гоняют настоящую цепочку
 * commit → attach → detach → reconcile → gc и доказывают, что осиротевший блоб
 * собирается БЕЗ единого forceFill в тесте.
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
});

it('реальный конвейер: detach→reconcile→mark томбствляет осиротевший committed-блоб (без ручного стампинга)', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $reconciler = app(BlobReconciler::class);
    $gc = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);

    // Коммитим блоб и навешиваем настоящее ребро — блоб живой.
    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('orphan-me'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $store->attach($version, $blob);

    // Снимаем единственное ребро: блоб осиротел, но ref_count-КЭШ ещё врёт.
    $store->detach($version, $blob);

    // Реконсиляция перестраивает кэш из числа рёбер → ref_count = 0.
    $reconciler->reconcile();
    expect($blob->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(0)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->delete_after_at)->toBeNull();

    // Mark обязан подобрать committed ∧ ref_count<=0 и затомбствить с grace-дедлайном —
    // НИКАКОГО ручного delete_after_at до этого не было.
    $result = $gc->mark(graceHours: 24);

    expect($result['tombstoned'])->toBe(1)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Tombstoned)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->delete_after_at)->not->toBeNull()
        ->and($blob->fresh()->delete_after_at->isFuture())->toBeTrue();
});

it('реальный конвейер: за время grace sweep не трогает свежий tombstone, после grace — удаляет байты', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $reconciler = app(BlobReconciler::class);
    $gc = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('full-pipeline'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $store->attach($version, $blob);
    $store->detach($version, $blob);

    $reconciler->reconcile();

    // Mark с маленьким (но >=1ч после флора) grace — байты ещё на диске, sweep ждёт.
    $gc->mark(graceHours: 1);
    $key = $blob->storage_key;
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($key);

    expect($gc->sweep()['swept'])->toBe(0)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Tombstoned);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($key);

    // Перематываем время за grace-окно — теперь sweep вправе физически удалить.
    $this->travel(2)->hours();

    $result = $gc->sweep();

    expect($result['swept'])->toBe(1)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Deleted)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->delete_after_at)->toBeNull();
    Storage::disk('local')->assertMissing($key);
});

it('реальный конвейер: всё ещё ссылающийся блоб mark пропускает (liveness = ребро)', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $reconciler = app(BlobReconciler::class);
    $gc = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('still-referenced'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $store->attach($version, $blob);

    // Реконсиляция выставит ref_count=1 — в скан кандидатов он не попадёт вовсе.
    $reconciler->reconcile();
    expect($blob->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(1);

    $result = $gc->mark(graceHours: 24);

    expect($result['tombstoned'])->toBe(0)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed);
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobOrphanPruneTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Symfony\Component\Console\Command\Command;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobReconciler;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Fixtures\Cas\PhantomListingContentStore;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * The DESTRUCTIVE orphan prune (master plan P4 #11 / H.3): physically deleting true
 * orphans under the per-(scope,sha256) lock. It is only safe on a driver whose lock
 * serialises a write-less critical section — PostgreSQL's advisory xact lock or
 * MySQL's FOR UPDATE — so on SQLite the prune is REFUSED (covered in
 * BlobOrphanSweepTest). These cases pin the actual deletion + the lock-then-recheck
 * concurrency guard + idempotency, and therefore run ONLY on pgsql.
 *
 * The project CI is sqlite (the base TestCase forces an in-memory sqlite connection),
 * so EVERY test here SKIPS unless the live connection is pgsql — they are ready for
 * the future pgsql job (master plan P4 #11 TODO). Run them with the suite pointed at
 * a Postgres connection to exercise the real delete path.
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
    Carbon::setTestNow('2026-06-30 12:00:00');
})->skip(
    fn (): bool => DB::connection()->getDriverName() !== 'pgsql',
    'orphan prune is PG/MySQL-only; needs the pgsql CI job (master plan P4 #11).',
);

// 11. Actual prune deletes the bytes.
it('prune на pgsql реально удаляет байты осиротевшего объекта', function (): void {
    $orphanKey = CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'pg prune me', scope: 't1', ageHours: 48);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: true);

    expect($report['pruneSupported'])->toBeTrue()
        ->and($report['pruned'])->toBe(1)
        ->and($report['orphans'])->toBe([$orphanKey]);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertMissing($orphanKey);
});

// 11b. Command-level: a prune that cleaned everything exits SUCCESS.
it('vaulter:reconcile-blobs --orphans --prune на pgsql очищает orphan и выходит SUCCESS', function (): void {
    $orphanKey = CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'pg cmd prune', scope: 't1', ageHours: 48);

    $this->artisan('vaulter:reconcile-blobs', ['--orphans' => true, '--prune' => true, '--orphan-grace' => 24])
        ->expectsOutputToContain('Orphan walk (pruned)')
        ->assertExitCode(Command::SUCCESS);

    Storage::disk('local')->assertMissing($orphanKey);
});

// 12. Concurrency / lock-then-recheck: a row that appears before the locked recheck
//     prevents the delete (the commit won the race for the content address).
it('lock-then-recheck на pgsql: строка, появившаяся до перепроверки под локом, спасает байты', function (): void {
    // A true parallel-connection interleave (commit racing the prune between the
    // cheap scan and the under-lock recheck) is impractical in a single-process
    // test. We pin the decisive invariant instead: the prune's under-lock probe
    // (findByStorageKey(lock: true)) is what gates the delete — so an owning row
    // present at recheck time means the bytes are NOT deleted.
    $orphanKey = CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'commit won', scope: 't1', ageHours: 48);

    // The "commit won" outcome: a committed row now owns this exact storage_key.
    // forceFill (P1.4/F-P1.5-04): Blob is fully guarded — bypassed here exactly
    // like BlobStoreRepository::insertCommitted() does.
    $winningBlob = new Blob;
    $winningBlob->forceFill([
        'tenant_id' => 1,
        'storage_id' => null,
        'hash_algo' => 'sha256',
        'sha256' => hash('sha256', 'commit won'),
        'size_bytes' => strlen('commit won'),
        'disk' => 'local',
        'storage_key' => $orphanKey,
        'status' => BlobStatus::Committed,
        'ref_count' => 0,
        'dedup_scope' => 'tenant',
        'scope_key' => 'tenant:1',
        'committed_at' => now(),
    ]);
    $winningBlob->save();

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: true);

    // Owned ⇒ never an orphan, never pruned; the committed bytes survive.
    expect($report['orphans'])->toBe([])
        ->and($report['pruned'])->toBe(0);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($orphanKey);
});

// 13. Missing object on prune is idempotent: an object listed + past grace at scan
//     time but GONE by the time the under-lock physical delete fires (a concurrent
//     remove) must NOT throw — the delete is a harmless no-op.
//
//     A true between-scan-and-delete vanish is not single-process-reproducible against
//     the real disk (listKeys is a snapshot generator), so we drive it with a fixture
//     that lists a PHANTOM key (old mtime, no bytes): detection classifies it as an
//     orphan, then the prune's delete no-ops over the absent object.
it('prune на pgsql идемпотентен: исчезнувший до удаления объект не бросает (phantom listing)', function (): void {
    $phantomKey = app(ContentStore::class)->keyFor(scope: BlobScope::fromSegment('t1'), sha256: hash('sha256', 'phantom'));

    app()->instance(ContentStore::class, new PhantomListingContentStore(
        phantomKey: $phantomKey,
        phantomMtime: now()->subHours(48)->getTimestamp(),
    ));

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: true);

    // No exception; the phantom is found as an orphan and its delete is a no-op.
    expect($report['orphans'])->toBe([$phantomKey])
        ->and($report['pruned'])->toBe(1);
});

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobOrphanSweepTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobReconciler;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Fixtures\Cas\NonListableContentStore;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Fixtures\Cas\NullMtimeContentStore;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Orphan-object disk-walk (master plan P4 #11 / H.3): the inverse of reconcile() —
 * BlobReconciler::sweepOrphans() walks the DISK for bytes that match the committed
 * CAS key layout "<scope>/ab/cd/<sha256>" but have NO blob row (a commit that
 * promoted bytes then rolled its row back, or leaked bytes), and — only on a driver
 * whose lock serialises a write-less critical section — physically prunes the true
 * orphans under the same per-(scope,sha256) lock + grace discipline the GC uses.
 *
 * These cases pin the SQLite-testable half: detection on every driver, the seven
 * round-trip guards that keep the sweep from EVER touching a non-canonical path,
 * tnull/snull scope, any-status ⇒ owned, the disk-agnostic ownership probe (the
 * data-loss fix), the orphan grace (fresh / aged / null-mtime), prune REFUSED on
 * sqlite, and the listing-unsupported refusal. The actual destructive prune is
 * PG/MySQL-only and lives in BlobOrphanPruneTest (skipped on sqlite).
 *
 * Time is frozen so the grace arithmetic (mtime < now − graceHours) is deterministic;
 * object age is set by touch()-ing the faked disk's real path.
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
    Carbon::setTestNow('2026-06-30 12:00:00');
});

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 1. Detection (report-only) — the happy path on every driver.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
it('обнаруживает настоящий orphan и пропускает закоммиченный объект (report-only)', function (): void {
    // A real committed blob (row + bytes) — must be SKIPPED as owned.
    $blob = app(BlobStore::class)->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('owned bytes'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    // A genuine orphan: valid canonical key, no row, aged past grace.
    $orphanKey = CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'orphan bytes', scope: 't1', ageHours: 48);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: false);

    expect($report['listingSupported'])->toBeTrue()
        ->and($report['orphans'])->toBe([$orphanKey])
        ->and($report['orphans'])->not->toContain($blob->storage_key)
        ->and($report['scanned'])->toBe(2)   // the committed object + the orphan
        ->and($report['skipped'])->toBe(1)   // the committed object
        ->and($report['pruned'])->toBe(0);
});

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 2. Round-trip guard — every non-canonical path is SKIPPED and survives.
//    Each spoof is its own assertion via a dataset (one scenario, many inputs).
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
it('round-trip guard: НЕ канонический путь пропускается и переживает обход', function (string $label, Closure $makePath): void {
    $sha = hash('sha256', "guard:{$label}");
    $path = $makePath($sha);

    Storage::disk('local')->put($path, "bytes:{$label}");
    // Age it well past grace so ONLY the path-guard (not the grace) can exclude it.
    CasTestHelpers::ageObject(key: $path, ageHours: 72);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: false);

    expect($report['orphans'])->toBe([])            // never classified as an orphan
        ->and($report['scanned'])->toBe(1)
        ->and($report['skipped'])->toBe(1);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($path);    // and never touched
})->with([
    // Staging temp object — the resumable/multipart landing zone, no scope prefix.
    'staging tmp/<sha>' => ['tmp', fn (string $sha): string => "tmp/{$sha}"],
    // A resumable-style part fragment hanging off a canonical-looking prefix.
    'resumable part' => ['part', fn (string $sha): string => 't1/'.substr($sha, 0, 2).'/'.substr($sha, 2, 2)."/{$sha}/part-1"],
    // A foreign file that has nothing to do with the CAS layout.
    'foreign file' => ['foreign', fn (): string => 'docs/readme.md'],
    // MIS-SHARDED: a real-looking key whose ab/cd shard is NOT this sha's shard, so
    // keyFor() re-derivation fails the round-trip even though the regex matches.
    'mis-sharded shard' => ['missharded', fn (string $sha): string => "global/00/00/{$sha}"],
    // UPPERCASE hex digest — the layout demands lowercase; the regex rejects it.
    'uppercase hex' => ['upper', fn (string $sha): string => 't1/'.substr($sha, 0, 2).'/'.substr($sha, 2, 2).'/'.strtoupper($sha)],
    // An extra path segment past the digest.
    'extra segment' => ['extra', fn (string $sha): string => 't1/'.substr($sha, 0, 2).'/'.substr($sha, 2, 2)."/{$sha}/extra"],
    // A bare 64-hex with no scope/shard structure at all.
    'bare 64-hex' => ['bare', fn (string $sha): string => $sha],
]);

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 3. tnull / snull scope — a null-tenant/storage orphan IS detected.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
it('tnull-scope: orphan с null-tenant (tenancy off) обнаруживается', function (): void {
    // The producer path: commit with NULL tenant under tenant scope emits a "tnull"
    // key segment — assert that's what BlobStore lays down, then orphan-detect it.
    $produced = app(BlobStore::class)->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('producer'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), null, 'unused'));
    expect($produced->storage_key)->toStartWith('g/');

    $orphanKey = CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'tnull orphan', scope: 'tnull', ageHours: 48);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: false);

    expect($orphanKey)->toStartWith('tnull/')
        ->and($report['orphans'])->toContain($orphanKey);
});

it('snull-scope: orphan с null-storage (storage scope, tenancy off) обнаруживается', function (): void {
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'storage');

    $orphanKey = CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'snull orphan', scope: 'snull', ageHours: 48);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: false);

    expect($orphanKey)->toStartWith('snull/')
        ->and($report['orphans'])->toContain($orphanKey);
});

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 4. Any-status row ⇒ owned (never an orphan), for every lifecycle status.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
it('любой статус строки делает объект owned (пропущен), а не orphan', function (BlobStatus $status): void {
    $blob = CasTestHelpers::seedBlobRow(bytes: "owned:{$status->value}", status: $status, disk: 'local');
    // Age the bytes past grace so ONLY the row-ownership (not the grace) can exclude.
    CasTestHelpers::ageObject(key: $blob->storage_key, ageHours: 72);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: false);

    expect($report['orphans'])->toBe([])
        ->and($report['skipped'])->toBe(1);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($blob->storage_key);
})->with([
    'pending' => [BlobStatus::Pending],
    'committed' => [BlobStatus::Committed],
    'tombstoned' => [BlobStatus::Tombstoned],
    'deleting' => [BlobStatus::Deleting],
    'deleted' => [BlobStatus::Deleted],
]);

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 5. Disk-agnostic ownership regression (review Finding A) — the data-loss fix.
//    Row records disk='other', bytes live on the scanned disk 'local': the row
//    probe must still see the object as OWNED (storage_key is globally unique).
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
it('owned кросс-диском: строка на disk=other, байты на local → НЕ orphan (storage_key глобально уникален)', function (): void {
    // Bytes on 'local' (the disk we will scan); the owning row records disk='other'.
    $blob = CasTestHelpers::seedBlobRow(
        bytes: 'cross-disk owned',
        status: BlobStatus::Committed,
        disk: 'other',
        bytesDisk: 'local',
    );
    CasTestHelpers::ageObject(key: $blob->storage_key, ageHours: 72);

    expect($blob->disk)->toBe('other');

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: false);

    // If the probe re-added the disk filter it would read this as row-less and
    // (under prune) delete LIVE bytes — this assertion is the guard against that.
    expect($report['orphans'])->toBe([])
        ->and($report['skipped'])->toBe(1);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($blob->storage_key);
});

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 6. Orphan grace — fresh skipped, aged detected, null-mtime skipped.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
it('grace: свежий row-less объект (mtime=now) пропускается', function (): void {
    // Fresh: no ageing — mtime is "now", inside any grace window.
    CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'fresh orphan', scope: 't1', ageHours: 0);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: false);

    expect($report['orphans'])->toBe([])
        ->and($report['skipped'])->toBe(1);
});

it('grace: тот же объект, состаренный за --orphan-grace, попадает в orphans', function (): void {
    $orphanKey = CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'aged orphan', scope: 't1', ageHours: 48);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: false);

    expect($report['orphans'])->toBe([$orphanKey]);
});

it('grace: null-mtime объект (возраст неизвестен) пропускается, не удаляется', function (): void {
    // The real store lays the bytes down (a valid canonical key, no row)...
    $orphanKey = CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'null mtime orphan', scope: 't1', ageHours: 48);

    // ...but the ACTIVE store reports a null lastModified for everything, so the
    // grace gate cannot establish the age ⇒ the object is skipped, never an orphan.
    app()->instance(ContentStore::class, new NullMtimeContentStore);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: false);

    expect($report['listingSupported'])->toBeTrue()  // it IS listable...
        ->and($report['scanned'])->toBe(1)           // ...the object was enumerated...
        ->and($report['orphans'])->toBe([])          // ...but age-unknown ⇒ skipped.
        ->and($report['skipped'])->toBe(1);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($orphanKey);
});

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 7. Prune REFUSED on sqlite — detection runs, deletion does not.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
it('prune отказан на sqlite: pruneSupported=false, байты живы, orphan всё ещё в отчёте', function (): void {
    expect(DB::connection()->getDriverName())->toBe('sqlite'); // the gate's premise

    $orphanKey = CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'survives prune', scope: 't1', ageHours: 48);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: true);

    expect($report['pruneSupported'])->toBeFalse()
        ->and($report['pruned'])->toBe(0)
        ->and($report['orphans'])->toBe([$orphanKey]);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($orphanKey);
})->skip(
    fn (): bool => DB::connection()->getDriverName() !== 'sqlite',
    'prune-refusal is the SQLite premise; PG/MySQL support prune (covered in BlobOrphanPruneTest).',
);

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 8. Listing unsupported — a non-listable store refuses the walk cleanly.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
it('listing не поддержан: не-listable store → listingSupported=false, scanned=0, ничего не удаляется', function (): void {
    // Bytes are a genuine, aged orphan — but the active store cannot enumerate.
    $orphanKey = CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'unwalkable orphan', scope: 't1', ageHours: 48);

    app()->instance(ContentStore::class, new NonListableContentStore);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(disk: 'local', graceHours: 24, prune: true);

    expect($report['listingSupported'])->toBeFalse()
        ->and($report['pruneSupported'])->toBeFalse()
        ->and($report['scanned'])->toBe(0)
        ->and($report['orphans'])->toBe([])
        ->and($report['pruned'])->toBe(0);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($orphanKey); // nothing deleted
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobReconcilerTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobReconciler;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Reconciliation (H.3): ref_count is a cache, rebuilt from the edge counts (the
 * liveness truth). It also flags dangling rows — a committed blob whose canonical
 * object is missing on disk.
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
});

it('пересчитывает ref_count из числа рёбер', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('counted'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    $v1 = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion(versionNo: 1, name: 'a');
    $v2 = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion(versionNo: 1, name: 'b');
    $store->attach($v1, $blob);
    $store->attach($v2, $blob);

    // Commit never bumps ref_count (H.3); the cache is stale at 0.
    expect($blob->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(0);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->reconcile();

    expect($blob->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(2)
        ->and($report['reconciled'])->toBe(1)
        ->and($report['changed'])->toBe(1)
        ->and($report['dangling'])->toBe([]);
});

it('сбрасывает ref_count к 0, когда все рёбра удалены', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('orphaned'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $store->attach($version, $blob);

    // Stale cache pretends it is referenced 5 times.
    $blob->forceFill(['ref_count' => 5])->save();
    $store->detach($version, $blob);

    app(BlobReconciler::class)->reconcile();

    expect($blob->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(0);
});

it('помечает dangling-строку: committed-блоб без объекта на диске', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobReconciler::class);

    $blob = app(BlobStore::class)->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('vanished'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    // Simulate a botched delete: the row stays committed but the object is gone.
    Storage::disk('local')->delete($blob->storage_key);

    $report = $store->reconcile();

    expect($report['dangling'])->toContain((string) $blob->getKey());
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobScopeKeyTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobGarbageCollector;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobReconciler;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

it('isolates real identifier strategies across dedup scopes and GC', function (string $strategy, string $dedup): void {
    config()->set('vaulter.ids.default', $strategy);
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', $dedup);
    $db = app(VaulterDatabase::class)->connection();
    expect($this->app->environment('testing'))->toBeTrue();
    expect($db->getDatabaseName() === ':memory:' || str_ends_with($db->getDatabaseName(), '_test'))->toBeTrue();
    $this->artisan('migrate:fresh', ['--force' => true])->assertSuccessful();
    Storage::fake('local');
    $aVersion = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $bVersion = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $aStorage = $aVersion->file->node->storage;
    $bStorage = $bVersion->file->node->storage;
    $aStorage->forceFill(['tenant_id' => (string) $aStorage->getKey(), 'owner_id' => (string) $aStorage->getKey()])->save();
    $bStorage->forceFill(['tenant_id' => (string) $bStorage->getKey(), 'owner_id' => (string) $bStorage->getKey()])->save();
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $a = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('same bytes'), BlobScope::forUpload($dedup, $aStorage->tenant_id, (string) $aStorage->getKey()));
    $b = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('same bytes'), BlobScope::forUpload($dedup, $bStorage->tenant_id, (string) $bStorage->getKey()));
    $store->attach($aVersion, $a);
    $store->attach($bVersion, $b);
    if ($dedup === 'global') {
        expect($a->getKey())->toBe($b->getKey());
    } else {
        expect($a->scope_key)->not->toBe($b->scope_key)
            ->and($a->getKey())->not->toBe($b->getKey())->and($a->storage_key)->not->toBe($b->storage_key);
    }
    expect(app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans('local', 0, false)['orphans'])->toBe([]);
    // Purge the first revision's edges, then sweep its past-grace tombstone.
    $aVersion->delete();
    if ($dedup !== 'global') {
        $a->forceFill(['status' => BlobStatus::Tombstoned, 'delete_after_at' => now()->subHour()])->save();
    }
    app(BlobGarbageCollector::class)->sweep();
    Storage::disk($b->disk)->assertExists($b->storage_key);
    expect(stream_get_contents($store->read($b)))->toBe('same bytes');
})->with([
    ['bigint', 'global'], ['bigint', 'tenant'], ['bigint', 'storage'],
    ['ulid', 'global'], ['ulid', 'tenant'], ['ulid', 'storage'],
    ['uuid7', 'global'], ['uuid7', 'tenant'], ['uuid7', 'storage'],
]);

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/CasTestHelpers.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas;

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage as StorageFacade;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Enums\Drive\NodeKind;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\Sha256ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\File;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersion;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Storage;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Shared builders for the CAS slice tests (M1b): a real Storage→Node→File→Version
 * chain so attach()/detach() can hang real edges off a committed blob, plus a
 * helper to turn a string into a stream the ContentStore can stage.
 */
final class CasTestHelpers
{
    /**
     * Build a committed FileVersion (with its File + Node + Storage) to attach a
     * blob edge to.
     */
    public static function makeVersion(int $versionNo = 1, string $name = 'a.bin'): FileVersion
    {
        // One storage per host owner (the M1c owner-unique index): mint a fresh
        // owner_id per call so repeated makeVersion()s in one test don't collide.
        static $owner = 0;
        $owner++;

        // forceFill (P1.4/F-P1.8-02, F-P1.5-04): Storage/Node/File/FileVersion all
        // now carry a closed $fillable/$guarded list — this trusted fixture
        // builder bypasses it exactly like the production store repositories do.
        $storage = new Storage;
        $storage->forceFill([
            'owner_type' => 'team',
            'owner_id' => $owner,
            'name' => 'Disk',
        ]);
        $storage->save();

        $node = new Node;
        $node->forceFill([
            'storage_id' => $storage->getKey(),
            'kind' => NodeKind::File,
            'name' => $name,
        ]);
        $node->save();

        $file = new File;
        $file->forceFill([
            'node_id' => $node->getKey(),
            'mime_type' => 'application/octet-stream',
            'size_bytes' => 0,
        ]);
        $file->save();

        $version = new FileVersion;
        $version->forceFill([
            'file_id' => $file->getKey(),
            'version_no' => $versionNo,
            'content_sha256' => str_repeat('0', 64),
            'size_bytes' => 0,
            'original_name' => $name,
            'status' => 'committed',
        ]);
        $version->save();

        return $version;
    }

    /**
     * An in-memory read stream over the given bytes.
     *
     * @return resource
     */
    public static function streamFor(string $bytes)
    {
        $stream = fopen('php://temp', 'r+b');
        fwrite($stream, $bytes);
        rewind($stream);

        return $stream;
    }

    /**
     * Seed a GENUINE orphan object on the disk: the bytes are written at their REAL
     * canonical CAS key (keyFor(scope, sha256(bytes))) so the round-trip guard
     * accepts them, with NO blob row, and aged $ageHours into the past via touch()
     * so the orphan grace can be exercised. Returns the storage key.
     *
     * Mirrors how the producer (BlobStore::commit) would lay the object down, minus
     * the row — exactly the "promoted bytes then rolled the row back / leaked bytes"
     * hazard the orphan walk hunts (H.3). The disk must already be a Storage::fake.
     */
    public static function seedOrphan(string $bytes, string $scope = 'tnull', int $ageHours = 48, string $disk = 'local'): string
    {
        $key = self::contentStore()->keyFor(scope: BlobScope::fromSegment($scope), sha256: hash('sha256', $bytes));

        StorageFacade::disk($disk)->put($key, $bytes);
        self::ageObject(key: $key, ageHours: $ageHours, disk: $disk);

        return $key;
    }

    /**
     * Write a canonical object at $key and back-date its mtime by $ageHours via
     * touch() on the faked disk's real path — the age lever the orphan grace reads
     * through ContentStore::lastModified(). A non-positive $ageHours leaves the mtime
     * at now (a FRESH object).
     */
    public static function ageObject(string $key, int $ageHours, string $disk = 'local'): void
    {
        if ($ageHours <= 0) {
            return;
        }

        touch(StorageFacade::disk($disk)->path($key), now()->subHours($ageHours)->getTimestamp());
    }

    /**
     * Create a blob row pinned to an EXACT storage_key + disk and forced to a given
     * lifecycle status, with the matching bytes on disk. Used to prove that an object
     * owned by a row of ANY status is skipped (never an orphan), and that ownership is
     * disk-agnostic (the row's $disk may differ from the scanned disk).
     *
     * The bytes are written on $bytesDisk (defaults to the row's $disk) so the object
     * physically exists for the walk to find; pass a different $bytesDisk to model the
     * cross-disk-ownership regression (row on one disk, bytes on another).
     */
    public static function seedBlobRow(
        string $bytes,
        BlobStatus $status,
        string $disk = 'local',
        ?string $bytesDisk = null,
        string $scope = 'tnull',
    ): Blob {
        $sha256 = hash('sha256', $bytes);
        $storageKey = self::contentStore()->keyFor(scope: BlobScope::fromSegment($scope), sha256: $sha256);

        StorageFacade::disk($bytesDisk ?? $disk)->put($storageKey, $bytes);

        // forceFill (P1.4/F-P1.5-04): Blob is fully guarded — bypassed here
        // exactly like BlobStoreRepository::insertCommitted() does.
        $blob = new Blob;
        $blob->forceFill([
            'tenant_id' => null,
            'storage_id' => null,
            'hash_algo' => 'sha256',
            'sha256' => $sha256,
            'size_bytes' => strlen($bytes),
            'disk' => $disk,
            'storage_key' => $storageKey,
            'status' => $status,
            'ref_count' => 0,
            'dedup_scope' => BlobScope::fromSegment($scope)->dedupScope(),
            'scope_key' => BlobScope::fromSegment($scope)->key(),
            'committed_at' => $status === BlobStatus::Committed ? now() : null,
        ]);
        $blob->save();

        return $blob;
    }

    private static function contentStore(): ContentStore
    {
        return app(Sha256ContentStore::class);
    }
}

```

function r18PgAdvisoryLockIsHeld(string $scope, string $sha256): bool
{
    config()->set('database.connections.r18_lock_probe', config('database.connections.pgsql'));
    DB::purge('r18_lock_probe');
    $connection = DB::connection('r18_lock_probe');
    $lockKey = 'cas:'.$scope.':'.$sha256;
    $acquired = (bool) $connection->selectOne(
        'SELECT pg_try_advisory_lock(hashtext(?)) AS acquired',
        [$lockKey],
    )->acquired;

    if ($acquired) {
        $connection->selectOne('SELECT pg_advisory_unlock(hashtext(?))', [$lockKey]);
    }

    DB::disconnect('r18_lock_probe');

    return !$acquired;
}

        ->and($emptyMetrics->emissions()[0]['name'])->toBe('vaulter_gc_duration_seconds')
        ->and($emptyMetrics->emissions()[0]['labels'])->toBe(['phase' => 'mark']);

    $blob = app(BlobStoreContract::class)->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('mark metric one'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

it('observes a successful local promotion result and an exact random temp suffix', function (): void {
    $tempKey = null;
    $filesystem = Mockery::mock(Filesystem::class);
    $filesystem->shouldReceive('writeStream')->once()->andReturnUsing(function (string $key) use (&$tempKey): bool {
        $tempKey = $key;

        return true;
    });
    $filesystem->shouldReceive('move')->once()->withArgs(function (string $from, string $to) use (&$tempKey): bool {
        return $from === $tempKey && $to === 'scope/final';
    })->andReturn(true);
    Storage::set('local', $filesystem);

    $result = (new FlysystemStorageDriver)->put(
        'scope/final',
        CasTestHelpers::streamFor('promote success'),
        'local',
    );

    expect($result)->toBeTrue()
        ->and($tempKey)->toMatch('/^scope\/final\.tmp-[0-9a-f]{16}$/');
});

it('returns false and reclaims the temp when the staged write fails', function (): void {
    $tempKey = null;
    $filesystem = Mockery::mock(Filesystem::class);
    $filesystem->shouldReceive('writeStream')->once()->andReturnUsing(function (string $key) use (&$tempKey): bool {
        $tempKey = $key;

        return false;
    });
    $filesystem->shouldReceive('exists')->once()->andReturn(true);
    $filesystem->shouldReceive('delete')->once()->andReturn(true);
    $filesystem->shouldNotReceive('move');
    Storage::set('local', $filesystem);

    expect((new FlysystemStorageDriver)->put(
        'scope/final',
        CasTestHelpers::streamFor('failed stage'),
        'local',
    ))->toBeFalse();
});

it('moves a completed temp for both conditional fallthrough cases', function (bool $ifNoneMatch, bool $canonicalExists): void {
    $tempKey = null;
    $filesystem = Mockery::mock(Filesystem::class);
    $filesystem->shouldReceive('exists')->andReturnUsing(function (string $key) use (&$tempKey, $canonicalExists): bool {
        if ($key === 'scope/final') {
            return $canonicalExists;
        }

        return $key === $tempKey;
    });
    $filesystem->shouldReceive('writeStream')->once()->andReturnUsing(function (string $key) use (&$tempKey): bool {
        $tempKey = $key;

        return true;
    });
    $filesystem->shouldReceive('move')->once()->withArgs(function (string $from, string $to) use (&$tempKey): bool {
        return $from === $tempKey && $to === 'scope/final';
    })->andReturn(true);
    Storage::set('local', $filesystem);

    expect((new FlysystemStorageDriver)->put(
        'scope/final',
        CasTestHelpers::streamFor('conditional fallthrough'),
        'local',
        $ifNoneMatch,
    ))->toBeTrue();
})->with([
    'overwrite may replace an existing key' => [false, true],
    'conditional create continues when the key is absent' => [true, false],
]);

it('returns the exact result of an atomic object-store write', function (bool $written): void {
    $filesystem = Mockery::mock(FilesystemAdapter::class);
    $filesystem->shouldReceive('providesTemporaryUrls')->once()->andReturn(true);
    $filesystem->shouldReceive('writeStream')->once()->andReturn($written);
    $filesystem->shouldNotReceive('move', 'delete');
    Storage::set('object-store', $filesystem);

    expect((new FlysystemStorageDriver)->put(
        'scope/final',
        CasTestHelpers::streamFor('atomic object store'),
        'object-store',
    ))->toBe($written);
})->with([
    'successful atomic write' => true,
    'failed atomic write' => false,
]);

it('closes the promoted staged stream even when the driver owns no reference to it', function (): void {
    $handle = CasTestHelpers::streamFor('close me');
    $staged = new class($handle) implements StagedObject
    {
        public function __construct(public mixed $handle) {}

        public function path(): string
        {
            return 'memory';
        }

        public function sha256(): string
        {
            return hash('sha256', 'close me');
        }

        public function sizeBytes(): int
        {
            return strlen('close me');
        }

        public function readStream(): mixed
        {
            return $this->handle;
        }

        public function discard(): void {}
    };
    $driver = Mockery::mock(StorageDriver::class);
    $driver->shouldReceive('exists')->once()->andReturn(false);
    $driver->shouldReceive('put')->once()->andReturn(true);

    (new Sha256ContentStore($driver))->promote($staged, 'key');

    expect(is_resource($staged->handle))->toBeFalse();
});

it('passes an empty checksum for an invalid staged digest', function (): void {
    $handle = CasTestHelpers::streamFor('invalid staged digest');
    $staged = new class($handle) implements StagedObject
    {
        public function __construct(public mixed $handle) {}

        public function path(): string
        {
            return 'memory';
        }

        public function sha256(): string
        {
            return 'invalid';
        }

        public function sizeBytes(): int
        {
            return 21;
        }

        public function readStream(): mixed
        {
            return $this->handle;
        }

        public function discard(): void {}
    };
    $checksum = null;
    $driver = Mockery::mock(StorageDriver::class);
    $driver->shouldReceive('exists')->once()->andReturn(false);
    $driver->shouldReceive('put')->once()->withArgs(function (
        string $key,
        mixed $stream,
        ?string $disk,
        bool $ifNoneMatch,
        array $options,
    ) use (&$checksum): bool {
        $checksum = $options['ChecksumSHA256'] ?? null;

        return $key === 'key' && is_resource($stream) && $disk === null && $ifNoneMatch;
    })->andReturn(true);

    set_error_handler(static fn (): bool => true);

    try {
        (new Sha256ContentStore($driver))->promote($staged, 'key');
    } finally {
        restore_error_handler();
    }

    expect($checksum)->toBe('')
        ->and(is_resource($staged->handle))->toBeFalse();
});

it('throws when a staged temp path disappears before it is reopened', function (): void {
    $staged = new StagedTempObject(
        path: sys_get_temp_dir().'/missing-r18-staged-object',
        sha256: str_repeat('0', 64),
        sizeBytes: 0,
    );

    expect(fn () => $staged->readStream())
        ->toThrow(RuntimeException::class, 'Unable to reopen staged temp file');
});

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

        ->toContain('Orphan objects (bytes with no blob row): '.$first.', '.$second);

    $exit = Artisan::call('vaulter:reconcile-blobs', [
        '--orphans' => true,
        '--prune' => true,

    expect($exit)->toBe(Command::FAILURE)

        ->toContain(

            'on sqlite use report-only or run on the production DB.',
        )
        ->toContain('Orphan objects (bytes with no blob row): '.$orphan);
})->skip(
    fn (): bool => DB::connection()->getDriverName() !== 'sqlite',
    'The refusal branch is specific to SQLite.',
);

        ->not->toContain('Orphan walk (report-only)')
        ->not->toContain('Orphan pruning needs PostgreSQL/MySQL');
});

    expect($exit)->toBe(Command::SUCCESS)
        ->and(Artisan::output())->toContain('--prune has no effect without --orphans; nothing pruned.');
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/MutationContractsTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Cas\BlobLock;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore as BlobStoreContract;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ListableStorageDriver;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StorageDriver;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobGarbageCollector;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobReconciler;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\FlysystemStorageDriver;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\Sha256ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.storage.default_disk', 'local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
    Carbon::setTestNow('2026-09-25 12:00:00');
});

it('stages the complete seekable stream and exposes its exact digest and size', function (): void {
    $stream = CasTestHelpers::streamFor('complete staged bytes');
    fread($stream, 8);

    $staged = app(Sha256ContentStore::class)->stage($stream);

    expect($staged->sha256())->toBe(hash('sha256', 'complete staged bytes'))
        ->and($staged->sizeBytes())->toBe(strlen('complete staged bytes'))
        ->and($staged->path())->toBeFile();

    $read = $staged->readStream();
    expect(stream_get_contents($read))->toBe('complete staged bytes');
    fclose($read);

    $path = $staged->path();
    $staged->discard();
    $staged->discard();

    expect($path)->not->toBeFile()
        ->and(fn () => $staged->readStream())
        ->toThrow(RuntimeException::class, 'Cannot read a discarded staged object.');
});

it('builds the canonical key and promotes the exact checksum and bytes', function (): void {
    $bytes = 'checksum contract';
    $sha256 = hash('sha256', $bytes);
    $stream = CasTestHelpers::streamFor($bytes);
    $driver = Mockery::mock(StorageDriver::class);
    $store = new Sha256ContentStore($driver);
    $staged = $store->stage($stream);
    $key = $store->keyFor(BlobScope::legacy('t17'), $sha256);

    expect($key)->toBe('t17/'.substr($sha256, 0, 2).'/'.substr($sha256, 2, 2).'/'.$sha256);

    $driver->shouldReceive('exists')->once()->with($key, 'archive')->andReturn(false);
    $driver->shouldReceive('put')->once()->withArgs(
        function (string $actualKey, $actualStream, ?string $disk, bool $ifNoneMatch, array $options) use ($bytes, $key, $sha256): bool {
            return $actualKey === $key
                && is_resource($actualStream)
                && stream_get_contents($actualStream) === $bytes
                && $disk === 'archive'
                && $ifNoneMatch
                && $options === ['ChecksumSHA256' => base64_encode(hex2bin($sha256))];
        },
    )->andReturn(true);

    $store->promote($staged, $key, 'archive');
    $staged->discard();
});

it('does not promote bytes that already exist', function (): void {
    $driver = Mockery::mock(StorageDriver::class);
    $store = new Sha256ContentStore($driver);
    $staged = $store->stage(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('already promoted'));

    $driver->shouldReceive('exists')->once()->with('existing-key', null)->andReturn(true);
    $driver->shouldNotReceive('put');

    $store->promote($staged, 'existing-key');
    $staged->discard();
});

it('delegates object age and listing only to listable drivers', function (): void {
    $listable = Mockery::mock(StorageDriver::class, ListableStorageDriver::class);
    $listable->shouldReceive('lastModified')->once()->with('one', 'archive')->andReturn(1234);
    $listable->shouldReceive('listContents')->once()->with('archive')->andReturn(['one', 'two']);
    $store = new Sha256ContentStore($listable);

    expect($store->lastModified('one', 'archive'))->toBe(1234)
        ->and(iterator_to_array($store->listKeys('archive')))->toBe(['one', 'two']);

    $plain = Mockery::mock(StorageDriver::class);
    $plain->shouldNotReceive('lastModified', 'listContents');
    $plainStore = new Sha256ContentStore($plain);

    expect($plainStore->lastModified('one'))->toBeNull()
        ->and(iterator_to_array($plainStore->listKeys()))->toBe([]);
});

it('round-trips Flysystem bytes, metadata, listing and idempotent deletion', function (): void {
    $driver = new FlysystemStorageDriver;
    $stream = CasTestHelpers::streamFor('flysystem bytes');

    expect($driver->put('aa/one.bin', $stream, 'local'))->toBeTrue()
        ->and($driver->exists('aa/one.bin', 'local'))->toBeTrue()
        ->and($driver->lastModified('aa/one.bin', 'local'))->toBeInt()
        ->and(iterator_to_array($driver->listContents('local')))->toBe(['aa/one.bin'])
        ->and($driver->temporaryUrl('aa/one.bin', now()->addMinute(), 'local'))->toContain('/aa/one.bin');

    $read = $driver->get('aa/one.bin', 'local');
    expect($read)->toBeResource()
        ->and(stream_get_contents($read))->toBe('flysystem bytes');
    fclose($read);

    $driver->delete('aa/one.bin', 'local');
    $driver->delete('aa/one.bin', 'local');

    expect($driver->exists('aa/one.bin', 'local'))->toBeFalse()
        ->and($driver->get('aa/one.bin', 'local'))->toBeNull()
        ->and($driver->lastModified('aa/one.bin', 'local'))->toBeNull();
});

it('preserves an existing object for a conditional Flysystem put', function (): void {
    Storage::disk('local')->put('same-key', 'winner bytes');
    $driver = new FlysystemStorageDriver;

    $result = $driver->put(
        key: 'same-key',
        stream: CasTestHelpers::streamFor('loser bytes'),
        disk: 'local',
        ifNoneMatch: true,
    );

    expect($result)->toBeFalse()
        ->and(Storage::disk('local')->get('same-key'))->toBe('winner bytes');
});

it('re-promotes a deduplicated blob when its canonical object disappeared', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStoreContract::class);
    $first = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('restore missing bytes'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 17, 'unused'));
    Storage::disk('local')->delete($first->storage_key);

    $second = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('restore missing bytes'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 17, 'unused'));
    $read = $store->read($second);

    expect($second->getKey())->toBe($first->getKey())
        ->and($read)->toBeResource()
        ->and(stream_get_contents($read))->toBe('restore missing bytes');
    fclose($read);
});

it('keeps exact mark, sweep and pending totals across multiple candidates', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStoreContract::class);
    $collector = app(BlobGarbageCollector::class);

    $markOne = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('mark one'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $markTwo = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('mark two'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $referenced = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('referenced mark'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $store->attach(CasTestHelpers::makeVersion(name: 'mark-reference'), $referenced);

    expect($collector->mark(graceHours: 24))->toBe(['tombstoned' => 2, 'skipped' => 1])
        ->and($markOne->fresh()->delete_after_at?->equalTo(now()->addHours(24)))->toBeTrue()
        ->and($markTwo->fresh()->delete_after_at?->equalTo(now()->addHours(24)))->toBeTrue();

    foreach ([$markOne, $markTwo] as $blob) {
        $blob->forceFill(['delete_after_at' => now()->subMinute()])->save();
    }
    $referenced->forceFill([
        'status' => BlobStatus::Tombstoned,
        'delete_after_at' => now()->subMinute(),
    ])->save();

    expect($collector->sweep())->toBe(['swept' => 2, 'skipped' => 1]);

    $pendingOne = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('pending one'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $pendingTwo = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('pending two'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $pendingLive = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('pending live'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    foreach ([$pendingOne, $pendingTwo, $pendingLive] as $blob) {
        $blob->forceFill([
            'status' => BlobStatus::Pending,
            'first_seen_at' => now()->subHours(3),
        ])->save();
    }
    $store->attach(CasTestHelpers::makeVersion(name: 'pending-reference'), $pendingLive);

    expect($collector->sweepPending(graceHours: 2))->toBe(['swept' => 2, 'skipped' => 1]);
});

it('reconciles every blob and preserves all dangling identities', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStoreContract::class);
    $counted = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('reconcile counted'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $zeroed = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('reconcile zeroed'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $dangling = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('reconcile dangling'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    $store->attach(CasTestHelpers::makeVersion(name: 'reconcile-a'), $counted);
    $store->attach(CasTestHelpers::makeVersion(name: 'reconcile-b'), $counted);
    $zeroed->forceFill(['ref_count' => 9])->save();
    Storage::disk('local')->delete($dangling->storage_key);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->reconcile();

    expect($report)->toBe([
        'reconciled' => 3,
        'changed' => 2,
        'dangling' => [(string) $dangling->getKey()],
    ])->and($counted->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(2)
        ->and($zeroed->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(0);
});

it('continues an orphan walk after every skipped object', function (): void {
    Storage::disk('local')->put('foreign/readme.txt', 'foreign');
    $owned = CasTestHelpers::seedBlobRow('owned bytes', BlobStatus::Committed);
    CasTestHelpers::ageObject($owned->storage_key, 48);
    CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'fresh candidate', scope: 't1', ageHours: 0);
    $firstOrphan = CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'old candidate one', scope: 't1', ageHours: 48);
    $secondOrphan = CasTestHelpers::seedOrphan(bytes: 'old candidate two', scope: 't1', ageHours: 48);

    $report = app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans(
        disk: 'local',
        graceHours: 24,
        prune: false,
    );

    $orphans = $report['orphans'];
    unset($report['orphans']);

    expect($report)->toBe([
        'scanned' => 5,
        'pruned' => 0,
        'skipped' => 3,
        'listingSupported' => true,
        'pruneSupported' => app(BlobLock::class)->serializesCriticalSection(),
    ])->and($orphans)->toEqualCanonicalizing([$firstOrphan, $secondOrphan]);
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/RepairBlobScopesCommandTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Fixtures\Cas\FailingPromoteContentStore;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersionBlob;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    if (app(VaulterDatabase::class)->driver() !== 'pgsql') {
        $this->markTestSkipped('Scope repair acceptance requires PostgreSQL.');
    }
    Storage::fake('local');
});

it('requires exactly one repair mode', function (): void {
    $this->artisan('vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes')->assertExitCode(2);
    $this->artisan('vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes', ['--dry-run' => true, '--execute' => true])->assertExitCode(2);
});

it('splits mixed storage edges without losing bytes and resumes a partial failure', function (bool $fail): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $old = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('legacy shared'), BlobScope::legacy('snull'));
    $a = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $b = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    foreach ([$a, $b] as $version) {
        $version->forceFill(['blob_id' => $old->getKey()])->save();
        $store->attach($version, $old);
    }
    $old->forceFill(['ref_count' => 2])->save();
    $this->artisan('vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes', ['--dry-run' => true])->expectsOutputToContain('move')->assertSuccessful();
    expect(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1)->and(FileVersionBlob::query()->where('blob_id', $old->getKey())->count())->toBe(2);
    $content = app(ContentStore::class);
    if ($fail) {
        app()->instance(ContentStore::class, new FailingPromoteContentStore($content, 2));
        $this->artisan('vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes', ['--execute' => true])->assertExitCode(1);
        expect(FileVersionBlob::query()->where('blob_id', $old->getKey())->count())->toBe(1)
            ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(2);
        app()->instance(ContentStore::class, $content);
    }
    $this->artisan('vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes', ['--execute' => true])->assertSuccessful();
    foreach ([$a, $b] as $version) {
        $blob = Blob::query()->where('scope_key', 'drive:'.$version->file->node->storage->getKey())->sole();
        expect($version->fresh()->blob_id)->toBe($blob->getKey())
            ->and(FileVersionBlob::query()->where('file_version_id', $version->getKey())->sole()->blob_id)->toBe($blob->getKey())
            ->and($blob->ref_count)->toBe(1)->and(stream_get_contents($store->read($blob)))->toBe('legacy shared');
    }
    expect($old->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(0)->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(3);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($old->storage_key);
    $this->artisan('vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes', ['--execute' => true])->expectsOutputToContain('Repaired: 0')->assertSuccessful();
    expect(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(3);
    $entries = array_map(static fn ($line): array => json_decode($line, true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR), file(storage_path('logs/vaulter-repair-scopes.log'), FILE_IGNORE_NEW_LINES));
    expect(array_filter($entries, static fn ($entry): bool => $entry['action'] === 'move' && $entry['edges'] === 1))->not->toBe([]);
})->with([false, true]);

it('uses global for a host without tenants and reports unreferenced and corrupt bytes', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $old = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('host legacy'), BlobScope::legacy('tnull'));
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $store->attach($version, $old);
    $this->artisan('vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes', ['--execute' => true])->assertSuccessful();
    expect(Blob::query()->where('scope_key', 'global')->count())->toBe(1);
    $this->artisan('vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes', ['--dry-run' => true])->expectsOutputToContain('unreferenced')->assertSuccessful();
    $bad = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('bad legacy'), BlobScope::legacy('snull'));
    $other = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $store->attach($other, $bad);
    Storage::disk('local')->put($bad->storage_key, 'tampered');
    $this->artisan('vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes', ['--dry-run' => true])->expectsOutputToContain('hash_mismatch')->assertSuccessful();
    $this->artisan('vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes', ['--execute' => true])->expectsOutputToContain('hash_mismatch')->assertSuccessful();
    expect(FileVersionBlob::query()->where('blob_id', $bad->getKey())->count())->toBe(1);
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/ScopeKeyReconcileTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobGarbageCollector;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobReconciler;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Fixtures\Cas\FailingPromoteContentStore;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Tests\Concurrency\ForkRace;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    ForkRace::skipUnlessSupported($this);
    config()->set('vaulter.ids.default', 'ulid');
    $this->artisan('migrate:fresh', ['--force' => true])->assertSuccessful();
    Storage::fake('local');
});

it('collects a post-promote SQL rollback only after grace under the lossless lock', function (): void {
    $content = app(ContentStore::class);
    app()->instance(ContentStore::class, new FailingPromoteContentStore($content, after: true));
    $scope = BlobScope::tenant('019a0000-1234-7000-8000-123456789abc');
    $bytes = 'promoted then rollback';
    expect(fn () => app(BlobStore::class)->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor($bytes), $scope))->toThrow(RuntimeException::class);
    expect(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(0);
    app()->instance(ContentStore::class, $content);
    $key = $content->keyFor($scope, hash('sha256', $bytes));
    expect(app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans('local', 24, true)['pruned'])->toBe(0);
    CasTestHelpers::ageObject($key, 48);
    $queries = [];
    app(VaulterDatabase::class)->connection()->listen(function ($query) use (&$queries): void {
        $queries[] = $query->bindings;
    });
    expect(app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans('local', 24, true)['pruned'])->toBe(1);
    expect($queries)->toContain(['cas:'.$scope->key().':'.hash('sha256', $bytes)]);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertMissing($key);
});

it('serializes GC against a writer of the same scope and keeps reachable bytes', function (): void {
    $scope = BlobScope::drive('01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV');
    $bytes = 'race bytes';
    $blob = app(BlobStore::class)->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor($bytes), $scope);
    $blob->forceFill(['status' => BlobStatus::Tombstoned, 'delete_after_at' => now()->subDay()])->save();
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $outcomes = ForkRace::run([
        fn (): array => app(BlobGarbageCollector::class)->sweep(),
        function () use ($scope, $bytes, $version): string {
            return app(VaulterDatabase::class)->transaction(function () use ($scope, $bytes, $version): string {
                $store = app(BlobStore::class);
                $fresh = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor($bytes), $scope);
                $store->attach($version, $fresh);

                return (string) $fresh->getKey();
            });
        },
    ]);
    foreach ($outcomes as $outcome) {
        expect($outcome->ok)->toBeTrue($outcome->message ?? '');
    }
    expect($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($blob->storage_key);
    expect(stream_get_contents(app(BlobStore::class)->read($blob)))->toBe($bytes);
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/SweepPendingSerializableTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Database\QueryException;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Cas\BlobLock;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StagedObject;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobGarbageCollector;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Cas\BlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileVersionBlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * sweepPending must run its critical section through the SERIALIZABLE + retry-on-40001
 * wrapper on PostgreSQL, symmetric with sweep() (F-P1.5-02). Without the wrapper a
 * transient serialization failure (SQLSTATE 40001) propagates as a QueryException and
 * aborts the whole GC pass instead of being retried. Real PG only — the retry path is
 * PG-specific (the FOR-UPDATE/single-writer path serialises elsewhere).
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    if (DB::connection()->getDriverName() !== 'pgsql') {
        $this->markTestSkipped('The 40001 serialization-retry path is PostgreSQL-specific.');
    }

    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
});

it('sweepPending ретраит транзиентный serialization-failure 40001 вместо проброса, F-P1.5-02', function (): void {
    // A pending blob past the grace window with no live edge — a sweepPending target.
    $blob = CasTestHelpers::seedBlobRow(bytes: 'crashed staging bytes', status: BlobStatus::Pending);
    $blob->forceFill(['first_seen_at' => now()->subDay()])->save();
    $key = $blob->storage_key;

    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($key);

    // A ContentStore that throws a 40001 serialization failure the FIRST time it is
    // asked to delete the object, then delegates — modelling a transient PG conflict
    // inside the sweepPending critical section.
    $real = app(ContentStore::class);
    $flaky = new class($real) implements ContentStore
    {
        private int $deletes = 0;

        public function __construct(private ContentStore $inner) {}

        public function stage(mixed $stream): StagedObject
        {
            return $this->inner->stage($stream);
        }

        public function promote(StagedObject $staged, string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
        {
            $this->inner->promote($staged, $key, $disk);
        }

        public function get(string $key, ?string $disk = null): mixed
        {
            return $this->inner->get($key, $disk);
        }

        public function exists(string $key, ?string $disk = null): bool
        {
            return $this->inner->exists($key, $disk);
        }

        public function delete(string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
        {
            if ($this->deletes++ === 0) {
                throw new QueryException(
                    'testing',
                    'delete blob object',
                    [],
                    new RuntimeException('SQLSTATE[40001]: serialization_failure: could not serialize access'),
                );
            }

            $this->inner->delete($key, $disk);
        }

        public function keyFor(BlobScope $scope, string $sha256): string
        {
            return $this->inner->keyFor($scope, $sha256);
        }
    };

    $gc = new BlobGarbageCollector(
        $flaky,
        app(BlobLock::class),
        app(BlobStoreRepository::class),
        app(FileVersionBlobStoreRepository::class),
        app(MetricsRecorder::class),
        app(VaulterDatabase::class),
    );

    // Pre-fix: the first 40001 propagates and aborts the pass. Post-fix: the unit is
    // retried under serializable() and the blob is swept on the second attempt.
    $result = $gc->sweepPending(graceHours: 1);

    expect($result['swept'])->toBe(1)
        ->and($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Deleted);

    Storage::disk('local')->assertMissing($key);
});

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Fixtures/Cas/FailingPromoteContentStore.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Tests\Fixtures\Cas;

use RuntimeException;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StagedObject;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

final class FailingPromoteContentStore implements ContentStore
{
    private int $promotes = 0;

    public function __construct(private ContentStore $inner, private int $failAt = 1, private bool $after = false) {}

    public function stage(mixed $stream): StagedObject
    {
        return $this->inner->stage($stream);
    }

    public function get(string $key, ?string $disk = null): mixed
    {
        return $this->inner->get($key, $disk);
    }

    public function exists(string $key, ?string $disk = null): bool
    {
        return $this->inner->exists($key, $disk);
    }

    public function delete(string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        $this->inner->delete($key, $disk);
    }

    public function keyFor(BlobScope $scope, string $sha256): string
    {
        return $this->inner->keyFor($scope, $sha256);
    }

    public function promote(StagedObject $staged, string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        $fail = ++$this->promotes === $this->failAt;
        if ($fail && !$this->after) {
            throw new RuntimeException('injected copy failure');
        }
        $this->inner->promote($staged, $key, $disk);
        if ($fail) {
            throw new RuntimeException('injected post-promote failure');
        }
    }
}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Fixtures/Cas/NonListableContentStore.php

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Tests\Fixtures\Cas;

use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ListableContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StagedObject;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobReconciler;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\FlysystemStorageDriver;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\Sha256ContentStore;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * A {@see ContentStore} that DELIBERATELY does NOT implement
 * {@see ListableContentStore} — the conservative, BC-safe default for any external
 * driver that cannot enumerate its disk (a streaming-only / non-FilesystemAdapter
 * store, e.g. S3 without listing). Binding it proves the orphan disk-walk refuses
 * cleanly: {@see BlobReconciler::sweepOrphans()} returns
 * listingSupported=false, scans 0 and deletes nothing, rather than failing.
 *
 * Every byte-level method delegates to a real {@see Sha256ContentStore} so a
 * test can still commit/read normally; only the listing capability is withheld.
 */
final readonly class NonListableContentStore implements ContentStore
{
    public function __construct(
        private ContentStore $inner = new Sha256ContentStore(new FlysystemStorageDriver),
    ) {}

    public function stage(mixed $stream): StagedObject
    {
        return $this->inner->stage($stream);
    }

    public function promote(StagedObject $staged, string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        $this->inner->promote($staged, $key, $disk);
    }

    public function get(string $key, ?string $disk = null): mixed
    {
        return $this->inner->get($key, $disk);
    }

    public function exists(string $key, ?string $disk = null): bool
    {
        return $this->inner->exists($key, $disk);
    }

    public function delete(string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        $this->inner->delete($key, $disk);
    }

    public function keyFor(BlobScope $scope, string $sha256): string
    {
        return $this->inner->keyFor($scope, $sha256);
    }
}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Fixtures/Cas/NullMtimeContentStore.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Tests\Fixtures\Cas;

use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ListableContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StagedObject;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobReconciler;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\FlysystemStorageDriver;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\Sha256ContentStore;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * A listable {@see ContentStore} whose {@see lastModified()} ALWAYS reports null —
 * an object whose age the disk cannot establish (an adapter that does not expose
 * mtime, or a transient metadata failure swallowed to null by the driver).
 *
 * The orphan grace must treat "age unknown" as NOT-past-grace, so such an object is
 * SKIPPED, never deleted (the data-safety conservative default). Binding this proves
 * {@see BlobReconciler::sweepOrphans()} never reports a null-mtime
 * object as an orphan, even when it otherwise matches the canonical key layout and
 * has no blob row.
 *
 * Everything else (staging, promotion, keyFor, and crucially listKeys) delegates to
 * a real {@see Sha256ContentStore} so the walk still SEES the object on disk.
 */
final readonly class NullMtimeContentStore implements ContentStore, ListableContentStore
{
    public function __construct(
        private ContentStore $inner = new Sha256ContentStore(new FlysystemStorageDriver),
    ) {}

    /**
     * Always null — the whole point of the fixture (age unknown ⇒ caller skips).
     */
    public function lastModified(string $key, ?string $disk = null): ?int
    {
        return null;
    }

    /**
     * The real recursive disk listing — the object must still be enumerated so the
     * grace gate (not the listing gate) is what skips it.
     *
     * @return iterable<string>
     */
    public function listKeys(?string $disk = null): iterable
    {
        /** @var Sha256ContentStore $inner */
        $inner = $this->inner;

        yield from $inner->listKeys($disk);
    }

    public function stage(mixed $stream): StagedObject
    {
        return $this->inner->stage($stream);
    }

    public function promote(StagedObject $staged, string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        $this->inner->promote($staged, $key, $disk);
    }

    public function get(string $key, ?string $disk = null): mixed
    {
        return $this->inner->get($key, $disk);
    }

    public function exists(string $key, ?string $disk = null): bool
    {
        return $this->inner->exists($key, $disk);
    }

    public function delete(string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        $this->inner->delete($key, $disk);
    }

    public function keyFor(BlobScope $scope, string $sha256): string
    {
        return $this->inner->keyFor($scope, $sha256);
    }
}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Fixtures/Cas/PhantomListingContentStore.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Tests\Fixtures\Cas;

use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ListableContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StagedObject;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\FlysystemStorageDriver;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\Sha256ContentStore;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * A listable {@see ContentStore} that yields a PHANTOM key — a path the walk
 * enumerates and that reports an old mtime (so it clears the orphan grace), but whose
 * bytes are NOT actually on disk. It models the race the prune must survive: the
 * object was present at the cheap scan but is GONE by the time the under-lock physical
 * delete fires (a concurrent sweep/operator removed it).
 *
 * The prune's delete must be IDEMPOTENT — deleting an already-absent object is a
 * harmless no-op, never an exception ({@see FlysystemStorageDriver::delete()} guards
 * on exists()). This fixture lets a single-process test exercise that without a
 * concurrent harness: the delete delegates to the real store, which finds nothing.
 *
 * Every other method delegates to a real {@see Sha256ContentStore}.
 */
final readonly class PhantomListingContentStore implements ContentStore, ListableContentStore
{
    public function __construct(
        private string $phantomKey,
        private int $phantomMtime,
        private ContentStore $inner = new Sha256ContentStore(new FlysystemStorageDriver),
    ) {}

    /**
     * The phantom key is the only thing on this "disk".
     *
     * @return iterable<string>
     */
    public function listKeys(?string $disk = null): iterable
    {
        yield $this->phantomKey;
    }

    /**
     * A fixed past mtime for the phantom so the orphan grace classifies it as a
     * deletable orphan (the real store would report null for an absent object).
     */
    public function lastModified(string $key, ?string $disk = null): ?int
    {
        return $key === $this->phantomKey ? $this->phantomMtime : null;
    }

    public function stage(mixed $stream): StagedObject
    {
        return $this->inner->stage($stream);
    }

    public function promote(StagedObject $staged, string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        $this->inner->promote($staged, $key, $disk);
    }

    public function get(string $key, ?string $disk = null): mixed
    {
        return $this->inner->get($key, $disk);
    }

    public function exists(string $key, ?string $disk = null): bool
    {
        return $this->inner->exists($key, $disk);
    }

    /**
     * Delegates to the real store — the phantom object is absent, so this is the
     * idempotent no-op the prune must tolerate.
     */
    public function delete(string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        $this->inner->delete($key, $disk);
    }

    public function keyFor(BlobScope $scope, string $sha256): string
    {
        return $this->inner->keyFor($scope, $sha256);
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Artisan;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;

    app(MetricsRecorder::class)->assertHistogram(name: 'vaulter_gc_duration_seconds', labels: ['phase' => 'mark']);

    app(MetricsRecorder::class)->assertHistogram(name: 'vaulter_reconcile_duration_seconds');
});
