# COMPLETE FILE packages/core/tests/Upgrade/LoadsV02Fixture.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Tests\Upgrade;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabaseState;
use RuntimeException;
use Symfony\Component\Process\Process;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;

trait LoadsV02Fixture
{
    protected function loadV02Fixture(string $strategy): void
    {
        if (!in_array($strategy, ['bigint', 'ulid'], true)) {
            throw new RuntimeException('Unknown v0.2 fixture strategy.');
        }

        $connection = app(VaulterDatabase::class)->connection();
        $config = $connection->getConfig();

        if (!$this->app->environment('testing') || $connection->getDriverName() !== 'pgsql'
            || !is_string($config['database']) || !str_ends_with($config['database'], '_test')
            || $connection->transactionLevel() !== 0) {
            throw new RuntimeException('Fixture loading requires an unwrapped PostgreSQL *_test database in APP_ENV=testing.');
        }

        $path = dirname(__DIR__, 4).'/tests/fixtures/v0.2.0/pgsql-'.$strategy.'.sql';
        if (!is_file($path)) {
            throw new RuntimeException('Missing committed fixture: '.$path);
        }

        config()->set('vaulter.ids.default', $strategy);
        config()->set('vaulter.ids.host_keys', 'string');
        $connection->statement('DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE');
        $connection->statement('CREATE SCHEMA public');
        $connection->disconnect();

        (new Process([
            'psql', '-X', '-q', '-v', 'ON_ERROR_STOP=1',
            '-h', (string) $config['host'], '-p', (string) $config['port'],
            '-U', (string) $config['username'], '-d', $config['database'], '-f', $path,
        ], env: ['PGPASSWORD' => (string) $config['password'], 'PGCONNECT_TIMEOUT' => '5']))->mustRun();

        RefreshDatabaseState::$migrated = false;
    }
}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/core/tests/Concurrency/ForkRace.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Tests\Concurrency;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use JsonException;
use LogicException;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;
use RuntimeException;
use Throwable;

/**
 * Real concurrency on PostgreSQL: every task runs in its OWN forked process on its
 * OWN database connection, and a file barrier releases all of them together.
 * SQLite serialises writes, so a race test there shows green on defective code —
 * a test built on this harness skips unless the connection is pgsql and the
 * pcntl + posix extensions are loaded.
 *
 * Fixtures the children must see have to be COMMITTED before run(): bind the test
 * directory with DatabaseMigrations, never RefreshDatabase (its wrapping
 * transaction is invisible to another connection).
 *
 * A task must return or throw, never exit(): the child would skip its result
 * (outcome ForkRace\MissingResult) and run PHPUnit's shutdown handlers.
 */
final class ForkRace
{
    private const int BARRIER_STEP_US = 200;

    private const int PARENT_GRACE_MS = 5_000;

    public static function supported(?string $connection = null): bool
    {
        return DB::connection($connection)->getDriverName() === 'pgsql'
            && function_exists('pcntl_fork')
            && function_exists('posix_kill');
    }

    public static function skipUnlessSupported(TestCase $test, ?string $connection = null): void
    {
        if (!self::supported($connection)) {
            $test->markTestSkipped('ForkRace needs a pgsql connection and the pcntl + posix extensions (SQLite serialises writes).');
        }
    }

    /**
     * @param  list<Closure(): mixed> $tasks
     * @param  list<string>           $connections connections each child re-opens; empty → the default one
     * @return list<RaceOutcome>
     */
    public static function run(array $tasks, array $connections = [], int $timeoutMs = 10_000): array
    {
        $names = $connections === [] ? [DB::getDefaultConnection()] : $connections;

        foreach ($names as $name) {
            $database = (string) DB::connection($name)->getDatabaseName();

            if (!str_ends_with($database, '_test')) {
                throw new LogicException("ForkRace refuses to race on a non-test database [{$database}] (connection {$name}).");
            }
        }

        $dir = sys_get_temp_dir().'/vlt_race_'.getmypid().'_'.bin2hex(random_bytes(4));
        mkdir($dir);

        $count = count($tasks);
        $pids = [];

        try {
            foreach (array_values($tasks) as $i => $task) {
                $pid = pcntl_fork();

                if ($pid === -1) {
                    self::killAll($pids);

                    throw new RuntimeException('ForkRace: unable to fork a child process.');
                }

                if ($pid === 0) {
                    self::child($task, $i, $count, $names, $dir, $timeoutMs);
                }

                $pids[$i] = $pid;
            }

            $timedOut = self::waitAll($pids, $timeoutMs + self::PARENT_GRACE_MS);

            // The children closed the inherited PDO handles; never reuse them.
            foreach ($names as $name) {
                DB::purge($name);
                DB::reconnect($name);
            }

            $outcomes = [];

            for ($i = 0; $i < $count; $i++) {
                $outcomes[] = isset($timedOut[$i])
                    ? new RaceOutcome(false, null, 'ForkRace\\Timeout', 'timeout')
                    : self::readResult("{$dir}/result.{$i}");
            }

            return $outcomes;
        } finally {
            array_map('unlink', glob("{$dir}/*") ?: []);
            @rmdir($dir);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Runs in the forked child and never returns: a child that went back into
     * PHPUnit would duplicate the test output and run the teardown twice.
     *
     * @param list<string> $names
     */
    private static function child(Closure $task, int $index, int $count, array $names, string $dir, int $timeoutMs): never
    {
        try {
            foreach ($names as $name) {
                DB::purge($name);
                DB::reconnect($name);
            }

            file_put_contents("{$dir}/ready.{$index}", '1');

            $deadline = hrtime(true) + $timeoutMs * 1_000_000;

            while (count(glob("{$dir}/ready.*") ?: []) < $count) {
                if (hrtime(true) >= $deadline) {
                    self::finish($dir, $index, ['ok' => false, 'value' => null, 'exception' => 'ForkRace\\BarrierTimeout', 'message' => 'barrier-timeout']);
                }

                usleep(self::BARRIER_STEP_US);
            }

            $value = $task();

            $result = self::isJsonValue($value)
                ? ['ok' => true, 'value' => $value, 'exception' => null, 'message' => null]
                : ['ok' => false, 'value' => null, 'exception' => 'ForkRace\\UnserializableValue', 'message' => get_debug_type($value)];
        } catch (Throwable $e) {
            $result = ['ok' => false, 'value' => null, 'exception' => $e::class, 'message' => $e->getMessage()];
        }

        self::finish($dir, $index, $result);
    }

    /**
     * @param array{ok: bool, value: mixed, exception: ?string, message: ?string} $result
     */
    private static function finish(string $dir, int $index, array $result): never
    {
        try {
            // Invalid UTF-8 (e.g. a bytea binding in a QueryException message) is
            // substituted, so a failed task keeps its exception class.
            $json = json_encode($result, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR | JSON_INVALID_UTF8_SUBSTITUTE);
        } catch (JsonException $e) {
            $json = json_encode(['ok' => false, 'value' => null, 'exception' => 'ForkRace\\UnserializableValue', 'message' => $e->getMessage()]);
        }

        file_put_contents("{$dir}/result.{$index}", $json);

        posix_kill(getmypid(), SIGKILL);

        exit(1); // unreachable: SIGKILL cannot be caught
    }

    /**
     * @param  array<int, int>  $pids
     * @return array<int, true> task indexes killed at the deadline
     */
    private static function waitAll(array $pids, int $deadlineMs): array
    {
        $deadline = hrtime(true) + $deadlineMs * 1_000_000;
        $pending = $pids;

        while ($pending !== [] && hrtime(true) < $deadline) {
            foreach ($pending as $i => $pid) {
                if (pcntl_waitpid($pid, $status, WNOHANG) !== 0) {
                    unset($pending[$i]);
                }
            }

            if ($pending !== []) {
                usleep(1_000);
            }
        }

        self::killAll($pending);

        return array_fill_keys(array_keys($pending), true);
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, int> $pids
     */
    private static function killAll(array $pids): void
    {
        foreach ($pids as $pid) {
            posix_kill($pid, SIGKILL);
            pcntl_waitpid($pid, $status);
        }
    }

    private static function readResult(string $path): RaceOutcome
    {
        $raw = @file_get_contents($path);

        if ($raw === false) {
            return new RaceOutcome(false, null, 'ForkRace\\MissingResult', 'the child exited without writing a result');
        }

        /** @var array{ok: bool, value: mixed, exception: ?string, message: ?string} $data */
        $data = json_decode($raw, true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);

        return new RaceOutcome($data['ok'], $data['value'], $data['exception'], $data['message']);
    }

    private static function isJsonValue(mixed $value): bool
    {
        if ($value === null || is_bool($value) || is_int($value) || is_string($value)) {
            return true;
        }

        if (is_float($value)) {
            return is_finite($value);
        }

        if (is_array($value)) {
            foreach ($value as $item) {
                if (!self::isJsonValue($item)) {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            return true;
        }

        return false;
    }
}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/core/database/migrations/2026_10_01_000003_host_key_columns.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Schema\HostKeyColumns;
use Vaulter\Schema\IdStrategyResolver;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;

return new class extends Migration
{
    /** @var array<string, array<string, bool>> */
    private const array COLUMNS = [
        'storages' => ['tenant_id' => true, 'owner_id' => false, 'created_by' => true],
        'nodes' => ['tenant_id' => true, 'owner_id' => true, 'trashed_by' => true, 'created_by' => true, 'updated_by' => true],
        'blobs' => ['tenant_id' => true],
        'files' => ['tenant_id' => true, 'created_by' => true],
        'file_versions' => ['tenant_id' => true, 'created_by' => true],
        'node_grants' => ['tenant_id' => true, 'subject_id' => false, 'granted_by' => true],
        'node_shares' => ['tenant_id' => true, 'granted_by' => true],
        'node_comments' => ['tenant_id' => true, 'author_id' => false, 'resolved_by' => true],
        'node_document_versions' => ['tenant_id' => true, 'created_by' => true],
        'upload_sessions' => ['tenant_id' => true, 'created_by' => true],
        'activity' => ['tenant_id' => true, 'actor_id' => true],
    ];

    public function up(): void
    {
        $this->convert(restore: false);
    }

    public function down(): void
    {
        $this->convert(restore: true);
    }

    private function convert(bool $restore): void
    {
        $connection = app(VaulterDatabase::class)->connection();
        $driver = $connection->getDriverName();

        if ($driver === 'sqlite') {
            return;
        }

        $schema = $connection->getSchemaBuilder();
        $grammar = $connection->getQueryGrammar();

        foreach (self::COLUMNS as $entity => $columns) {
            $table = Config::table($entity);

            if (!$schema->hasTable($table)) {
                continue;
            }

            $actual = array_column($schema->getColumns($table), null, 'name');

            foreach ($columns as $column => $nullable) {
                if (!isset($actual[$column])) {
                    continue;
                }

                $type = $restore
                    ? $this->originalType(new Blueprint($connection, $table), $entity, $column, $driver)
                    : HostKeyColumns::sqlType($driver);

                // type_name alone cannot distinguish varchar(26) from varchar(64)
                // or a signed bigint from an unsigned MySQL bigint.
                $actualType = str_replace(['character varying', 'character'], ['varchar', 'char'], strtolower($actual[$column]['type']));
                if ($actualType === $type) {
                    continue;
                }

                $wrappedTable = $grammar->wrapTable($table);
                $wrappedColumn = $grammar->wrap($column);

                if ($driver === 'pgsql') {
                    $cast = match ($type) {
                        'bigint' => 'bigint',
                        'uuid' => 'uuid',
                        default => 'text',
                    };
                    $connection->statement("ALTER TABLE {$wrappedTable} ALTER COLUMN {$wrappedColumn} TYPE {$type} USING {$wrappedColumn}::{$cast}");
                } else {
                    $null = $nullable ? 'NULL' : 'NOT NULL';
                    $connection->statement("ALTER TABLE {$wrappedTable} MODIFY {$wrappedColumn} {$type} {$null}");
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private function originalType(Blueprint $blueprint, string $entity, string $column, string $driver): string
    {
        $strategy = app(IdStrategyResolver::class)->resolve();
        $reference = $column === 'tenant_id'
            || ($entity === 'storages' && $column === 'owner_id')
            || $column === 'subject_id'
            || $column === 'author_id';

        if ($reference) {
            $strategy->reference($blueprint, $column);
        } else {
            $strategy->actor($blueprint, $column);
        }

        $definition = $blueprint->getColumns()[0];
        $type = $definition->get('type');
        $length = $definition->get('length');

        if (!is_string($type) || (in_array($type, ['char', 'string'], true) && !is_int($length))) {
            throw new LogicException('[Vaulter] Invalid rollback host-key definition.');
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'bigInteger' => $driver !== 'pgsql' && $definition->get('unsigned') ? 'bigint unsigned' : 'bigint',
            'char' => 'char('.$length.')',
            'string' => 'varchar('.$length.')',
            'uuid' => $driver === 'pgsql' ? 'uuid' : 'char(36)',
            default => throw new LogicException('[Vaulter] Unsupported rollback host-key type ['.$type.'].'),
        };
    }
};

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/src/Actions/ForceDeleteNodeAction.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Actions;

use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeForceDeleted;
use Vaulter\Enums\Drive\NodeKind;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNotTrashedException;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\File;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersion;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersionBlob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeReadRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\StorageStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\TreeGuard;

/**
 * Permanently soft-delete a trashed node and its subtree (§2.10 stage 2). Only a
 * node already in the trash qualifies (a live node goes through the trash first)
 * and the root never does; the subtree is read under the storage tree lock. The
 * order matters and is the GC contract (§2.3 / §2.4):
 *   1. detach every version↔blob edge in the subtree — the edge IS the liveness
 *      truth (H.3), so dropping it makes the blob GC-eligible; the bytes are
 *      NEVER deleted here (M1b's two-phase GC reclaims them);
 *   2. soft-delete (deleted_at) the node rows in one bulk UPDATE;
 *   3. decrement used_bytes by the freed LIVE size (§1.5 — trash kept the bytes;
 *      forceDelete is where the quota is actually reclaimed).
 *
 * Authorization (M2a / H.2) is enforced upstream at the HTTP controller
 * boundary (NodeController + AccessResolver); this Action stays pure.
 */
final readonly class ForceDeleteNodeAction
{
    public function __construct(
        private NodeReadRepository $nodes,
        private NodeStoreRepository $store,
        private StorageStoreRepository $storageStore,
        private BlobStore $blobs,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
        private TreeGuard $tree,
    ) {}

    public function execute(Node $node, int|string|null $actorId = null): void
    {
        $storage = $node->storage;

        $this->database->transaction(function () use ($node, $storage, $actorId): void {
            $this->database->lockTree($node->storage_id);

            $current = $this->tree->sync($node, $this->tree->locked($this->tree->lock([$node->id]), $node->id));

            $this->tree->assertNotRoot(node: $current, operation: 'delete');

            if ($current->trashed_at === null) {
                throw NodeNotTrashedException::forNode($current);
            }

            $subtree = $this->nodes->subtree($current);
            $ids = $subtree->modelKeys();
            $freedBytes = 0;

            foreach ($subtree as $member) {
                if ($member->kind !== NodeKind::File) {
                    continue;
                }

                $freedBytes += $this->detachFilePayload($member);
            }

            $this->store->softDeleteSubtree(collect($ids));

            if ($freedBytes > 0) {
                $this->storageStore->adjustUsedBytes(storage: $storage, delta: -$freedBytes);
            }

            event(new NodeForceDeleted(
                storageId: $current->storage_id,
                nodeIds: array_values($ids),
                freedBytes: $freedBytes,
                actorId: $actorId,
            ));
        });
    }

    /**
     * Detach every blob edge of every version of a file node and return its live
     * head size (the bytes the storage stops accounting for). Detaching only drops
     * the reference — the physical object is reclaimed later by GC.
     */
    private function detachFilePayload(Node $node): int
    {
        /** @var File|null $file */
        $file = $node->file()->first();

        if (!$file instanceof File) {
            return 0;
        }

        foreach ($file->versions()->get() as $version) {
            $this->detachVersionEdges($version);
        }

        return $node->size_bytes;
    }

    private function detachVersionEdges(FileVersion $version): void
    {
        foreach ($version->blobs as $blob) {
            /** @var FileVersionBlob $pivot */
            $pivot = $blob->getRelationValue('pivot');

            $this->blobs->detach(
                version: $version,
                blob: $blob,
                role: $pivot->role,
                ordinal: $pivot->ordinal,
            );
        }
    }
}
